commit c4297eab27939301a004dd25fa4338d865f9b0c8
Author: xliry <xliry@users.noreply.github.com>
Date:   Mon Feb 16 12:18:11 2026 +0300

    feat: add LTX-2 multi-frame guide images via latent injection
    
    New ltx2_multiframe task type that supports N guide images at arbitrary
    frame positions, reusing the existing latent injection mechanism.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py b/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
index 83839f8..92774c9 100644
--- a/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
+++ b/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
@@ -860,6 +860,18 @@ class LTX2:
             if image_end is not None:
                 images.append((image_end, _to_latent_index(frame_num - 1, latent_stride), 1.0))
 
+        # Multi-frame guide images: inject additional images at arbitrary frame positions
+        guide_images = kwargs.get("guide_images")
+        if guide_images:
+            for guide_img, frame_idx, strength in guide_images:
+                if frame_idx == -1:
+                    frame_idx = frame_num - 1
+                latent_idx = _to_latent_index(frame_idx, latent_stride)
+                entry = (guide_img, latent_idx, strength)
+                images.append(entry)
+                if isinstance(self.pipeline, TI2VidTwoStagesPipeline):
+                    images_stage2.append(entry)
+
         tiling_config = _build_tiling_config(VAE_tile_size, fps)
         interrupt_check = lambda: self._interrupt
         loras_slists = kwargs.get("loras_slists")

diff --git a/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py b/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
index fb141ec..83839f8 100644
--- a/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
+++ b/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
@@ -634,12 +634,29 @@ class LTX2:
         loras = []
         video_prompt_type = video_prompt_type or ""
         preload_urls = get_model_recursive_prop(model_type, "preload_URLs")
-        for letter, signature in map.items():
-            if letter in video_prompt_type:
-                for file_name in preload_urls:
-                    if signature in file_name:
-                        loras.append(fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name)))
-                        break
+
+        # Which control types are requested?
+        requested_controls = [letter for letter in map if letter in video_prompt_type]
+
+        # Check if union control LoRA is available
+        union_lora = None
+        for file_name in preload_urls:
+            if "union-control" in file_name or "union_control" in file_name:
+                union_lora = fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name))
+                break
+
+        # Multiple control types + union available → use single union LoRA
+        if len(requested_controls) > 1 and union_lora is not None:
+            loras.append(union_lora)
+        else:
+            # Existing logic: separate LoRAs per control type
+            for letter, signature in map.items():
+                if letter in video_prompt_type:
+                    for file_name in preload_urls:
+                        if signature in file_name and "union" not in file_name:
+                            loras.append(fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name)))
+                            break
+
         loras_mult = [1.0] * len(loras)
         return loras, loras_mult

commit 2dede9b743ebad59ed3850383b52f3fe2b893b2f
Author: POM <[REDACTED]>
Date:   Wed Feb 18 16:00:02 2026 +0100

diff --git a/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py b/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py
index df985d1..f52085b 100644
--- a/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py
+++ b/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py
@@ -392,6 +392,176 @@ class WanAny2V:
 
         return mocha_latents, (torch.cat(cos_parts, dim=0), torch.cat(sin_parts, dim=0))
 
+    def _load_uni3c_guide_video(
+        self,
+        guide_video_path: str,
+        target_height: int,
+        target_width: int,
+        target_frames: int,
+        frame_policy: str = "fit",
+    ) -> torch.Tensor:
+        """Load and preprocess guide video for Uni3C."""
+        import cv2
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Loading guide video from {guide_video_path}")
+
+        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(guide_video_path)
+        if not cap.isOpened():
+            raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] Could not open guide video: {guide_video_path}")
+
+        frames = []
+        while True:
+            ret, frame = cap.read()
+            if not ret:
+                break
+            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
+            frame = cv2.resize(frame, (target_width, target_height))
+            frames.append(frame)
+        cap.release()
+
+        if len(frames) == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] No frames loaded from guide video: {guide_video_path}")
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Loaded {len(frames)} frames from guide video")
+        frames = self._apply_uni3c_frame_policy(frames, target_frames, frame_policy)
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: After frame policy '{frame_policy}': {len(frames)} frames (target: {target_frames})")
+
+        video = np.stack(frames, axis=0)
+        video = video.astype(np.float32)
+        video = (video / 127.5) - 1.0
+        video = torch.from_numpy(video).permute(3, 0, 1, 2)
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Guide video tensor shape: {tuple(video.shape)}, dtype: {video.dtype}")
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   value range: [{video.min().item():.2f}, {video.max().item():.2f}]")
+        return video
+
+    def _apply_uni3c_frame_policy(
+        self,
+        frames: list,
+        target_frames: int,
+        policy: str,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Apply frame alignment policy to match target frame count."""
+        current = len(frames)
+
+        if policy == "off":
+            if current != target_frames:
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"[UNI3C] Frame count mismatch: guide has {current} frames, "
+                    f"target is {target_frames}. Use a different frame_policy."
+                )
+            return frames
+
+        if policy == "fit":
+            if current == target_frames:
+                return frames
+            indices = np.linspace(0, current - 1, target_frames).astype(int)
+            return [frames[i] for i in indices]
+
+        if policy == "trim":
+            if current >= target_frames:
+                return frames[:target_frames]
+            return frames + [frames[-1]] * (target_frames - current)
+
+        if policy == "loop":
+            if current >= target_frames:
+                return frames[:target_frames]
+            result = []
+            while len(result) < target_frames:
+                result.extend(frames)
+            return result[:target_frames]
+
+        raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] Unknown frame_policy: {policy}")
+
+    def _detect_empty_frames(
+        self,
+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,
+        threshold: float = 0.02,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Detect near-black frames in guide video and mark them empty."""
+        with torch.no_grad():
+            delta_from_black = (guide_video + 1.0).abs()
+            per_frame_delta = delta_from_black.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+            empty = per_frame_delta < threshold
+        return [bool(x) for x in empty.tolist()]
+
+    def _map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames(
+        self,
+        num_pixel_frames: int,
+        num_latent_frames: int,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Map pixel frame indices to latent frame indices."""
+        mapping = []
+        for lat_f in range(num_latent_frames):
+            start_pix = lat_f * 4
+            end_pix = min(start_pix + 4, num_pixel_frames)
+            mapping.append(list(range(start_pix, end_pix)))
+        return mapping
+
+    def _encode_uni3c_guide(
+        self,
+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,
+        VAE_tile_size: int,
+        expected_channels: int = 20,
+        zero_empty_frames: bool = True,
+    ) -> torch.Tensor:
+        """VAE-encode guide video and optionally zero empty-frame latent groups."""
+        num_pixel_frames = guide_video.shape[1]
+        empty_pixel_mask = []
+        if zero_empty_frames:
+            empty_pixel_mask = self._detect_empty_frames(guide_video)
+            num_empty = sum(empty_pixel_mask)
+            if num_empty > 0:
+                print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Detected {num_empty}/{num_pixel_frames} empty pixel frames")
+                ranges = []
+                start = None
+                for i, is_empty in enumerate(empty_pixel_mask):
+                    if is_empty and start is None:
+                        start = i
+                    elif not is_empty and start is not None:
+                        ranges.append(f"{start}-{i-1}" if i - 1 > start else str(start))
+                        start = None
+                if start is not None:
+                    ranges.append(f"{start}-{num_pixel_frames-1}" if num_pixel_frames - 1 > start else str(start))
+                if ranges:
+                    print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   Empty frame ranges: {', '.join(ranges)}")
+
+        guide_video = guide_video.to(device=self.device, dtype=self.VAE_dtype)
+        latent = self.vae.encode([guide_video], tile_size=VAE_tile_size)[0]

commit 2dede9b743ebad59ed3850383b52f3fe2b893b2f

diff --git a/Wan2GP/models/wan/uni3c/controlnet.py b/Wan2GP/models/wan/uni3c/controlnet.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..16658dc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Wan2GP/models/wan/uni3c/controlnet.py
@@ -0,0 +1,431 @@
+"""
+WanControlNet - Uni3C ControlNet for Wan video generation.
+
+Directly ported from Kijai's ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper implementation.
+https://github.com/kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper/blob/main/uni3c/controlnet.py
+
+This provides video-to-video structural guidance through a ControlNet that
+extracts features from a guide/reference video and injects them into the
+main transformer's generation process.
+"""
+
+import torch
+import torch.nn as nn
+import numpy as np
+from typing import Optional, Tuple, Union, List
+import torch.nn.functional as F
+from diffusers.models.attention_processor import Attention
+
+from einops import rearrange
+
+
+def get_1d_rotary_pos_embed(
+    dim: int,
+    pos: Union[np.ndarray, int],
+    theta: float = 10000.0,
+    use_real=False,
+    linear_factor=1.0,
+    ntk_factor=1.0,
+    repeat_interleave_real=True,
+    freqs_dtype=torch.float32,
+):
+    """
+    Precompute the frequency tensor for complex exponentials (cis) with given dimensions.
+
+    This function calculates a frequency tensor with complex exponentials using the given dimension 'dim' and the end
+    index 'end'. The 'theta' parameter scales the frequencies. The returned tensor contains complex values in complex64
+    data type.
+
+    Args:
+        dim (`int`): Dimension of the frequency tensor.
+        pos (`np.ndarray` or `int`): Position indices for the frequency tensor. [S] or scalar
+        theta (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 10000.0):
+            Scaling factor for frequency computation. Defaults to 10000.0.
+        use_real (`bool`, *optional*):
+            If True, return real part and imaginary part separately. Otherwise, return complex numbers.
+        linear_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0):
+            Scaling factor for the context extrapolation. Defaults to 1.0.
+        ntk_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0):
+            Scaling factor for the NTK-Aware RoPE. Defaults to 1.0.
+        repeat_interleave_real (`bool`, *optional*, defaults to `True`):
+            If `True` and `use_real`, real part and imaginary part are each interleaved with themselves to reach `dim`.
+            Otherwise, they are concateanted with themselves.
+        freqs_dtype (`torch.float32` or `torch.float64`, *optional*, defaults to `torch.float32`):
+            the dtype of the frequency tensor.
+    Returns:
+        `torch.Tensor`: Precomputed frequency tensor with complex exponentials. [S, D/2]
+    """
+    assert dim % 2 == 0
+
+    if isinstance(pos, int):
+        pos = torch.arange(pos)
+    if isinstance(pos, np.ndarray):
+        pos = torch.from_numpy(pos)  # type: ignore  # [S]
+
+    theta = theta * ntk_factor
+    freqs = (
+        1.0 / (theta ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=freqs_dtype, device=pos.device) / dim)) / linear_factor
+    )  # [D/2]
+    freqs = torch.outer(pos, freqs)  # type: ignore   # [S, D/2]
+    is_npu = freqs.device.type == "npu"
+    if is_npu:
+        freqs = freqs.float()
+    if use_real and repeat_interleave_real:
+        # flux, hunyuan-dit, cogvideox
+        freqs_cos = freqs.cos().repeat_interleave(2, dim=1, output_size=freqs.shape[1] * 2).float()  # [S, D]
+        freqs_sin = freqs.sin().repeat_interleave(2, dim=1, output_size=freqs.shape[1] * 2).float()  # [S, D]
+        return freqs_cos, freqs_sin
+    elif use_real:
+        # stable audio, allegro
+        freqs_cos = torch.cat([freqs.cos(), freqs.cos()], dim=-1).float()  # [S, D]
+        freqs_sin = torch.cat([freqs.sin(), freqs.sin()], dim=-1).float()  # [S, D]
+        return freqs_cos, freqs_sin
+    else:
+        # lumina
+        freqs_cis = torch.polar(torch.ones_like(freqs), freqs)  # complex64     # [S, D/2]
+        return freqs_cis
+
+

## Custom Patches to Vendored Wan2GP/ Directory (Post-Upstream Sync)

Based on commit history after `a4f1b0b` ("feat: Wan2GP upstream sync v10.83 + LTX-2 19B integration") on the `codex/pr19-layered-port` branch, here are **all custom patches** applied to the vendored Wan2GP/ code:

### Commit Summary (Newest First):

#### 1. **2dede9b** - "feat: restore Uni3C path on top of Wan2GP v10.83" (Feb 18, 2026)
   **Purpose**: Add Uni3C (video structural guidance) support back to Wan2GP after upstream sync
   
   **Modified Files**:
   - `Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py` — +291 lines
     - Added `_load_uni3c_guide_video()` method to load and preprocess guide videos
     - Added `_apply_uni3c_frame_policy()` method with policies: "off", "fit", "trim", "loop"
     - Added `_detect_empty_frames()` to identify black frames in guide video
     - Added `_map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames()` for frame mapping
     - Added `_encode_uni3c_guide()` to VAE-encode guide video with optional frame zeroing
   
   - `Wan2GP/wgp.py` — +32 lines
     - Added 8 new `uni3c_*` parameters to `generate_video()` function signature:
       - `use_uni3c`, `uni3c_guide_video`, `uni3c_strength`
       - `uni3c_start_percent`, `uni3c_end_percent`, `uni3c_keep_on_gpu`
       - `uni3c_frame_policy`, `uni3c_zero_empty_frames`, `uni3c_blackout_last_frame`, `uni3c_controlnet`
     - Added debug logging for Uni3C initialization
     - Pass all uni3c params down to transformer call
   
   - `Wan2GP/models/wan/modules/model.py` — +134 lines (new file - ControlNet model integration)
   - `Wan2GP/models/wan/uni3c/__init__.py` — +35 lines (new file - module initialization)
   - `Wan2GP/models/wan/uni3c/controlnet.py` — +431 lines (new file - ported from Kijai's ComfyUI wrapper)
     - WanControlNet implementation with RoPE embeddings
     - Direct port from kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper
   
   - **New default configs** (6 new `.json` files):
     - `Wan2GP/defaults/wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2.json`
     - `Wan2GP/defaults/wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_3_3.json`
     - `Wan2GP/defaults/wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_svi_3_3.json`

     - `Wan2GP/defaults/wan_2_2_vace_lightning_baseline_2_2_2.json`
     - `Wan2GP/defaults/wan_2_2_vace_lightning_baseline_3_3.json`
     - `Wan2GP/defaults/z_image_img2img.json`

---

#### 2. **c4297ea** - "feat: add LTX-2 multi-frame guide images via latent injection" (Feb 16, 2026)
   **Purpose**: Support multiple guide images at arbitrary frame positions in LTX-2
   
   **Modified Files**:
   - `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py` — +12 lines
     - Added `guide_images` handling in the latent injection loop (after existing `image_start`/`image_end` logic)
     - Accepts `guide_images = [(img, frame_idx, strength), ...]` from kwargs
     - Frame index -1 means last frame
     - Injects into both stage-1 and stage-2 pipelines (if available)
     ```python
     guide_images = kwargs.get("guide_images")
     if guide_images:
         for guide_img, frame_idx, strength in guide_images:
             if frame_idx == -1:
                 frame_idx = frame_num - 1
             latent_idx = _to_latent_index(frame_idx, latent_stride)
             entry = (guide_img, latent_idx, strength)
             images.append(entry)
             if isinstance(self.pipeline, TI2VidTwoStagesPipeline):
                 images_stage2.append(entry)
     ```

---

#### 3. **a97186f** - "fix: post-merge bug fixes for LTX-2 IC-LoRA + v10.83 compatibility" (Feb 15, 2026)
   **Purpose**: Fix union control LoRA URL typo and compatibility issues
   
   **Modified Files**:
   - `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_19B.json` — 1 line change
     - Fixed union control LoRA filename from `ltx-2-19b-ic-lora-union-control.safetensors` to `ltx-2-19b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors`

   **Context**: This fixes a 404 error when downloading the wrong filename

---

#### 4. **69cc46b** - "feat: add union control LoRA support for LTX-2 IC-LoRA pose workflow" (Feb 15, 2026)
   **Purpose**: Use single union LoRA for multiple control types (Pose+Depth, Pose+Edge, etc.)
   
   **Modified Files**:
   - `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py` — +25 lines, -7 lines (net +18)
     - Rewrote `get_loras_transformer()` method
     - **Key change**: If multiple control types requested AND union LoRA available → use single union LoRA
     - Otherwise fall back to separate LoRAs per control type
     - **Logic**:
       ```python
       requested_controls = [letter for letter in map if letter in video_prompt_type]
       
       # Check if union control LoRA is available
       union_lora = None
       for file_name in preload_urls:
           if "union-control" in file_name or "union_control" in file_name:
               union_lora = fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name))
               break
       
       # Multiple control types + union available → use single union LoRA
       if len(requested_controls) > 1 and union_lora is not None:
           loras.append(union_lora)
       else:
           # Existing logic: separate LoRAs per control type
           for letter, signature in map.items():
               if letter in video_prompt_type:
                   for file_name in preload_urls:
                       if signature in file_name and "union" not in file_name:
                           loras.append(fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name)))
                           break
       ```
   
   - `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_19B.json` — 1 line added
     - Added union control LoRA URL to `preload_URLs`:
       `https://huggingface.co/Lightricks/LTX-2-19b-IC-LoRA-Union-Control/resolve/main/ltx-2-19b-ic-lora-union-control.safetensors`

---

   **Modified Files**:

## How to Re-apply After Upstream Update

When updating to a newer upstream Wan2GP version:

1. **Apply in reverse chronological order** (newest first, before base upstream sync):
   1. Uni3C support (2dede9b)
   2. Multi-frame guide images (c4297ea)
   3. Union control LoRA filename fix (a97186f)
   4. Union control LoRA logic (69cc46b)
   5. ICLoraPipeline routing (209b7ff)
   6. Qwen Path imports (ef573a3)
   7. Wgp.py headless fix (40da649)

2. **Watch for API breaking changes**:
   - `apply_changes()` was removed → replaced with `get_default_settings()` + `set_model_settings()`
   - ICLoraPipeline signature changed → now accepts WanGP-managed models

3. **Key integration points**:
   - Wan2GP/wgp.py `generate_video()` function signature now has 8+ Uni3C parameters
   - Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py now routes "ic_lora" pipeline type
   - ICLoraPipeline now bridges with WanGP's model management (stage_1_models/stage_2_models)

+You can override the choice of Resolutions offered by WanGP, if you create a file "resolutions.json" in the main WanGP folder.
+This file is composed of a list of 2 elements sublists. Each 2 elements sublist should have the format ["Label", "WxH"] where W, H are respectively the Width and Height of the resolution. Please make sure that W and H are multiples of 16. The letter "x" should be placed inbetween these two dimensions.
+
+Here is below a sample "resolutions.json" file :
+
+[
+	["1280x720 (16:9, 720p)", "1280x720"],
+	["720x1280 (9:16, 720p)", "720x1280"], 
+	["1024x1024 (1:1, 720p)", "1024x1024"],
+	["1280x544 (21:9, 720p)", "1280x544"],
+	["544x1280 (9:21, 720p)", "544x1280"],

device = get_device()


class ICLoraPipeline:
    """
    Two-stage video generation pipeline with In-Context (IC) LoRA support.
    Allows conditioning the generated video on control signals such as depth maps,
    human pose, or image edges via the video_conditioning parameter.
    The specific IC-LoRA model should be provided via the loras parameter.
    Stage 1 generates video at the target resolution, then Stage 2 upsamples
    by 2x and refines with additional denoising steps for higher quality output.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        checkpoint_path: str,
        spatial_upsampler_path: str,
        gemma_root: str,
        loras: list[LoraPathStrengthAndSDOps],
        device: torch.device = device,
        fp8transformer: bool = False,
    ):
        self.dtype = torch.bfloat16
        self.stage_1_model_ledger = ModelLedger(
            dtype=self.dtype,
            device=device,
            checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,
            spatial_upsampler_path=spatial_upsampler_path,
            gemma_root_path=gemma_root,
            loras=loras,
            fp8transformer=fp8transformer,
        )
        self.stage_2_model_ledger = ModelLedger(
            dtype=self.dtype,
            device=device,
            checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,
            spatial_upsampler_path=spatial_upsampler_path,
            gemma_root_path=gemma_root,
            loras=[],
            fp8transformer=fp8transformer,
        )
        self.pipeline_components = PipelineComponents(
            dtype=self.dtype,
            device=device,
        )
        self.device = device
        self.text_encoder_cache = TextEncoderCache()

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def __call__(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        seed: int,
        height: int,
        width: int,
        num_frames: int,
        frame_rate: float,
        images: list[tuple[str, int, float]],

+def _normalize_config(config_value):
+    if isinstance(config_value, dict):
+        return config_value
+    if isinstance(config_value, (bytes, bytearray, memoryview)):
+        try:
+            config_value = bytes(config_value).decode("utf-8")
+        except Exception:
+            return {}
+    if isinstance(config_value, str):
+        try:
+            return json.loads(config_value)
+        except json.JSONDecodeError:
+            return {}
+    return {}
+
+
+def _load_config_from_checkpoint(path):
+    from mmgp import quant_router
+
+    if isinstance(path, (list, tuple)):
+        if not path:
+            return {}
+        path = path[0]
+    if not path:
+        return {}
+    _, metadata = quant_router.load_metadata_state_dict(path)
+    if not metadata:
+        return {}
+    return _normalize_config(metadata.get("config"))
+
+
+def _strip_model_prefix(key: str) -> str:
+    if key.startswith("model."):
+        return key[len("model.") :]
+    return key
+
+
+def _apply_sd_ops(state_dict: dict, quantization_map: dict | None, sd_ops):
+    if sd_ops is not None:
+        has_match = False
+        for key in state_dict.keys():
+            [REDACTED](key)
+            if sd_ops.apply_to_key(key) is not None:
+                has_match = True
+                break
+        if not has_match:
+            new_sd = {_strip_model_prefix(k): v for k, v in state_dict.items()}
+            new_qm = {}
+            if quantization_map:
+                new_qm = {_strip_model_prefix(k): v for k, v in quantization_map.items()}
+            return new_sd, new_qm
+
+    new_sd = {}
+    for key, value in state_dict.items():
+        [REDACTED](key)
+        if sd_ops is None:
+            new_sd[key] = value
+            continue
+        else:
+            [REDACTED](key)
+            if new_key is None:
+                continue
+            new_pairs = sd_ops.apply_to_key_value(new_key, value)
+        for pair in new_pairs:
+            new_sd[pair.new_key] = pair.new_value
+
+    new_qm = {}
+    if quantization_map:
+        for key, value in quantization_map.items():
+            [REDACTED](key)

+            new_qm[new_key] = value
+    return new_sd, new_qm
+
+
+def _make_sd_postprocess(sd_ops):
+    def postprocess(state_dict, quantization_map):
+        return _apply_sd_ops(state_dict, quantization_map, sd_ops)
+
+    return postprocess

 See [STRUCTURE.md](STRUCTURE.md) for detailed project layout.
 
+## LTX-2 Integration Status
+
+LTX-2 19B has been integrated into the worker alongside the existing Wan 2.2 pipeline. Below is the current status of each integration phase.
+
+### Phase 1 — LTX-2 + Upstream Wan2GP Updates ✅ Complete
+
+| Component | Status | Location |
+|-----------|--------|----------|
+| LTX-2 model detection (`_is_ltx2()`) | ✅ | `headless_wgp.py:774` |
+| LTX-2 generation path | ✅ | `headless_wgp.py:999-1700` |
+| Model loading (`ltx2_19B`) | ✅ | WGP generate_video pattern |
+| Worker task routing (`ltx2` task type) | ✅ | `source/task_types.py:90` → `ltx2_19B` |
+| HeadlessTaskQueue routing | ✅ | `source/task_registry.py` |
+| Upstream WGP v10.83 synced | ✅ | Handler, defaults, configs present |
+| Headless `app` reference fix | ✅ | `Wan2GP/wgp.py:8048` |
+| GPU test passed | ✅ | 168s generation, video output verified |
+
+### Phase 1b — Testing & Validation ⚠️ Partial
+
+| Test | Status | Notes |
+|------|--------|-------|
+| LTX-2 GPU generation test | ✅ | `test_ltx2_gpu.py` — 512x320, 17 frames, seed 42 |
+| LTX-2 headless smoke tests (26 cases) | ✅ | `tests/test_ltx2_headless.py` |
+| Image-anchored test (start/end image) | ✅ | Covered in smoke tests |
+| **Wan 2.2 baseline generation test** | ❌ Missing | No test to verify existing Wan behavior is unchanged |
+| **HeadlessTaskQueue integration test** | ❌ Missing | Tests call orchestrator directly; production path (queue → conversion → orchestrator) is untested |
+
+### Phase 2 — Image Anchoring + Audio-Driven Video ✅ Complete
+
+| Feature | Status | Location |
+|---------|--------|----------|
+| `start_image` → `image_start` (PIL) bridging | ✅ | `headless_wgp.py:1113` |
+| `end_image` → `image_end` (PIL) bridging | ✅ | `headless_wgp.py:1124` |
+| `audio_input` → `audio_guide` bridging | ✅ | `headless_wgp.py:1135` |
+| `image_prompt_type` auto-detect (T/TS/TE/TSE) | ✅ | `headless_wgp.py:1140-1153` |
+| Audio encoding/conditioning in LTX-2 | ✅ | `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py:844-880` |
+| Travel segment image/audio passthrough | ✅ | `source/task_handlers/travel_between_images.py:2024-2058` |
+
+### Phase 3 — Wan vs LTX-2 Toggle ✅ Complete
+
+| Feature | Status | Location |
+|---------|--------|----------|
+| `use_ltx2` flag in orchestrator payload | ✅ | `source/task_handlers/travel_between_images.py:1670` |
+| Model selection (`ltx2_19B` vs Wan) | ✅ | `source/task_handlers/travel_between_images.py:1864` |
+| Segment dependency chain (LTX-2 sequential) | ✅ | Lines 1678-1697 |
+| Stitch task creation for LTX-2 | ✅ | Lines 368-372 |
+| Task type → model mapping | ✅ | `source/task_types.py:71-106` |
+| Worker dispatch routing | ✅ | `source/task_registry.py` |
+
+### Remaining Gaps
+
+1. **Wan 2.2 baseline test** — No test exists to confirm existing Wan generation behavior is unchanged after integration.
+2. **HeadlessTaskQueue integration test** — The production path (queue → task conversion → orchestrator) has no end-to-end test coverage.
+

diff --git a/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py b/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
index fb141ecd..83839f8b 100644
--- a/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
+++ b/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py
@@ -634,12 +634,29 @@ class LTX2:
         loras = []
         video_prompt_type = video_prompt_type or ""
         preload_urls = get_model_recursive_prop(model_type, "preload_URLs")
-        for letter, signature in map.items():
-            if letter in video_prompt_type:
-                for file_name in preload_urls:
-                    if signature in file_name:
-                        loras.append(fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name)))
-                        break
+
+        # Which control types are requested?
+        requested_controls = [letter for letter in map if letter in video_prompt_type]
+
+        # Check if union control LoRA is available
+        union_lora = None
+        for file_name in preload_urls:
+            if "union-control" in file_name or "union_control" in file_name:
+                union_lora = fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name))
+                break
+
+        # Multiple control types + union available → use single union LoRA
+        if len(requested_controls) > 1 and union_lora is not None:
+            loras.append(union_lora)
+        else:
+            # Existing logic: separate LoRAs per control type
+            for letter, signature in map.items():
+                if letter in video_prompt_type:
+                    for file_name in preload_urls:
+                        if signature in file_name and "union" not in file_name:
+                            loras.append(fl.locate_file(os.path.basename(file_name)))
+                            break
+
         loras_mult = [1.0] * len(loras)
         return loras, loras_mult

+
+### Bug Fixes (Post-Merge)
+
+Several bugs were discovered and fixed after the v10.83 + LTX-2 merge:
+
+| Bug | Root Cause | Fix |
+|-----|-----------|-----|
+| `apply_changes` import error | Merge conflict brought back deleted API; upstream replaced `apply_changes` with `get_default_settings` + `set_model_settings` | Replaced import and call site in `orchestrator.py` |
+| Missing v10.x WGP parameters | 12 new parameters (`alt_prompt`, `duration_seconds`, `audio_scale`, `self_refiner_*`, etc.) not passed to `generate_video()` | Added to both passthrough and normal mode in `wgp_params.py` |
+| Union control LoRA 404 | Wrong filename in `ltx2_19B.json` (missing `-ref0.5` suffix) | Corrected URL to `ltx-2-19b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors` |
+| `NoneType / int` crash in control_net_weight2 | Default values only set inside `if is_vace:` block; LTX-2 is not VACE | Moved defaults outside the VACE-specific block |
+| Test searching wrong file | `test_new_params_present_in_headless_wgp` only checked `orchestrator.py` after params were refactored to `wgp_params.py` | Updated test to search both files |
+
+### IC-LoRA Pipeline Status
+
+The IC-LoRA control weights (pose, depth, canny, union) load correctly as LoRA adapters. However, the specialized `ICLoraPipeline` (with reference downscale and video conditioning guide injection, equivalent to ComfyUI's `LTXICLoRALoaderModelOnly` + `LTXAddVideoICLoRAGuide` nodes) is **not yet active** — the config defaults to the `two_stage` pipeline.
+
+To activate the full IC-LoRA pipeline, add `"ltx2_pipeline": "ic_lora"` to the model definition in `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_19B.json`. This would enable:
+- Reference frame downscale factor control
+- Video conditioning guide injection into the diffusion process
+- Two-stage generation with IC-LoRA-specific conditioning
+
 ### Remaining Gaps
 
 1. **Wan 2.2 baseline test** — No test exists to confirm existing Wan generation behavior is unchanged after integration.
 2. **HeadlessTaskQueue integration test** — The production path (queue → task conversion → orchestrator) has no end-to-end test coverage.
+3. **IC-LoRA pipeline activation** — `ICLoraPipeline` is available but not enabled by default (see above).

commit 2dede9b743ebad59ed3850383b52f3fe2b893b2f
Author: POM <[REDACTED]>
Date:   Wed Feb 18 16:00:02 2026 +0100

    feat: restore Uni3C path on top of Wan2GP v10.83

diff --git a/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py b/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py
index df985d1b..f52085b4 100644
--- a/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py
+++ b/Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py
@@ -392,6 +392,176 @@ class WanAny2V:
 
         return mocha_latents, (torch.cat(cos_parts, dim=0), torch.cat(sin_parts, dim=0))
 
+    def _load_uni3c_guide_video(
+        self,
+        guide_video_path: str,
+        target_height: int,
+        target_width: int,
+        target_frames: int,
+        frame_policy: str = "fit",
+    ) -> torch.Tensor:
+        """Load and preprocess guide video for Uni3C."""
+        import cv2
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Loading guide video from {guide_video_path}")
+
+        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(guide_video_path)
+        if not cap.isOpened():
+            raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] Could not open guide video: {guide_video_path}")
+
+        frames = []
+        while True:
+            ret, frame = cap.read()
+            if not ret:
+                break
+            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
+            frame = cv2.resize(frame, (target_width, target_height))
+            frames.append(frame)
+        cap.release()
+
+        if len(frames) == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] No frames loaded from guide video: {guide_video_path}")
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Loaded {len(frames)} frames from guide video")
+        frames = self._apply_uni3c_frame_policy(frames, target_frames, frame_policy)
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: After frame policy '{frame_policy}': {len(frames)} frames (target: {target_frames})")
+
+        video = np.stack(frames, axis=0)
+        video = video.astype(np.float32)
+        video = (video / 127.5) - 1.0
+        video = torch.from_numpy(video).permute(3, 0, 1, 2)
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Guide video tensor shape: {tuple(video.shape)}, dtype: {video.dtype}")
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   value range: [{video.min().item():.2f}, {video.max().item():.2f}]")
+        return video
+
+    def _apply_uni3c_frame_policy(
+        self,
+        frames: list,
+        target_frames: int,
+        policy: str,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Apply frame alignment policy to match target frame count."""
+        current = len(frames)
+
+        if policy == "off":
+            if current != target_frames:
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"[UNI3C] Frame count mismatch: guide has {current} frames, "
+                    f"target is {target_frames}. Use a different frame_policy."
+                )
+            return frames
+
+        if policy == "fit":
+            if current == target_frames:
+                return frames
+            indices = np.linspace(0, current - 1, target_frames).astype(int)
+            return [frames[i] for i in indices]
+
+        if policy == "trim":
+            if current >= target_frames:
+                return frames[:target_frames]
+            return frames + [frames[-1]] * (target_frames - current)
+
+        if policy == "loop":
+            if current >= target_frames:
+                return frames[:target_frames]
+            result = []
+            while len(result) < target_frames:
+                result.extend(frames)
+            return result[:target_frames]
+
+        raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] Unknown frame_policy: {policy}")
+
+    def _detect_empty_frames(
+        self,
+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,
+        threshold: float = 0.02,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Detect near-black frames in guide video and mark them empty."""
+        with torch.no_grad():
+            delta_from_black = (guide_video + 1.0).abs()
+            per_frame_delta = delta_from_black.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+            empty = per_frame_delta < threshold
+        return [bool(x) for x in empty.tolist()]
+
+    def _map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames(
+        self,
+        num_pixel_frames: int,
+        num_latent_frames: int,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Map pixel frame indices to latent frame indices."""
+        mapping = []
+        for lat_f in range(num_latent_frames):
+            start_pix = lat_f * 4
+            end_pix = min(start_pix + 4, num_pixel_frames)
+            mapping.append(list(range(start_pix, end_pix)))
+        return mapping
+
+    def _encode_uni3c_guide(
+        self,
+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,
+        VAE_tile_size: int,
+        expected_channels: int = 20,
+        zero_empty_frames: bool = True,
+    ) -> torch.Tensor:
+        """VAE-encode guide video and optionally zero empty-frame latent groups."""
+        num_pixel_frames = guide_video.shape[1]
+        empty_pixel_mask = []
+        if zero_empty_frames:
+            empty_pixel_mask = self._detect_empty_frames(guide_video)
+            num_empty = sum(empty_pixel_mask)
+            if num_empty > 0:
+                print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Detected {num_empty}/{num_pixel_frames} empty pixel frames")
+                ranges = []
+                start = None
+                for i, is_empty in enumerate(empty_pixel_mask):
+                    if is_empty and start is None:
+                        start = i
+                    elif not is_empty and start is not None:
+                        ranges.append(f"{start}-{i-1}" if i - 1 > start else str(start))
+                        start = None
+                if start is not None:
+                    ranges.append(f"{start}-{num_pixel_frames-1}" if num_pixel_frames - 1 > start else str(start))
+                if ranges:
+                    print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   Empty frame ranges: {', '.join(ranges)}")
+
+        guide_video = guide_video.to(device=self.device, dtype=self.VAE_dtype)
+        latent = self.vae.encode([guide_video], tile_size=VAE_tile_size)[0]
```

    34→    "z_image_turbo", "z_image_turbo_i2i",
    35→})
    36→
    37→# =============================================================================
    38→# Direct Queue Task Types
    39→# =============================================================================
    40→# Task types that can be directly submitted to HeadlessTaskQueue without
    41→# orchestration/special handling. These bypass the task_registry handlers.
    42→
    43→DIRECT_QUEUE_TASK_TYPES: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset({
    44→    # VACE models
    45→    "vace", "vace_21", "vace_22",
    46→    # Flux model
    47→    "flux",

    60→    "qwen_image", "qwen_image_2512", "z_image_turbo",
    61→    # Image-to-image tasks
    62→    "z_image_turbo_i2i",
    63→})
    64→
    65→# =============================================================================
    66→# Task Type to Model Mapping
    67→# =============================================================================
    68→# Default model to use for each task type when not explicitly specified.
    69→# This maps task types to their canonical WGP model identifiers.
    70→
    71→TASK_TYPE_TO_MODEL: Dict[str, str] = {
    72→    # Generic/default generation
    73→    "generate_video": "t2v",
    74→    # VACE models
    75→    "vace": "vace_14B_cocktail_2_2",

    86→    "i2v_22": "i2v_2_2",
    87→    # Other video models
    88→    "hunyuan": "hunyuan",
    89→    "ltxv": "ltxv_13B",
    90→    "ltx2": "ltx2_19B",
    91→    "ltx2_multiframe": "ltx2_19B",

    95→    "inpaint_frames": "wan_2_2_vace_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",

    98→    "qwen_image_hires": "qwen_image_edit_20B",
    99→    "qwen_image_style": "qwen_image_edit_20B",
   100→    "image_inpaint": "qwen_image_edit_20B",
   101→    "annotated_image_edit": "qwen_image_edit_20B",
   102→    # Text-to-image tasks

   104→    "qwen_image_2512": "qwen_image_2512_20B",
   105→    "z_image_turbo": "z_image",
   106→    # Image-to-image tasks
   107→    "z_image_turbo_i2i": "z_image_img2img",
   108→}
   109→
   110→
   111→def get_default_model(task_type: str) -> str:
   112→    """
   113→    Get the default model for a given task type.
   114→
   115→    Args:
   116→        task_type: The task type identifier
   117→
   118→    Returns:
   119→        The default model identifier, or "t2v" as fallback
   120→    """
   121→    return TASK_TYPE_TO_MODEL.get(task_type, "t2v")
   122→
   123→
   124→def is_wgp_task(task_type: str) -> bool:
   125→    """Check if a task type is a WGP generation task."""
   126→    return task_type in WGP_TASK_TYPES
   127→
   128→
   129→def is_direct_queue_task(task_type: str) -> bool:
   130→    """Check if a task type can be directly queued."""
   131→    return task_type in DIRECT_QUEUE_TASK_TYPES
   132→
```

     4→Initializes WGP runtime state (sys.path, config hygiene, Matplotlib backend),
     5→manages output-path configuration, traces LoRA application, runs preflight
     6→checks (directory verification, model-definition loading), and delegates

    10→import os

    13→from typing import Optional, List, TYPE_CHECKING

1→"""
     2→Uni3C ControlNet checkpoint loader with auto-download.
     3→
     4→This module handles downloading the Uni3C checkpoint from HuggingFace
     5→and loading it with automatic config inference.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import os
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Optional, Tuple, Dict, Any
    11→
    12→import torch
    13→from safetensors.torch import load_file
    14→
    15→# Module-level cache for controlnet (avoids reloading from disk each generation)
    16→_controlnet_cache = {
    17→    "model": None,
    18→    "device": None,
    19→    "dtype": None,
    20→    "used_this_task": False,  # Track if cache was used in current task
    21→}
    22→
    23→# HuggingFace repo and filename
    24→UNI3C_REPO_ID = "Kijai/WanVideo_comfy"
    25→UNI3C_CHECKPOINT_FILENAME = "Wan21_Uni3C_controlnet_fp16.safetensors"
    26→
    27→
    28→def get_uni3c_checkpoint_path(ckpts_dir: str = "ckpts") -> Path:
    29→    """Get the path where Uni3C checkpoint should be stored."""
    30→    return Path(ckpts_dir) / "controlnets" / UNI3C_CHECKPOINT_FILENAME
    31→
    32→
    33→def download_uni3c_checkpoint_if_missing(
    34→    ckpts_dir: str = "ckpts",
    35→    force_download: bool = False
    36→) -> str:
    37→    """
    38→    Download Uni3C checkpoint from HuggingFace if not present.
    39→    
    40→    Args:
    41→        ckpts_dir: Base checkpoints directory
    42→        force_download: If True, download even if file exists
    43→        
    44→    Returns:
    45→        Path to the checkpoint file
    46→    """
    47→    from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download
    48→    
    49→    target_path = get_uni3c_checkpoint_path(ckpts_dir)
    50→    target_dir = target_path.parent
    51→    
    52→    if target_path.exists() and not force_download:
    53→        print(f"[UNI3C] Checkpoint already exists: {target_path}")
    54→        return str(target_path)
    55→    
    56→    print(f"[UNI3C] Downloading checkpoint from {UNI3C_REPO_ID}...")
    57→    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    58→    
    59→    # Download using HuggingFace hub
    60→    downloaded_path = hf_hub_download(
    61→        repo_id=UNI3C_REPO_ID,
    62→        filename=UNI3C_CHECKPOINT_FILENAME,
    63→        local_dir=str(target_dir),
    64→        local_dir_use_symlinks=False
    65→    )
    66→    
    67→    print(f"[UNI3C] Downloaded checkpoint to: {downloaded_path}")
    68→    return downloaded_path
    69→
    70→
    71→def infer_config_from_checkpoint(state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    72→    """
    73→    Infer Uni3C config from checkpoint tensor shapes.
    74→    
    75→    This matches Kijai's approach in nodes.py for automatic config detection.
    76→    
    77→    Args:
    78→        state_dict: Loaded checkpoint state dict
    79→        
    80→    Returns:
    81→        Config dict with architecture parameters
    82→        
    83→    Raises:
    84→        ValueError: If checkpoint is invalid/missing required keys
    85→    """
    86→    # Required keys to verify checkpoint validity
    87→    if "controlnet_patch_embedding.weight" not in state_dict:
    88→        raise ValueError("[UNI3C] Invalid checkpoint: missing controlnet_patch_embedding.weight")
    89→    
    90→    if "controlnet_blocks.0.ffn.0.bias" not in state_dict:
    91→        raise ValueError("[UNI3C] Invalid checkpoint: missing controlnet_blocks.0.ffn.0.bias")
    92→    
    93→    # Infer from tensor shapes (matching Kijai's approach)
    94→    in_channels = state_dict["controlnet_patch_embedding.weight"].shape[1]
    95→    ffn_dim = state_dict["controlnet_blocks.0.ffn.0.bias"].shape[0]
    96→    
    97→    return {
    98→        "in_channels": in_channels,
    99→        "conv_out_dim": 5120,
   100→        "time_embed_dim": 5120,

1→"""
     2→WanControlNet - Uni3C ControlNet for Wan video generation.
     3→
     4→Directly ported from Kijai's ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper implementation.
     5→https://github.com/kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper/blob/main/uni3c/controlnet.py
     6→
     7→This provides video-to-video structural guidance through a ControlNet that
     8→extracts features from a guide/reference video and injects them into the
     9→main transformer's generation process.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→import torch
    13→import torch.nn as nn
    14→import numpy as np
    15→from typing import Optional, Tuple, Union, List
    16→import torch.nn.functional as F
    17→from diffusers.models.attention_processor import Attention
    18→
    19→from einops import rearrange
    20→
    21→
    22→def get_1d_rotary_pos_embed(
    23→    dim: int,
    24→    pos: Union[np.ndarray, int],
    25→    theta: float = 10000.0,
    26→    use_real=False,
    27→    linear_factor=1.0,
    28→    ntk_factor=1.0,
    29→    repeat_interleave_real=True,
    30→    freqs_dtype=torch.float32,
    31→):
    32→    """
    33→    Precompute the frequency tensor for complex exponentials (cis) with given dimensions.
    34→
    35→    This function calculates a frequency tensor with complex exponentials using the given dimension 'dim' and the end
    36→    index 'end'. The 'theta' parameter scales the frequencies. The returned tensor contains complex values in complex64
    37→    data type.
    38→
    39→    Args:
    40→        dim (`int`): Dimension of the frequency tensor.
    41→        pos (`np.ndarray` or `int`): Position indices for the frequency tensor. [S] or scalar
    42→        theta (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 10000.0):
    43→            Scaling factor for frequency computation. Defaults to 10000.0.
    44→        use_real (`bool`, *optional*):
    45→            If True, return real part and imaginary part separately. Otherwise, return complex numbers.
    46→        linear_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0):
    47→            Scaling factor for the context extrapolation. Defaults to 1.0.
    48→        ntk_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0):
    49→            Scaling factor for the NTK-Aware RoPE. Defaults to 1.0.
    50→        repeat_interleave_real (`bool`, *optional*, defaults to `True`):
    51→            If `True` and `use_real`, real part and imaginary part are each interleaved with themselves to reach `dim`.
    52→            Otherwise, they are concateanted with themselves.
    53→        freqs_dtype (`torch.float32` or `torch.float64`, *optional*, defaults to `torch.float32`):
    54→            the dtype of the frequency tensor.
    55→    Returns:
    56→        `torch.Tensor`: Precomputed frequency tensor with complex exponentials. [S, D/2]
    57→    """
    58→    assert dim % 2 == 0
    59→
    60→    if isinstance(pos, int):
    61→        pos = torch.arange(pos)
    62→    if isinstance(pos, np.ndarray):
    63→        pos = torch.from_numpy(pos)  # type: ignore  # [S]
    64→
    65→    theta = theta * ntk_factor
    66→    freqs = (
    67→        1.0 / (theta ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=freqs_dtype, device=pos.device) / dim)) / linear_factor
    68→    )  # [D/2]
    69→    freqs = torch.outer(pos, freqs)  # type: ignore   # [S, D/2]
    70→    is_npu = freqs.device.type == "npu"
    71→    if is_npu:
    72→        freqs = freqs.float()
    73→    if use_real and repeat_interleave_real:
    74→        # flux, hunyuan-dit, cogvideox
    75→        freqs_cos = freqs.cos().repeat_interleave(2, dim=1, output_size=freqs.shape[1] * 2).float()  # [S, D]
    76→        freqs_sin = freqs.sin().repeat_interleave(2, dim=1, output_size=freqs.shape[1] * 2).float()  # [S, D]
    77→        return freqs_cos, freqs_sin
    78→    elif use_real:
    79→        # stable audio, allegro
    80→        freqs_cos = torch.cat([freqs.cos(), freqs.cos()], dim=-1).float()  # [S, D]
    81→        freqs_sin = torch.cat([freqs.sin(), freqs.sin()], dim=-1).float()  # [S, D]
    82→        return freqs_cos, freqs_sin
    83→    else:
    84→        # lumina
    85→        freqs_cis = torch.polar(torch.ones_like(freqs), freqs)  # complex64     # [S, D/2]
    86→        return freqs_cis
    87→
    88→
    89→class WanRotaryPosEmbed(nn.Module):
    90→    def __init__(
    91→        self, attention_head_dim: int, patch_size: Tuple[int, int, int], max_seq_len: int, theta: float = 10000.0
    92→    ):
    93→        super().__init__()
    94→
    95→        self.attention_head_dim = attention_head_dim
    96→        self.patch_size = patch_size
    97→        self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len
    98→
    99→        h_dim = w_dim = 2 * (attention_head_dim // 6)
   100→        t_dim = attention_head_dim - h_dim - w_dim
```

1→"""
     2→WGP Generation Helpers
     3→
     4→Miscellaneous helper functions used during generation:
     5→- VACE wrapper for generate_video
     6→- Image loading and media path resolution
     7→- Model type checker functions
     8→- Worker notification and directory verification
     9→"""
    10→
    11→import os
    12→from typing import Optional
    13→
    14→from source.core.log import model_logger, generation_logger
    15→
    16→
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→# Worker / directory helpers (module-level functions)
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→
    21→def notify_worker_model_switch(old_model: Optional[str], new_model: str):
    22→    """
    23→    Notify Supabase edge function that a model switch is about to happen.
    24→
    25→    Called BEFORE the actual model load so the worker status can be updated
    26→    to show it's switching models (which can take 30-60+ seconds).
    27→
    28→    Only fires if WORKER_ID is set (i.e., running in cloud worker context).
    29→    """
    30→    worker_id = os.getenv("WORKER_ID")
    31→    if not worker_id:
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    # Prefer service role; fall back to older env var name if present.
    35→    supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") or os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY")
    36→    if not supabase_key:
    37→        model_logger.debug("No SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, skipping model switch notification")
    38→        return
    39→
    40→    try:
    41→        import httpx
    42→    except ImportError as e:
    43→        model_logger.warning(f"Failed to notify model switch: {e}")
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    try:
    47→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "https://[REDACTED]")
    48→        response = httpx.post(
    49→            f"{supabase_url.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/update-worker-model",
    50→            headers={
    51→                "Authorization": f"Bearer {supabase_key}",
    52→                "Content-Type": "application/json"
    53→            },
    54→            json={
    55→                "worker_id": worker_id,
    56→                # Keep payload minimal to avoid schema mismatches server-side.
    57→                "model": new_model,
    58→            },
    59→            timeout=5.0  # Don't block model loading on this
    60→        )
    61→
    62→        if response.status_code == 200:
    63→            model_logger.debug(f"Notified edge function of model switch: {old_model} → {new_model}")
    64→        else:
    65→            model_logger.warning(f"Edge function returned {response.status_code}: {response.text[:200]}")
    66→
    67→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
    68→        # Don't let notification failures block model loading
    69→        model_logger.warning(f"Failed to notify model switch: {e}")
    70→
    71→
    72→def verify_wgp_directory(logger, context: str = ""):
    73→    """
    74→    Verify we're still in the Wan2GP directory after any operation.
    75→
    76→    Wan2GP expects to run from its directory and uses relative paths.
    77→    Call this after any wgp operation to catch directory changes early.
    78→
    79→    Args:
    80→        logger: Logger instance to use
    81→        context: Description of what just happened (e.g., "after wgp.generate_video()")
    82→    """
    83→    current_dir = os.getcwd()
    84→    expected_substr = "Wan2GP"
    85→
    86→    if expected_substr not in current_dir:
    87→        logger.warning(
    88→            f"[PATH_CHECK] {context}: Current directory may be wrong!\n"
    89→            f"  Current: {current_dir}\n"
    90→            f"  Expected: Path containing 'Wan2GP'\n"
    91→            f"  This could cause issues with wgp.py's relative paths!"
    92→        )
    93→    else:
    94→        logger.debug(f"[PATH_CHECK] {context}: Still in Wan2GP directory ✓ ({current_dir})")
    95→
    96→    # Also verify critical dirs still accessible
    97→    if not os.path.exists("defaults"):
    98→        logger.error(
    99→            f"[PATH_CHECK] {context}: CRITICAL - defaults/ no longer accessible!\n"
   100→            f"  Current directory: {current_dir}"
   101→        )
   102→
   103→    return current_dir
   104→
   105→
   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→# VACE wrapper
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→
   110→def create_vace_fixed_generate_video(original_generate_video):
   111→    """Create a wrapper around generate_video for VACE models.
   112→
   113→    VACE models are properly loaded via load_models() now, so no special handling needed.
   114→    Also handles parameter name mapping for compatibility.
   115→
   116→    Args:
   117→        original_generate_video: The upstream wgp.generate_video function
   118→
   119→    Returns:
   120→        Wrapped generate_video function
   121→    """
   122→    def vace_fixed_generate_video(*args, **kwargs):
   123→        # Map parameter names for compatibility
   124→        if "denoise_strength" in kwargs:
   125→            kwargs["denoising_strength"] = kwargs.pop("denoise_strength")
   126→
   127→        # VACE modules are now properly loaded via load_models() - no patching needed
   128→        return original_generate_video(*args, **kwargs)
   129→
   130→    return vace_fixed_generate_video
   131→
   132→
   133→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   134→# Image loading / media path resolution
   135→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   136→
   137→def load_image(orchestrator, path: Optional[str], mask: bool = False):
   138→    """Load an image from a path, resolving it via the orchestrator's media path logic.
   139→
   140→    Args:
   141→        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance (provides _resolve_media_path)
   142→        path: Path to the image file
   143→        mask: If True, convert to grayscale ("L") mode
   144→
   145→    Returns:
   146→        PIL.Image or None
   147→    """
   148→    if not path:
   149→        return None
   150→    try:
   151→        from PIL import Image  # type: ignore
   152→        p = resolve_media_path(orchestrator, path)
   153→        img = Image.open(p)
   154→        if mask:
   155→            try:
   156→                return img.convert("L")
   157→            except (OSError, ValueError):
   158→                return img
   159→        else:
   160→            try:
   161→                return img.convert("RGB")
   162→            except (OSError, ValueError):
   163→                return img
   164→    except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError) as e:
   165→        generation_logger.warning(f"Could not load image from {path}: {e}")
   166→        return None
   167→
   168→
   169→def resolve_media_path(orchestrator, path: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
   170→    """Resolve media paths relative to the local Headless-Wan2GP repo.
   171→
   172→    - If the path exists as-is (absolute), return it.
   173→    - If relative, prefer repo root (agent_tasks/Headless-Wan2GP), then Wan2GP.
   174→
   175→    Args:
   176→        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance (provides wan_root)
   177→        path: Path to resolve
   178→
   179→    Returns:
   180→        Resolved path string or original path
   181→    """
   182→    if not path:
   183→        return path
   184→    try:
   185→        from pathlib import Path
   186→        p = Path(path)
   187→        if p.exists():
   188→            return str(p.resolve())
   189→        wan_root = Path(orchestrator.wan_root)
   190→        repo_root = wan_root.parent
   191→        if not p.is_absolute():
   192→            candidate = repo_root / p
   193→            if candidate.exists():
   194→                return str(candidate.resolve())
   195→            candidate = wan_root / p
   196→            if candidate.exists():
   197→                return str(candidate.resolve())
   198→    except (OSError, ValueError):
   199→        pass  # Path resolution failed; fall back to returning original path
   200→    return path
   201→
   202→
   203→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   204→# Model type checkers
   205→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   206→
   207→def is_vace(orchestrator) -> bool:
   208→    """Check if current model is a VACE model."""
   209→    return orchestrator._test_vace_module(orchestrator.current_model)
   210→
   211→
   212→def is_model_vace(orchestrator, model_name: str) -> bool:
   213→    """Check if a given model name is a VACE model (model-agnostic).
   214→
   215→    This method doesn't require the model to be loaded, making it suitable
   216→    for VACE detection during task processing when the orchestrator may not
   217→    have the model loaded yet.
   218→
   219→    Args:
   220→        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance
   221→        model_name: The model identifier to check (e.g., "vace_14B", "t2v")
   222→
   223→    Returns:
   224→        True if the model is a VACE model, False otherwise
   225→    """
   226→    return orchestrator._test_vace_module(model_name)
   227→
   228→
   229→def is_flux(orchestrator) -> bool:
   230→    """Check if current model is a Flux model."""
   231→    return orchestrator._get_base_model_type(orchestrator.current_model) == "flux"
   232→
   233→
   234→def is_t2v(orchestrator) -> bool:
   235→    """Check if current model is a T2V model."""
   236→    base_type = orchestrator._get_base_model_type(orchestrator.current_model)
   237→    return base_type in ["t2v", "t2v_1.3B", "hunyuan", "ltxv_13B", "ltx2_19B"]
   238→
   239→
   240→def is_ltx2(orchestrator) -> bool:
   241→    """Check if current model is an LTX-2 model."""
   242→    base_type = (orchestrator._get_base_model_type(orchestrator.current_model) or "").lower()
   243→    return base_type.startswith("ltx2")
   244→
   245→
   246→def is_qwen(orchestrator) -> bool:
   247→    """Check if current model is a Qwen image model."""
   248→    try:
   249→        if orchestrator._get_model_family(orchestrator.current_model) == "qwen":
   250→            return True
   251→    except (ValueError, KeyError, AttributeError) as e:
   252→        model_logger.debug("Failed to check model family for qwen detection: %s", e)
   253→    base_type = (orchestrator._get_base_model_type(orchestrator.current_model) or "").lower()
   254→    return base_type.startswith("qwen")
   255→

1→"""Pre-generation setup: SVI bridging, model-specific parameter configuration,
     2→and image input preparation.
     3→
     4→These functions run before the WGP generate_video() call to ensure all
     5→parameters are in the format that WGP expects.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, Optional
     9→
    10→from source.core.log import generation_logger, is_debug_enabled
    11→
    12→
    13→def prepare_svi_image_refs(kwargs: dict) -> None:
    14→    """Convert ``image_refs_paths`` (list[str]) to PIL ``image_refs`` (in-place).
    15→
    16→    Our task pipeline passes paths for JSON-serializability, but Wan2GP/WGP
    17→    expects ``image_refs`` as a list of PIL.Image objects.
    18→    """
    19→    try:
    20→        if (
    21→            "image_refs_paths" in kwargs
    22→            and kwargs.get("image_refs") in (None, "", [])
    23→            and isinstance(kwargs["image_refs_paths"], (list, tuple))
    24→            and len(kwargs["image_refs_paths"]) > 0
    25→        ):
    26→            from PIL import Image
    27→            from PIL import ImageOps
    28→
    29→            refs: list = []
    30→            for p in kwargs["image_refs_paths"]:
    31→                if not p:
    32→                    continue
    33→                try:
    34→                    img = Image.open(str(p)).convert("RGB")
    35→                    img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(img)
    36→                    refs.append(img)
    37→                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_img:
    38→                    if is_debug_enabled():
    39→                        generation_logger.warning(
    40→                            f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Failed to load image_ref path '{p}': {e_img}"
    41→                        )
    42→            if refs:
    43→                kwargs["image_refs"] = refs
    44→                if is_debug_enabled():
    45→                    generation_logger.info(
    46→                        f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Converted image_refs_paths -> image_refs (count={len(refs)})"
    47→                    )
    48→            else:
    49→                if is_debug_enabled():
    50→                    generation_logger.warning(
    51→                        "[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] image_refs_paths provided but no images could be loaded"
    52→                    )
    53→    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, TypeError) as e_refs:
    54→        if is_debug_enabled():
    55→            generation_logger.warning(
    56→                f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Exception while converting image_refs_paths -> image_refs: {e_refs}"
    57→            )
    58→
    59→
    60→def configure_model_specific_params(
    61→    *,
    62→    is_flux: bool,
    63→    is_qwen: bool,
    64→    is_vace: bool,
    65→    resolved_params: dict,
    66→    final_video_length: int,
    67→    final_batch_size: int,
    68→    final_guidance_scale: float,
    69→    final_embedded_guidance: float,
    70→    video_guide: Optional[str],
    71→    video_mask: Optional[str],
    72→    video_prompt_type: Optional[str],
    73→    control_net_weight: Optional[float],
    74→    control_net_weight2: Optional[float],
    75→    min_frames: int = 5,
    76→) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    77→    """Compute model-specific generation parameters.
    78→
    79→    Returns a dict with keys:
    80→        image_mode, actual_video_length, actual_batch_size, actual_guidance,
    81→        video_guide, video_mask, video_prompt_type, control_net_weight, control_net_weight2
    82→    """
    83→    if is_flux:
    84→        image_mode = 1
    85→        actual_video_length = 1
    86→        actual_batch_size = final_video_length
    87→        actual_guidance = final_embedded_guidance
    88→    elif is_qwen:
    89→        image_mode = 1
    90→        actual_video_length = 1
    91→        actual_batch_size = resolved_params.get("batch_size", 1)
    92→        actual_guidance = final_guidance_scale
    93→    else:
    94→        # T2V or VACE
    95→        image_mode = 0
    96→        actual_video_length = final_video_length
    97→
    98→        # Safety check: models crash if video_length is too short
    99→        if actual_video_length < min_frames:
   100→            generation_logger.warning(
   101→                f"[SAFETY] Boosting video_length from {actual_video_length} to {min_frames} "
   102→                "to prevent quantization crash"
   103→            )
   104→            actual_video_length = min_frames
   105→
   106→        actual_batch_size = final_batch_size
   107→        actual_guidance = final_guidance_scale
   108→
   109→    # Disable VACE controls when not applicable
   110→    if not is_vace and not video_guide and not video_mask:
   111→        video_guide = None
   112→        video_mask = None
   113→        if video_prompt_type is None or video_prompt_type == "":
   114→            video_prompt_type = "disabled"
   115→        control_net_weight = 0.0
   116→        control_net_weight2 = 0.0
   117→
   118→    return {
   119→        "image_mode": image_mode,
   120→        "actual_video_length": actual_video_length,
   121→        "actual_batch_size": actual_batch_size,
   122→        "actual_guidance": actual_guidance,
   123→        "video_guide": video_guide,
   124→        "video_mask": video_mask,
   125→        "video_prompt_type": video_prompt_type,
   126→        "control_net_weight": control_net_weight,
   127→        "control_net_weight2": control_net_weight2,
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def prepare_image_inputs(
   132→    wgp_params: Dict[str, Any],
   133→    *,
   134→    is_qwen: bool,
   135→    image_mode: int,
   136→    load_image_fn: Callable,
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Load PIL images for image_start/image_end/image_guide/image_mask (in-place).
   139→
   140→    WGP expects PIL Image objects, not file paths.  This function converts
   141→    string paths to loaded images and handles resolution matching.
   142→    """
   143→    # Extract target resolution for resizing
   144→    target_width, target_height = None, None
   145→    if wgp_params.get('resolution'):
   146→        try:
   147→            w_str, h_str = wgp_params['resolution'].split('x')
   148→            target_width, target_height = int(w_str), int(h_str)
   149→            generation_logger.info(
   150→                f"[PREFLIGHT] Target resolution for image resizing: {target_width}x{target_height}"
   151→            )
   152→        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as e_res:
   153→            generation_logger.warning(
   154→                f"[PREFLIGHT] Could not parse resolution '{wgp_params.get('resolution')}': {e_res}"
   155→            )
   156→
   157→    # Load image_start / image_end
   158→    for img_param in ('image_start', 'image_end'):
   159→        val = wgp_params.get(img_param)
   160→        if not val:
   161→            continue
   162→
   163→        if isinstance(val, str):
   164→            generation_logger.info(f"[PREFLIGHT] Loading {img_param} from path: {val}")
   165→            img = load_image_fn(val, mask=False)
   166→            if img and target_width and target_height:
   167→                from PIL import Image
   168→                if img.size != (target_width, target_height):
   169→                    generation_logger.info(
   170→                        f"[PREFLIGHT] Resizing {img_param} from "
   171→                        f"{img.size[0]}x{img.size[1]} to {target_width}x{target_height}"
   172→                    )
   173→                    img = img.resize((target_width, target_height), Image.LANCZOS)
   174→            wgp_params[img_param] = img
   175→
   176→        elif isinstance(val, list) and len(val) > 0 and isinstance(val[0], str):
   177→            generation_logger.info(f"[PREFLIGHT] Loading {img_param} from list of paths")
   178→            loaded_imgs = []
   179→            for p in val:
   180→                img = load_image_fn(p, mask=False)
   181→                if img and target_width and target_height:
   182→                    from PIL import Image
   183→                    if img.size != (target_width, target_height):
   184→                        generation_logger.info(
   185→                            f"[PREFLIGHT] Resizing {img_param} image from "
   186→                            f"{img.size[0]}x{img.size[1]} to {target_width}x{target_height}"
   187→                        )
   188→                        img = img.resize((target_width, target_height), Image.LANCZOS)
   189→                loaded_imgs.append(img)
   190→            wgp_params[img_param] = loaded_imgs
   191→
   192→    # For image-based models, load guide and mask images
   193→    if is_qwen or image_mode == 1:
   194→        if wgp_params.get('image_guide') and isinstance(wgp_params['image_guide'], str):
   195→            wgp_params['image_guide'] = load_image_fn(wgp_params['image_guide'], mask=False)
   196→        if wgp_params.get('image_mask') and isinstance(wgp_params['image_mask'], str):
   197→            wgp_params['image_mask'] = load_image_fn(wgp_params['image_mask'], mask=True)
   198→
   199→        # Ensure proper parameter coordination for Qwen models
   200→        if is_qwen:
   201→            if not wgp_params.get('image_mask'):
   202→                wgp_params['image_mask'] = None
   203→                generation_logger.debug("[PREFLIGHT] Ensured image_mask=None for Qwen regular generation")
   204→            else:
   205→                wgp_params['model_mode'] = 1
   206→                generation_logger.info("[PREFLIGHT] Set model_mode=1 for Qwen inpainting (image_mask present)")
   207→
   208→        # Preflight logs for image models
   209→        try:
   210→            ig = wgp_params.get('image_guide')
   211→            if ig is not None:
   212→                from PIL import Image as _PILImage
   213→                if isinstance(ig, _PILImage.Image):
   214→                    generation_logger.info(
   215→                        "[PREFLIGHT] image_guide resolved to PIL.Image with size %sx%s and mode %s"
   216→                        % (ig.size[0], ig.size[1], ig.mode)
   217→                    )
   218→                else:
   219→                    generation_logger.warning(
   220→                        f"[PREFLIGHT] image_guide is not a PIL image (type={type(ig)})"
   221→                    )
   222→            else:
   223→                generation_logger.warning("[PREFLIGHT] image_guide is None for image model")
   224→        except (AttributeError, TypeError) as _e:
   225→            generation_logger.warning(f"[PREFLIGHT] Could not inspect image_guide: {_e}")
   226→
   227→    # Sanitize image_refs: WGP expects None when there are no refs
   228→    try:
   229→        if isinstance(wgp_params.get('image_refs'), list) and len(wgp_params['image_refs']) == 0:
   230→            wgp_params['image_refs'] = None
   231→    except (TypeError, KeyError):
   232→        pass
   233→
```

@@ -392,6 +392,176 @@ class WanAny2V:
 
         return mocha_latents, (torch.cat(cos_parts, dim=0), torch.cat(sin_parts, dim=0))
 
+    def _load_uni3c_guide_video(
+        self,
+        guide_video_path: str,
+        target_height: int,
+        target_width: int,
+        target_frames: int,
+        frame_policy: str = "fit",
+    ) -> torch.Tensor:
+        """Load and preprocess guide video for Uni3C."""
+        import cv2
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Loading guide video from {guide_video_path}")
+
+        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(guide_video_path)
+        if not cap.isOpened():
+            raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] Could not open guide video: {guide_video_path}")
+
+        frames = []
+        while True:
+            ret, frame = cap.read()
+            if not ret:
+                break
+            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
+            frame = cv2.resize(frame, (target_width, target_height))
+            frames.append(frame)
+        cap.release()
+
+        if len(frames) == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] No frames loaded from guide video: {guide_video_path}")
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Loaded {len(frames)} frames from guide video")
+        frames = self._apply_uni3c_frame_policy(frames, target_frames, frame_policy)
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: After frame policy '{frame_policy}': {len(frames)} frames (target: {target_frames})")
+
+        video = np.stack(frames, axis=0)
+        video = video.astype(np.float32)
+        video = (video / 127.5) - 1.0
+        video = torch.from_numpy(video).permute(3, 0, 1, 2)
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Guide video tensor shape: {tuple(video.shape)}, dtype: {video.dtype}")
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   value range: [{video.min().item():.2f}, {video.max().item():.2f}]")
+        return video
+
+    def _apply_uni3c_frame_policy(
+        self,
+        frames: list,
+        target_frames: int,
+        policy: str,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Apply frame alignment policy to match target frame count."""
+        current = len(frames)
+
+        if policy == "off":
+            if current != target_frames:
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"[UNI3C] Frame count mismatch: guide has {current} frames, "
+                    f"target is {target_frames}. Use a different frame_policy."
+                )
+            return frames
+
+        if policy == "fit":
+            if current == target_frames:
+                return frames
+            indices = np.linspace(0, current - 1, target_frames).astype(int)
+            return [frames[i] for i in indices]
+
+        if policy == "trim":
+            if current >= target_frames:
+                return frames[:target_frames]
+            return frames + [frames[-1]] * (target_frames - current)
+
+        if policy == "loop":
+            if current >= target_frames:
+                return frames[:target_frames]
+            result = []
+            while len(result) < target_frames:
+                result.extend(frames)
+            return result[:target_frames]
+
+        raise ValueError(f"[UNI3C] Unknown frame_policy: {policy}")
+
+    def _detect_empty_frames(
+        self,
+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,
+        threshold: float = 0.02,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Detect near-black frames in guide video and mark them empty."""
+        with torch.no_grad():
+            delta_from_black = (guide_video + 1.0).abs()
+            per_frame_delta = delta_from_black.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+            empty = per_frame_delta < threshold
+        return [bool(x) for x in empty.tolist()]
+
+    def _map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames(
+        self,
+        num_pixel_frames: int,
+        num_latent_frames: int,
+    ) -> list:
+        """Map pixel frame indices to latent frame indices."""
+        mapping = []
+        for lat_f in range(num_latent_frames):
+            start_pix = lat_f * 4
+            end_pix = min(start_pix + 4, num_pixel_frames)
+            mapping.append(list(range(start_pix, end_pix)))
+        return mapping
+
+    def _encode_uni3c_guide(
+        self,
+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,
+        VAE_tile_size: int,
+        expected_channels: int = 20,
+        zero_empty_frames: bool = True,
+    ) -> torch.Tensor:
+        """VAE-encode guide video and optionally zero empty-frame latent groups."""
+        num_pixel_frames = guide_video.shape[1]
+        empty_pixel_mask = []
+        if zero_empty_frames:
+            empty_pixel_mask = self._detect_empty_frames(guide_video)
+            num_empty = sum(empty_pixel_mask)
+            if num_empty > 0:
+                print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Detected {num_empty}/{num_pixel_frames} empty pixel frames")
+                ranges = []
+                start = None
+                for i, is_empty in enumerate(empty_pixel_mask):
+                    if is_empty and start is None:
+                        start = i
+                    elif not is_empty and start is not None:
+                        ranges.append(f"{start}-{i-1}" if i - 1 > start else str(start))
+                        start = None
+                if start is not None:
+                    ranges.append(f"{start}-{num_pixel_frames-1}" if num_pixel_frames - 1 > start else str(start))
+                if ranges:
+                    print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   Empty frame ranges: {', '.join(ranges)}")
+
+        guide_video = guide_video.to(device=self.device, dtype=self.VAE_dtype)
+        latent = self.vae.encode([guide_video], tile_size=VAE_tile_size)[0]
+        render_latent = latent.unsqueeze(0)
+
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: VAE encoded render_latent shape: {tuple(render_latent.shape)}")
+        print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   Expected channels: {expected_channels}, actual: {render_latent.shape[1]}")
+        print(
+            f"[UNI3C_DIAG] Latent stats: mean={render_latent.mean().item():.4f}, "
+            f"std={render_latent.std().item():.4f}"
+        )
+        print(
+            f"[UNI3C_DIAG] Latent range: min={render_latent.min().item():.4f}, "
+            f"max={render_latent.max().item():.4f}"
+        )
+
+        if zero_empty_frames and any(empty_pixel_mask):
+            num_latent_frames = render_latent.shape[2]
+            pixel_to_latent = self._map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames(num_pixel_frames, num_latent_frames)
+
+            zeroed_count = 0
+            for lat_f, pixel_indices in enumerate(pixel_to_latent):
+                if all(empty_pixel_mask[p] for p in pixel_indices if p < len(empty_pixel_mask)):
+                    render_latent[:, :, lat_f, :, :] = 0.0
+                    zeroed_count += 1
+
+            if zeroed_count > 0:
+                print(f"[UNI3C] any2video: Zeroed {zeroed_count}/{num_latent_frames} latent frames (no control signal)")
+
+        if render_latent.shape[1] == 16 and expected_channels == 20:
+            print("[UNI3C] any2video: Padding channels 16 -> 20")
+            padding = torch.zeros_like(render_latent[:, :4])
+            render_latent = torch.cat([render_latent, padding], dim=1)
+            print(f"[UNI3C] any2video:   After padding: {tuple(render_latent.shape)}")
+
+        return render_latent
+

   110→    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   111→        batch_size, num_channels, num_frames, height, width = hidden_states.shape
   112→        p_t, p_h, p_w = self.patch_size
   113→        ppf, pph, ppw = num_frames // p_t, height // p_h, width // p_w
   114→
   115→        self.freqs = self.freqs.to(hidden_states.device)
   116→        freqs = self.freqs.split_with_sizes(
   117→            [
   118→                self.attention_head_dim // 2 - 2 * (self.attention_head_dim // 6),
   119→                self.attention_head_dim // 6,
   120→                self.attention_head_dim // 6,
   121→            ],
   122→            dim=1,
   123→        )
   124→
   125→        freqs_f = freqs[0][:ppf].view(ppf, 1, 1, -1).expand(ppf, pph, ppw, -1)
   126→        freqs_h = freqs[1][:pph].view(1, pph, 1, -1).expand(ppf, pph, ppw, -1)
   127→        freqs_w = freqs[2][:ppw].view(1, 1, ppw, -1).expand(ppf, pph, ppw, -1)
   128→        freqs = torch.cat([freqs_f, freqs_h, freqs_w], dim=-1).reshape(1, 1, ppf * pph * ppw, -1)
   129→        return freqs
