   132→def sageattn_func(query, key, value, attn_mask=None, dropout_p=0.0, is_causal=False):
   133→    """
   134→    Fallback to SDPA if SageAttention is not available.
   135→    SageAttention is an optimized attention implementation, but SDPA works fine.
   136→    """
   137→    try:
   138→        from sageattention import sageattn
   139→        return sageattn(query, key, value, attn_mask=attn_mask, dropout_p=dropout_p, is_causal=is_causal)
   140→    except ImportError:
   141→        # Fallback to standard SDPA
   142→        return F.scaled_dot_product_attention(query, key, value, attn_mask=attn_mask, dropout_p=dropout_p, is_causal=is_causal)

   149→    def __call__(

   158→        query = attn.to_q(hidden_states)

   171→        if rotary_emb is not None:
   172→            def apply_rotary_emb(hidden_states: torch.Tensor, freqs: torch.Tensor):
   173→                x_rotated = torch.view_as_complex(hidden_states.to(torch.float64).unflatten(3, (-1, 2)))
   174→                x_out = torch.view_as_real(x_rotated * freqs).flatten(3, 4)

   183→            )

   196→class SimpleCogVideoXLayerNormZero(nn.Module):
   197→    def __init__(
   198→            self,
   199→            conditioning_dim: int,
   200→            embedding_dim: int,
   201→            elementwise_affine: bool = True,
   202→            eps: float = 1e-5,
   203→            bias: bool = True,
   204→    ) -> None:
   205→        super().__init__()
   206→
   207→        self.silu = nn.SiLU()
   208→        self.linear = nn.Linear(conditioning_dim, 3 * embedding_dim, bias=bias)
   209→        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(embedding_dim, eps=eps, elementwise_affine=elementwise_affine)
   210→
   211→    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, temb: torch.Tensor):
   212→        shift, scale, gate = self.linear(self.silu(temb)).chunk(3, dim=1)
   213→        hidden_states = self.norm(hidden_states) * (1 + scale)[:, None, :] + shift[:, None, :]
   214→        return hidden_states, gate[:, None, :]
   215→
   216→
   217→class SingleAttentionBlock(nn.Module):
   218→
   219→    def __init__(
   220→            self,
   221→            dim,
   222→            ffn_dim,
   223→            num_heads,
   224→            time_embed_dim=512,
   225→            qk_norm="rms_norm_across_heads",
   226→            eps=1e-6,
   227→            attention_mode="sdpa",
   228→    ):
   229→        super().__init__()
   230→        self.dim = dim
   231→        self.ffn_dim = ffn_dim
   232→        self.num_heads = num_heads
   233→        self.qk_norm = qk_norm
   234→        self.eps = eps
   235→
   236→        # layers
   237→        self.norm1 = SimpleCogVideoXLayerNormZero(
   238→            time_embed_dim, dim, elementwise_affine=True, eps=1e-5, bias=True
   239→        )
   240→        self.self_attn = Attention(
   241→            query_dim=dim,
   242→            heads=num_heads,
   243→            kv_heads=num_heads,
   244→            dim_head=dim // num_heads,
   245→            qk_norm=qk_norm,
   246→            eps=eps,
   247→            bias=True,
   248→            cross_attention_dim=None,
   249→            out_bias=True,
   250→            processor=SimpleAttnProcessor2_0(attention_mode),
   251→        )
   252→        self.norm2 = SimpleCogVideoXLayerNormZero(
   253→            time_embed_dim, dim, elementwise_affine=True, eps=1e-5, bias=True
   254→        )
   255→        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(
   256→            nn.Linear(dim, ffn_dim),
   257→            nn.GELU(approximate='tanh'),
   258→            nn.Linear(ffn_dim, dim)
   259→        )
   260→
   261→    def forward(
   262→            self,
   263→            hidden_states,
   264→            temb,
   265→            rotary_emb,
   266→    ):
   267→        # norm & modulate
   268→        norm_hidden_states, gate_msa = self.norm1(hidden_states, temb)
   269→
   270→        # attention
   271→        attn_hidden_states = self.self_attn(hidden_states=norm_hidden_states,
   272→                                            rotary_emb=rotary_emb)
   273→
   274→        hidden_states = hidden_states + gate_msa * attn_hidden_states
   275→
   276→        # norm & modulate
   277→        norm_hidden_states, gate_ff = self.norm2(hidden_states, temb)
   278→
   279→        # feed-forward
   280→        ff_output = self.ffn(norm_hidden_states)
   281→
   282→        hidden_states = hidden_states + gate_ff * ff_output
   283→
   284→        return hidden_states
   285→
   286→
   287→class MaskCamEmbed(nn.Module):
   288→    def __init__(self, controlnet_cfg) -> None:
   289→        super().__init__()
   290→
   291→        # padding bug fixed
   292→        if controlnet_cfg.get("interp", False):
   293→            self.mask_padding = [0, 0, 0, 0, 3, 3]  # left/right/top/bottom/front/back, I2V-interp
   294→        else:
   295→            self.mask_padding = [0, 0, 0, 0, 3, 0]  # left/right/top/bottom/front/back, I2V
   296→        add_channels = controlnet_cfg.get("add_channels", 1)
   297→        mid_channels = controlnet_cfg.get("mid_channels", 64)
   298→        self.mask_proj = nn.Sequential(
   299→            nn.Conv3d(add_channels, mid_channels, kernel_size=(4, 8, 8), stride=(4, 8, 8)),
   300→            nn.GroupNorm(mid_channels // 8, mid_channels),
   301→            nn.SiLU()
   302→        )
   303→        self.mask_zero_proj = nn.Conv3d(mid_channels, controlnet_cfg["conv_out_dim"], kernel_size=(1, 2, 2), stride=(1, 2, 2))
   304→
   305→    def forward(self, add_inputs: torch.Tensor):
   306→        # render_mask.shape [b,c,f,h,w]
   307→        warp_add_pad = F.pad(add_inputs, self.mask_padding, mode="constant", value=0)
   308→        add_embeds = self.mask_proj(warp_add_pad)  # [B,C,F,H,W]
   309→        add_embeds = self.mask_zero_proj(add_embeds)
   310→        add_embeds = rearrange(add_embeds, "b c f h w -> b (f h w) c")
   311→
   312→        return add_embeds
   313→
   314→
   315→class WanControlNet(nn.Module):
   316→    """
   317→    Uni3C ControlNet for Wan video generation.
   318→
   319→    Ported directly from Kijai's ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper implementation
   320→    to ensure checkpoint compatibility.
   321→    """
   322→
   323→    def __init__(self, controlnet_cfg):
   324→        super().__init__()
   325→
   326→        self.rope_max_seq_len = 1024
   327→        self.patch_size = (1, 2, 2)
   328→        self.in_channels = controlnet_cfg["in_channels"]
   329→        self.dim = controlnet_cfg["dim"]
   330→        self.num_heads = controlnet_cfg["num_heads"]
   331→        self.quantized = controlnet_cfg.get("quantized", False)
   332→        self.base_dtype = controlnet_cfg.get("base_dtype", torch.float16)
   333→
   334→        if controlnet_cfg["conv_out_dim"] != controlnet_cfg["dim"]:
   335→            self.proj_in = nn.Linear(controlnet_cfg["conv_out_dim"], controlnet_cfg["dim"])
   336→        else:
   337→            self.proj_in = nn.Identity()
   338→
   339→        self.controlnet_blocks = nn.ModuleList(
   340→            [
   341→                SingleAttentionBlock(
   342→                    dim=self.dim,
   343→                    ffn_dim=controlnet_cfg["ffn_dim"],
   344→                    num_heads=self.num_heads,
   345→                    time_embed_dim=controlnet_cfg["time_embed_dim"],
   346→                    qk_norm="rms_norm_across_heads",
   347→                    attention_mode=controlnet_cfg.get("attention_mode", "sdpa"),
   348→                )
   349→                for _ in range(controlnet_cfg["num_layers"])
   350→            ]
   351→        )
   352→        self.proj_out = nn.ModuleList(
   353→            [
   354→                nn.Linear(self.dim, 5120)
   355→                for _ in range(controlnet_cfg["num_layers"])
   356→            ]
   357→        )
   358→
   359→        self.gradient_checkpointing = False
   360→
   361→        self.controlnet_rope = WanRotaryPosEmbed(
   362→            self.dim // self.num_heads,
   363→            self.patch_size,
   364→            self.rope_max_seq_len
   365→        )
   366→
   367→        self.controlnet_patch_embedding = nn.Conv3d(
   368→            self.in_channels,
   369→            controlnet_cfg["conv_out_dim"],
   370→            kernel_size=self.patch_size,
   371→            stride=self.patch_size,
   372→            dtype=torch.float32
   373→        )
   374→
   375→        self.controlnet_mask_embedding = MaskCamEmbed(controlnet_cfg)
   376→
   377→    def forward(
   378→        self,
   379→        render_latent: torch.Tensor,
   380→        render_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor],
   381→        camera_embedding: Optional[torch.Tensor],
   382→        temb: torch.Tensor,
   383→        out_device: torch.device,
   384→    ) -> List[torch.Tensor]:
   385→        """
   386→        Forward pass through the ControlNet.
   387→
   388→        Args:
   389→            render_latent: Guide video latents [B, C, F, H, W] (36 channels: 20 noisy + 16 guide)
   390→            render_mask: Optional mask tensor
   391→            camera_embedding: Optional camera embedding
   392→            temb: Timestep embedding [B, 5120]
   393→            out_device: Device to move outputs to
   394→
   395→        Returns:
   396→            List of 20 tensors, one per block, each shape [B, seq_len, 5120]
   397→        """
   398→        controlnet_rotary_emb = self.controlnet_rope(render_latent)
   399→        # Patch embedding is always float32 for precision (matching Kijai)
   400→        # Convert input to float32, run through embedding, convert back to working dtype
   401→        controlnet_inputs = self.controlnet_patch_embedding(render_latent.to(torch.float32))
   402→        if not self.quantized:
   403→            controlnet_inputs = controlnet_inputs.to(render_latent.dtype)
   404→        else:
   405→            controlnet_inputs = controlnet_inputs.to(self.base_dtype)
   406→
   407→        controlnet_inputs = controlnet_inputs.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)
   408→
   409→        # additional inputs (mask, camera embedding)
   410→        add_inputs = None
   411→        if camera_embedding is not None and render_mask is not None:
   412→            add_inputs = torch.cat([render_mask, camera_embedding], dim=1)
   413→        elif render_mask is not None:
   414→            add_inputs = render_mask
   415→
   416→        if add_inputs is not None:
   417→            add_inputs = self.controlnet_mask_embedding(add_inputs)
   418→            controlnet_inputs = controlnet_inputs + add_inputs
   419→
   420→        hidden_states = self.proj_in(controlnet_inputs)
   421→
   422→        controlnet_states = []
   423→        for i, block in enumerate(self.controlnet_blocks):
   424→            hidden_states = block(
   425→                hidden_states=hidden_states,
   426→                temb=temb,
   427→                rotary_emb=controlnet_rotary_emb
   428→            )
   429→            controlnet_states.append(self.proj_out[i](hidden_states).to(out_device))
   430→
   431→        return controlnet_states
   432→
```

+    def _compute_uni3c_states(self, uni3c_data: dict, temb: torch.Tensor, noisy_latent: torch.Tensor) -> list:
+        """Run Uni3C ControlNet forward pass and return per-block residuals."""
+        if uni3c_data is None:
+            return None
+        if "controlnet" not in uni3c_data or uni3c_data["controlnet"] is None:
+            raise ValueError("[UNI3C] Missing required key uni3c_data['controlnet']")
+        if "render_latent" not in uni3c_data or uni3c_data["render_latent"] is None:
+            raise ValueError("[UNI3C] Missing required key uni3c_data['render_latent']")
+
+        controlnet = uni3c_data["controlnet"]
+        render_latent = uni3c_data["render_latent"]
+        main_device = self.patch_embedding.weight.device
+        offload_device = torch.device("cpu")
+
+        controlnet = controlnet.to(main_device)
+        if temb.dim() == 1:
+            temb = temb.unsqueeze(0)
+        render_latent = render_latent.to(main_device)
+
+        hidden_states = noisy_latent.unsqueeze(0).clone().float() if noisy_latent.dim() == 4 else noisy_latent.clone().float()
+        if hidden_states.shape[1] == 16:
+            padding = torch.zeros_like(hidden_states[:, :4])
+            hidden_states = torch.cat([hidden_states, padding], dim=1)
+        hidden_states = hidden_states.to(main_device)
+
+        current_step = offload.shared_state.get("step_no", 0)
+        if current_step == 0:
+            print("[UNI3C DEBUG] Shape alignment before concat:")
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   hidden_states (noisy) shape: {tuple(hidden_states.shape)}")
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   render_latent (guide) shape: {tuple(render_latent.shape)}")
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   controlnet.in_channels: {getattr(controlnet, 'in_channels', 'N/A')}")
+
+        if hidden_states.shape[2:] != render_latent.shape[2:]:
+            if current_step == 0:
+                print(f"[UNI3C] Resampling render_latent from {tuple(render_latent.shape[2:])} -> {tuple(hidden_states.shape[2:])}")
+            render_latent = torch.nn.functional.interpolate(
+                render_latent.float(),
+                size=tuple(hidden_states.shape[2:]),
+                mode="trilinear",
+                align_corners=False,
+            ).to(render_latent.dtype)
+
+        render_latent_input = torch.cat([hidden_states[:, :20], render_latent], dim=1)
+        if current_step == 0:
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   final concatenated input shape: {tuple(render_latent_input.shape)}")
+            noisy_part = hidden_states[:, :20]
+            guide_part = render_latent
+            print(
+                f"[UNI3C_DIAG] Noisy latent (ch0-19): mean={noisy_part.mean().item():.4f}, "
+                f"std={noisy_part.std().item():.4f}, range=[{noisy_part.min().item():.4f}, {noisy_part.max().item():.4f}]"
+            )
+            print(
+                f"[UNI3C_DIAG] Guide latent (ch20-35): mean={guide_part.mean().item():.4f}, "
+                f"std={guide_part.std().item():.4f}, range=[{guide_part.min().item():.4f}, {guide_part.max().item():.4f}]"
+            )
+
+        controlnet_dtype = getattr(controlnet, "base_dtype", torch.float16)
+        with torch.autocast(device_type="cuda", dtype=controlnet_dtype, enabled=getattr(controlnet, "quantized", False)):
+            controlnet_states = controlnet(
+                render_latent=render_latent_input.to(main_device, controlnet_dtype),
+                render_mask=uni3c_data.get("render_mask"),
+                camera_embedding=uni3c_data.get("camera_embedding"),
+                temb=temb.to(main_device, controlnet_dtype),
+                out_device=offload_device if uni3c_data.get("offload") else main_device,
+            )
+
+        if current_step == 0 and controlnet_states:
+            print("[UNI3C DEBUG] Controlnet output:")
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   num states: {len(controlnet_states)}")
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   state[0] shape: {tuple(controlnet_states[0].shape)}")
+            print(f"[UNI3C DEBUG]   state[-1] shape: {tuple(controlnet_states[-1].shape)}")
+
+        if uni3c_data.get("offload", True):
+            controlnet.to(offload_device)
+        return controlnet_states
+

   129→def _make_orchestrator(wan_root: str, output_dir: Path):

   131→    os.environ["HEADLESS_WAN2GP_SMOKE"] = "1"
   132→    try:
   133→        from headless_wgp import WanOrchestrator
   134→        orch = WanOrchestrator(wan_root, main_output_dir=str(output_dir))
   135→    finally:
   136→        os.environ.pop("HEADLESS_WAN2GP_SMOKE", None)
   137→    return orch

   271→    def test_new_params_present_in_generate_video_signature(self):
   272→        """All new parameters should be in the upstream generate_video()
   273→        signature (confirming the Wan2GP sync is correct)."""
   274→        import ast
   275→
   276→        wgp_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "Wan2GP" / "wgp.py"
   277→        tree = ast.parse(wgp_path.read_text())
   278→        upstream_params = set()
   279→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
   280→            if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name == "generate_video":
   281→                upstream_params = {arg.arg for arg in node.args.args}
   282→                break
   283→
   284→        for p in self.NEW_PARAMS:
   285→            assert p in upstream_params, (
   286→                f"'{p}' not in upstream generate_video() signature"
   287→            )

   415→class TestLTX2EndToEndSmoke:
   416→    """End-to-end smoke test: submit a headless LTX-2 task with
   417→    use_ltx2=True, a start image, and an audio file.  Verify the
   418→    output video is produced in the output directory."""
   419→
   420→    def test_e2e_ltx2_smoke_generation(
   421→        self, _chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir, start_image, end_image, audio_file, sample_video
   422→    ):
   423→        """
   424→        Full end-to-end smoke test:
   425→        1. Create WanOrchestrator in smoke mode
   426→        2. Load ltx2_19B model
   427→        3. Call generate() with start_image, end_image, audio_input
   428→        4. Verify output file exists in the output directory
   429→        """
   430→        orch = _make_orchestrator(_chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir)
   431→
   432→        # --- Step 1: Load model ---
   433→        switched = orch.load_model("ltx2_19B")
   434→        assert switched is True, "Model should have switched (was None before)"
   435→        assert orch.current_model == "ltx2_19B"
   436→        assert orch._is_ltx2() is True
   437→
   438→        # --- Step 2: Generate with LTX-2 specific parameters ---
   439→        result_path = orch.generate(
   440→            prompt="A cinematic scene with ambient audio",
   441→            resolution="768x512",
   442→            video_length=49,
   443→            seed=12345,
   444→            start_image=start_image,
   445→            end_image=end_image,
   446→            audio_input=audio_file,
   447→            audio_scale=0.8,
   448→        )
   449→
   450→        # --- Step 3: Verify output ---
   451→        assert result_path is not None, "generate() returned None — no output produced"
   452→        assert os.path.isfile(result_path), f"Output file not found at: {result_path}"
   453→        assert os.path.getsize(result_path) > 0, f"Output file is empty: {result_path}"
   454→
   455→        # Verify the file is inside the expected output tree
   456→        result_abs = os.path.abspath(result_path)
   457→        output_abs = os.path.abspath(str(output_dir))
   458→        # Smoke mode writes to cwd/outputs, not necessarily our tmp output_dir,
   459→        # but the file should still exist on disk.
   460→        assert os.path.isfile(result_abs), (
   461→            f"Output not at expected absolute path: {result_abs}"
   462→        )
   463→
   464→        print(f"\n[PASS] LTX-2 E2E smoke test produced output: {result_path}")
   465→        print(f"       File size: {os.path.getsize(result_path)} bytes")
   466→
   467→    def test_e2e_model_reload_is_noop(
   468→        self, _chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir, sample_video
   469→    ):
   470→        """Loading the same model twice should not 'switch'."""
   471→        orch = _make_orchestrator(_chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir)
   472→        assert orch.load_model("ltx2_19B") is True   # first load
   473→        assert orch.load_model("ltx2_19B") is False  # noop
   474→

   476→class TestICLoraAudioImageSmoke:
   477→    """IC LoRA pipeline tests: signature introspection, audio conditioning
   478→    format, and smoke test with images + audio via WanOrchestrator.
   479→
   480→    NOTE: ICLoraPipeline is not currently reachable from the pipeline routing
   481→    in ltx2.py (line 447). Only "distilled" and "two_stage" kinds are handled.
   482→    The smoke test exercises the standard TI2VidTwoStagesPipeline path.
   483→    """
   484→
   485→    def test_ic_lora_with_images_and_audio(
   486→        self, _chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir, start_image, end_image, audio_file, sample_video
   487→    ):
   488→        """Smoke test: LTX-2 generation with start/end images and audio."""
   489→        orch = _make_orchestrator(_chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir)
   490→        orch.load_model("ltx2_19B")
   491→        result = orch.generate(
   492→            prompt="IC LoRA test with images and audio conditioning",
   493→            resolution="768x512",
   494→            video_length=49,
   495→            start_image=start_image,
   496→            end_image=end_image,
   497→            audio_input=audio_file,
   498→            audio_scale=0.8,
   499→            seed=42,
   500→        )
   501→        assert result is not None, "generate() returned None"
   502→        assert os.path.exists(result), f"Output file does not exist: {result}"
   503→        assert os.path.getsize(result) > 0, f"Output file is empty: {result}"
   504→
   505→    def test_ic_lora_pipeline_accepts_all_conditioning_types(self):
   506→        """ICLoraPipeline.__call__ should accept images, video_conditioning,
   507→        and audio_conditionings parameters (via AST to avoid heavy imports)."""
   508→        import ast
   509→
   510→        src_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "Wan2GP" / "models" / "ltx2" / "ltx_pipelines" / "ic_lora.py"
   511→        tree = ast.parse(src_path.read_text())
   512→
   513→        call_params = set()
   514→        call_annotations = {}
   515→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
   516→            if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name == "__call__":
   517→                for arg in node.args.args:
   518→                    call_params.add(arg.arg)
   519→                    if arg.annotation:
   520→                        call_annotations[arg.arg] = ast.dump(arg.annotation)
   521→                break
   522→
   523→        # Verify key conditioning parameters exist
   524→        assert "images" in call_params
   525→        assert "video_conditioning" in call_params
   526→        assert "audio_conditionings" in call_params
   527→
   528→        # Verify list type annotations via AST
   529→        assert "list" in call_annotations.get("images", "").lower()
   530→        assert "list" in call_annotations.get("video_conditioning", "").lower()
   531→
   532→    def test_audio_conditioning_format(self):
   533→        """AudioConditionByLatent should be instantiable and expose
   534→        latent, strength, and a callable apply_to method."""
   535→        try:
   536→            import torch
   537→            from Wan2GP.models.ltx2.ltx_core.conditioning.types.latent_cond import AudioConditionByLatent
   538→        except ImportError as exc:
   539→            pytest.skip(f"Skipping due to missing dependency: {exc}")
   540→
   541→        dummy = torch.zeros(1, 8, 4, 64)
   542→        cond = AudioConditionByLatent(latent=dummy, strength=0.8)
   543→
   544→        assert cond.latent is dummy
   545→        assert cond.strength == 0.8
   546→        assert hasattr(cond, "apply_to")
   547→        assert callable(cond.apply_to)
   548→
   549→
   550→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   551→# Standalone runner
   552→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   553→
   554→if __name__ == "__main__":
   555→    # Allow running without pytest installed
   556→    exit_code = pytest.main([__file__, "-v", "--tb=short"])
   557→    sys.exit(exit_code)
   558→

def generate(
        self,
        input_prompt: str,
        n_prompt: str | None = None,
        image_start=None,
        image_end=None,
        sampling_steps: int = 40,
        guide_scale: float = 4.0,
        alt_guide_scale: float = 1.0,
        input_video=None,
        prefix_frames_count: int = 0,
        input_frames=None,
        input_frames2=None,
        input_ref_images=None,
        input_masks=None,
        input_masks2=None,
        frames_relative_positions_list=None,
        masking_strength: float | None = None,
        input_video_strength: float | None = None,
        return_latent_slice=None,
        video_prompt_type: str = "",
        denoising_strength: float | None = None,
        cfg_star_switch: int = 0,
        apg_switch: int = 0,
        perturbation_switch: int = 0,
        perturbation_layers: list[int] | None = None,
        perturbation_start: float = 0.0,
        perturbation_end: float = 1.0,
        audio_cfg_scale: float | None = None,
        alt_scale: float = 0.0,
        self_refiner_setting: int = 0,
        self_refiner_plan: str = "",
        self_refiner_f_uncertainty: float = 0.1,
        self_refiner_certain_percentage: float = 0.999,
        loras_slists=None,
        loras_selected=None,
        text_connectors=None,
        input_waveform=None,
        input_waveform_sample_rate=None,
        audio_scale: float | None = None,
        masking_source: dict | None = None,
        frame_num: int = 121,
        height: int = 1024,
        width: int = 1536,
        fps: float = 24.0,
        seed: int = 0,
        callback=None,
        VAE_tile_size=None,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        if self._interrupt:
            return None

        image_start = _coerce_image_list(image_start)
        image_end = _coerce_image_list(image_end)
        if input_ref_images is None:
            input_ref_images = []
        elif isinstance(input_ref_images, (list, tuple)):
            input_ref_images = list(input_ref_images)
        else:
            input_ref_images = [input_ref_images]
        if frames_relative_positions_list is None:
            frames_relative_positions_list = []
        elif isinstance(frames_relative_positions_list, (list, tuple)):
            frames_relative_positions_list = list(frames_relative_positions_list)
        else:
            frames_relative_positions_list = [frames_relative_positions_list]

        prefix_frames_count = int(prefix_frames_count or 0)
        video_prompt_type = video_prompt_type or ""
        self_refiner_max_plans = int(self.model_def.get("self_refiner_max_plans", 1))

        def _get_frame_dim(video_tensor: torch.Tensor) -> int | None:
            if video_tensor.dim() < 2:
                return None
            if video_tensor.dim() == 5:
                if video_tensor.shape[1] in (1, 3, 4):
                    return 2
                if video_tensor.shape[-1] in (1, 3, 4):
                    return 1
            if video_tensor.shape[0] in (1, 3, 4):
                return 1
            if video_tensor.shape[-1] in (1, 3, 4):
                return 0
            return 0

        def _frame_count(video_value) -> int | None:
            if not torch.is_tensor(video_value):
                return None
            frame_dim = _get_frame_dim(video_value)
            if frame_dim is None:
                return None

class TaskRegistry:
    """Registry for task handlers."""
    
    @staticmethod
    def dispatch(task_type: str, context: "TaskDispatchContext") -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
        """Dispatch a task to the appropriate handler.

        Args:
            task_type: The type of task to execute.
            context: TaskDispatchContext with task_id, task_params_dict,
                     main_output_dir_base, project_id, task_queue, etc.

        Returns:
            Tuple (success, output_location)
        """
        task_id = context["task_id"]
        params = context["task_params_dict"]

        # 1. Direct Queue Tasks
        if task_type in DIRECT_QUEUE_TASK_TYPES and context["task_queue"]:
            return TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task(task_type, context)

        # 2. Orchestrator & Specialized Handlers
        handlers = {
            "travel_orchestrator": lambda: travel_orchestrator.handle_travel_orchestrator_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                orchestrator_task_id_str=task_id,
                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
            "travel_segment": lambda: handle_travel_segment_via_queue(
                task_params_dict=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id,
                colour_match_videos=context["colour_match_videos"],
                mask_active_frames=context["mask_active_frames"],
                task_queue=context["task_queue"],
                is_standalone=False
            ),
            "individual_travel_segment": lambda: handle_travel_segment_via_queue(
                task_params_dict=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id,
                colour_match_videos=context["colour_match_videos"],
                mask_active_frames=context["mask_active_frames"],
                task_queue=context["task_queue"],
                is_standalone=True
            ),
            "travel_stitch": lambda: _handle_travel_stitch_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                stitch_task_id_str=task_id),
            "magic_edit": lambda: me.handle_magic_edit_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id),
            "join_clips_orchestrator": lambda: _handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                orchestrator_task_id_str=task_id,
                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
            "edit_video_orchestrator": lambda: _handle_edit_video_orchestrator_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                orchestrator_task_id_str=task_id,
                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
            "join_clips_segment": lambda: handle_join_clips_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id,
                task_queue=context["task_queue"]),
            "join_final_stitch": lambda: handle_join_final_stitch(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id),
            "inpaint_frames": lambda: _handle_inpaint_frames_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id,
                task_queue=context["task_queue"]),
            "create_visualization": lambda: _handle_create_visualization_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                viz_task_id_str=task_id),
            "extract_frame": lambda: handle_extract_frame_task(
                params, context["main_output_dir_base"], task_id
            ),
            "rife_interpolate_images": lambda: handle_rife_interpolate_task(
                params, context["main_output_dir_base"], task_id, task_queue=context["task_queue"]
            ),
            "comfy": lambda: handle_comfy_task(
                task_params_from_db=params,
                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
                task_id=task_id)
        }

        if task_type in handlers:
            # Orchestrator setup
            if task_type in ["travel_orchestrator", "join_clips_orchestrator", "edit_video_orchestrator"]:
                params["task_id"] = task_id
                if "orchestrator_details" in params:
                    params["orchestrator_details"]["orchestrator_task_id"] = task_id
            
            return handlers[task_type]()

        # Default fallthrough to queue
        if context["task_queue"]:
             return TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task(task_type, context)
        
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown task type {task_type} and no queue available")

    @staticmethod
    def _handle_direct_queue_task(task_type: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
        task_id = context["task_id"]
        task_queue = context["task_queue"]
        params = context["task_params_dict"]
        
        try:
            generation_task = db_task_to_generation_task(
                params, task_id, task_type, context["wan2gp_path"], context["debug_mode"]
            )
            
            if task_type == "wan_2_2_t2i":
                generation_task.parameters["video_length"] = 1
            
            if context["colour_match_videos"]:
                generation_task.parameters["colour_match_videos"] = True
            if context["mask_active_frames"]:
                generation_task.parameters["mask_active_frames"] = True
            
            task_queue.submit_task(generation_task)
            
            # Wait for completion
            max_wait_time = 3600
            elapsed = 0
            while elapsed < max_wait_time:
                status = task_queue.get_task_status(task_id)
                if not status: return False, "Task status became None"
                
                if status.status == "completed":
                    return True, status.result_path
                elif status.status == "failed":
                    return False, status.error_message or "Failed without message"
                
                time.sleep(2)
                elapsed += 2
            
            return False, "Timeout"
            
        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
            task_logger.error(f"Queue error: {e}", exc_info=True)
            return False, f"Queue error: {e}"

### Key Custom Patch Details

#### **Uni3C ControlNet Implementation (Committed in 2dede9b7)**
- **Ported from:** Kijai's ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper
- **HuggingFace repo:** `Kijai/WanVideo_comfy`
- **Checkpoint:** `Wan21_Uni3C_controlnet_fp16.safetensors`

#### **Uni3C in any2video.py (291 new lines)**
- `_load_uni3c_guide_video()` — Load + resize guide video, apply frame policies (fit/trim/loop/off)
- `_apply_uni3c_frame_policy()` — Frame count alignment
- `_detect_empty_frames()` — Detect near-black frames for zeroing
- `_map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames()` — Map pixel indices to latent indices (4:1 ratio)
- `_encode_uni3c_guide()` — VAE encode guide, optionally zero empty latent groups, pad channels 16→20
- **11 new parameters** to `generate()` method for Uni3C control

#### **Uni3C in model.py (134 new lines)**
- `_compute_uni3c_states()` — ControlNet forward pass:
  - Device management (main GPU + CPU offload)
  - Shape alignment (trilinear resampling for spatial mismatch)
  - Concatenation of noisy (channels 0-19) + guide (full) latents
  - Dtype handling (fp32 for patch embedding, fp16 for computation)
  - Per-block residual outputs
- Injection points in transformer block loop (~39 lines)
  - Window-based activation (`start_percent` to `end_percent`)
  - Strength scaling of residuals
  - Shape reconciliation at injection
  - Detailed logging on step 0

#### **wgp.py Passthrough (32 lines)**
- 11 Uni3C parameters added to `generate_video()` signature
- Logged at function start (enabled/disabled state)
- Forwarded to `any2video.generate()`

#### **New Presets (6 Lightning variants + 1 img2img)**
All define 2-phase guidance, custom step counts, Lightning high/low guidance scales:
- `wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_*` (3 variants: 2-2-2, 3-3, svi_3_3 steps)
- `wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_svi_endframe.json` (special end-frame variant)
- `wan_2_2_vace_lightning_baseline_*` (2 variants for VACE)
- `z_image_img2img.json` (img2img with denoising_strength)

---

**Summary:** This branch is a focused PR19 that layers Uni3C ControlNet support + Lightning presets on top of the PR18 Wan2GP v10.83 + LTX-2 merge. All changes are committed, worktree is clean. The implementation is port-compatible with Kijai's ComfyUI architecture.

## Research Summary: Upstream Wan2GP generate_video() Integration

Based on my analysis of the upstream code at commit 974af602, here are the key findings:

### 1. **generate_video() Function Signature**
- **Location**: `upstream/main:wgp.py` line 5738
- **Extremely large parameter set** (~100+ parameters) covering:
  - Image/video source parameters: `image_start`, `image_end`, `image_guide`, `image_refs`, `video_source`, `video_guide`
  - Model configuration: `model_type`, `model_mode`, `base_model_type`
  - Generation settings: `sampling_steps`, `guidance_scale`, `seed`, `batch_size`, `num_inference_steps`
  - Prompt variants: `prompt`, `alt_prompt`, `negative_prompt`, `audio_prompt_type`
  - Advanced features: `flow_shift`, `sample_solver`, `perturbation_layers`, `apg_switch`, `cfg_star_switch`, `self_refiner_*`
  - Guidance variants: `guidance_scale`, `guidance2_scale`, `guidance3_scale`, `alt_guidance_scale`, `audio_guidance_scale`
  - Audio: `audio_guide`, `audio_guide2`, `audio_source`, `audio_prompt_type`
  - Video guides: `video_guide`, `video_mask`, `video_guide_outpainting`
  - Masking/denoising: `masking_strength`, `denoising_strength`
  - LoRA handling: `activated_loras`, `loras_multipliers`, `loras_selected`
  - Sliding window: `sliding_window_*` parameters
  - Custom guides and refs: `custom_guide`, `image_refs_relative_size`, `remove_background_images_ref`
  - UI/progress: `set_progress_status` callback

### 2. **Model.generate() Call (Line 6624)**
The actual invocation to `wan_model.generate()` passes:
- **Core inputs**: `input_prompt`, `alt_prompt`, `n_prompt` (negative)
- **Visual inputs**: 
  - `image_start_tensor`, `image_end_tensor` (PIL-converted tensors)
  - `src_video`, `src_video2` (video frames)
  - `src_ref_images`, `src_ref_masks` (reference images)
  - `src_mask`, `src_mask2` (masks)
  - `input_video` (normalized float tensor)
  - `src_faces`, `custom_guide`
- **Strength parameters**: `denoising_strength`, `masking_strength`, `input_video_strength`
- **Generation params**: `frame_num`, `batch_size`, `height`, `width`, `fit_into_canvas`, `shift`, `sample_solver`, `sampling_steps`
- **Guidance params**: `guide_scale`, `guide2_scale`, `guide3_scale`, `switch_threshold`, `switch2_threshold`, `guide_phases`, `model_switch_phase`, `embedded_guidance_scale`
- **LoRAs**: `loras_slists`, `loras_selected`
- **Audio**: `input_waveform`, `input_waveform_sample_rate`, `audio_guide`, `audio_guide2`, `audio_prompt_type`, `audio_proj_split`, `audio_scale`, `audio_context_lens`, `audio_cfg_scale`
- **Advanced**: `perturbation_switch/layers/start/end`, `apg_switch`, `cfg_star_switch`, `cfg_zero_step`, `enable_RIFLEx`, `VAE_tile_size`
- **Model metadata**: `model_filename`, `model_type`, `model_mode`, `video_prompt_type`, `offloadobj`
- **Position/context**: `prefix_frames_count`, `prefix_video`, `original_input_ref_images`, `image_refs_relative_size`, `frames_relative_positions_list`
- **Callbacks**: `callback`, `set_progress_status`
- **Self-refiner**: `self_refiner_setting/plan/f_uncertainty/certain_percentage`
- **Newer additions**: `top_p`, `top_k`, `temperature`, `custom_settings`, `duration_seconds`, `pause_seconds`

### 3. **WanAny2V Class Structure** (models/wan/any2video.py:77)
- Constructor takes `model_def` dict with keys like:
  - `"URLs2"`, `"mocha_mode"`, `"vace_class"`, `"multitalk_class"`, `"standin_class"`, `"lynx_class"`, `"wan_5B_class"`, `"alpha_class"`, `"i2v_class"`, `"i2v_2_2"`
  - `"VAE_URLs"`, `"video_guide_outpainting"`, `"source"`, `"source2"`, `"module_source"`, `"module_source2"`
  - `"config_file"`, `"text_encoder_folder"`, `"ignore_unused_weights"`, `"custom_video_length"`
- Key methods:
  - `encode_reference_images(ref_images, ref_prompt, any_guidance, tile_size, enable_loras)`
  - `vace_encode_frames(frames, ref_images, masks, tile_size, overlapped_latents)`
  - `get_vae_latents(ref_images, device, tile_size)`
  - `generate()` — the main generation method
  - `get_loras_transformer()` — LoRA handling

### 4. **LTX2 Model Integration** (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:759)
- LTX2 `generate()` signature uses:
  - `input_ref_images`, `frames_relative_positions_list` for reference image injection
  - `image_start`, `image_end` for frame boundaries
  - `guiding_images`, `guiding_images_stage2` lists built dynamically by `_append_injected_ref_entries()`
  - Internal mechanism (line 965-998):
    - `_append_injected_ref_entries()` appends tuples: `(image, frame_idx, strength, "lanczos")`
    - Two-stage pipeline maintains separate `images` and `guiding_images` lists
    - `image_start`/`image_end` conditionally added to `guiding_images` or regular `images` based on flags

### 5. **Key Settings Functions**
- `get_default_settings(model_type)` — retrieves model-specific UI defaults
- `set_model_settings(state, model_type, settings)` — propagates settings into state dict
- Used extensively during model initialization and parameter resolution

### 6. **Requirements Changes** (upstream vs HEAD)
**Moved/updated dependencies**:
- `mmgp`: `3.7.4` → `3.7.6` (moved to top-level from bottom section)
- **Added**: `flash-linear-attention==0.4.1`, `munch`, `wetext==0.1.2`
- Suggests optimized attention and text processing improvements

### 7. **Integration Flow Summary**
```
generate_video() processes inputs (image_guide, video_guide, etc.)
  ↓
Validates and prepares tensors (image_start_tensor, src_video, etc.)
  ↓
Calls wan_model.generate() with ~80 parameters + callbacks
  ↓
WanAny2V.generate() orchestrates:
  - VAE encoding of reference images
  - Prompt encoding via T5
  - Potentially CLIP encoding for image refs
  - LoRA application
  - Denoising loop with guidance phases
  ↓
For LTX2-based models: injects guiding_images at specific frame positions
```

### Key Integration Points for Your Codebase:
1. **input_prompt + alt_prompt split** — alt_prompt used when switching guidance
2. **Tensor normalization** — video inputs normalized to float [-1, 1]
3. **frames_relative_positions_list** — drives which reference images inject at which frames
4. **model_def dictionary** — all model behavior conditional on this config
5. **offloadobj callback** — VRAM management during generation
6. **set_progress_status callback** — UI progress updates during inference
7. **model_type/base_model_type distinction** — router for model selection logic

All code is read-only research. No modifications were made.

elif task_type in ("ltx2_multiframe", "ltx2_ic_multiframe"):
    # Download guide images from URLs to local paths
    guide_images_raw = db_task_params.get("guide_images", [])
    if guide_images_raw:
        import tempfile
        import requests
        resolved_guides = []
        for i, entry in enumerate(guide_images_raw):
            img_source = entry.get("image") or entry.get("image_url")
            if img_source and img_source.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
                response = requests.get(img_source, timeout=30)
                response.raise_for_status()
                with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".png", delete=False) as tmp:
                    tmp.write(response.content)
                    local_path = tmp.name
            else:
                local_path = img_source
            resolved_guides.append({
                "image": local_path,
                "frame_idx": entry.get("frame_idx", 0),
                "strength": entry.get("strength", 1.0),
            })
        generation_params["guide_images"] = resolved_guides
        headless_logger.info(
            f"[{task_type.upper()}] Resolved {len(resolved_guides)} guide images",
            task_id=task_id
        )
```

# start_image → image_start (PIL)
_si = kwargs.pop("start_image", None)
if _si and isinstance(_si, str) and "image_start" not in kwargs:
    try:

        _img = _ImageOps.exif_transpose(_img)
        kwargs["image_start"] = _img
        generation_logger.info(f"[LTX2_BRIDGE] Converted start_image path → image_start PIL ({_img.size})")
    except Exception as _e:
        generation_logger.warning(f"[LTX2_BRIDGE] Failed to load start_image '{_si}': {_e}")

# end_image → image_end (PIL)
_ei = kwargs.pop("end_image", None)
if _ei and isinstance(_ei, str) and "image_end" not in kwargs:
    try:
        _img = _PILImage.open(_ei).convert("RGB")
        _img = _ImageOps.exif_transpose(_img)
        kwargs["image_end"] = _img
        generation_logger.info(f"[LTX2_BRIDGE] Converted end_image path → image_end PIL ({_img.size})")

# audio_input → audio_guide
_ai = kwargs.pop("audio_input", None)
if _ai and isinstance(_ai, str) and "audio_guide" not in kwargs:
    kwargs["audio_guide"] = _ai
    generation_logger.info(f"[LTX2_BRIDGE] Mapped audio_input → audio_guide: {_ai}")

# guide_images bridging: convert paths to PIL images
_gi = kwargs.get("guide_images")
if _gi and isinstance(_gi, list):
    bridged = []
    for img_or_path, frame_idx, strength in _gi:
        if isinstance(img_or_path, str):
            try:
                _img = _PILImage.open(img_or_path).convert("RGB")
                _img = _ImageOps.exif_transpose(_img)
                bridged.append((_img, frame_idx, strength))
            else:
                bridged.append((img_or_path, frame_idx, strength))
    kwargs["guide_images"] = bridged
    generation_logger.info(f"[LTX2_BRIDGE] Converted {len(bridged)} guide_images to PIL")

# Auto-detect image_prompt_type for LTX-2 when not explicitly set
base_type_check = (self._get_base_model_type(self.current_model) or "").lower()
if base_type_check.startswith("ltx2") and "image_prompt_type" not in kwargs:
    has_start = kwargs.get("image_start") is not None
    has_end = kwargs.get("image_end") is not None
    has_guides = kwargs.get("guide_images") is not None and len(kwargs.get("guide_images", [])) > 0

    if has_start and has_end:
        kwargs["image_prompt_type"] = "TSE"
    elif has_start:
        kwargs["image_prompt_type"] = "TS"
    elif has_end:
        kwargs["image_prompt_type"] = "TE"
    elif has_guides:
        kwargs["image_prompt_type"] = "T"
    else:
        kwargs["image_prompt_type"] = "T"
    generation_logger.info(f"[LTX2_BRIDGE] Auto-detected image_prompt_type='{kwargs['image_prompt_type']}' (start={has_start}, end={has_end}, guides={has_guides})")

```python
def _apply_model_settings_overrides(self, model_type: str, overrides: Optional[dict] = None) -> dict:
    """Apply settings via upstream get_default_settings/set_model_settings with optional overrides."""
    defaults = self._get_default_settings(model_type)
    merged = dict(defaults or {})
    if overrides:
        merged.update({k: v for k, v in overrides.items() if v is not None})
    self._set_model_settings(self.state, model_type, merged)
    return merged
```

### Key Wiring Observations

1. **Task Types Fully Wired**: `ltx2`, `ltx2_multiframe`, `ltx2_ic_multiframe` are registered in both `WGP_TASK_TYPES` and `DIRECT_QUEUE_TASK_TYPES`, mapping to `ltx2_19B` model.

2. **Uni3C Parameters Complete**: All 8 Uni3C params (`use_uni3c`, `uni3c_guide_video`, `uni3c_strength`, `uni3c_start_percent`, `uni3c_end_percent`, `uni3c_keep_on_gpu`, `uni3c_frame_policy`, `uni3c_zero_empty_frames`, `uni3c_blackout_last_frame`) are in task_conversion.py param_whitelist with **layer-1 logging for early detection**.

3. **guide_images Parameter**: Present in param_whitelist (line 78 task_conversion.py) with **full URL→local-path resolution** for ltx2_multiframe/ltx2_ic_multiframe tasks (lines 250-276).

4. **LTX2_BRIDGE Complete**: orchestrator.py contains comprehensive bridging for:
   - `start_image` → `image_start` (PIL)
   - `end_image` → `image_end` (PIL)
   - `audio_input` → `audio_guide`
   - `guide_images` → PIL images conversion
   - Auto-detection of `image_prompt_type` (TS/TSE/TE/T)

5. **Model Settings**: `get_default_settings` / `set_model_settings` imported from wgp and applied via `_apply_model_settings_overrides()`.

6. **Safety Logic**: LTX-2 has **17-frame minimum** (vs 5 for T2V) enforced in `configure_model_specific_params()`.

7. **Test Coverage**: PR #19 regression tests confirm Uni3C filtering behavior (keep when supported, fail-fast when not) and full end-to-end generation paths.

   111→def get_default_model(task_type: str) -> str:
   112→    """
   113→    Get the default model for a given task type.
   114→
   115→    Args:
   116→        task_type: The task type identifier
   117→

   128→

    76→class TestRealLTX2Travel:
    77→    """Real GPU generation with LTX-2 model and test images."""
    78→
    79→    def test_ltx2_single_segment_start_end(
    80→        self, _chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir, test_images
    81→    ):
    82→        """Generate a short video from CICEK.png → CICEK2.png using LTX-2."""
    83→        orch = _make_orchestrator(_chdir_to_wan2gp, output_dir)
    84→
    85→        print("\n[GPU] Loading LTX-2 19B model...")
    86→        switched = orch.load_model("ltx2_19B")
    87→        assert switched is True
    88→        assert orch._is_ltx2() is True
    89→        print("[GPU] Model loaded.")
    90→
    91→        print(f"[GPU] Generating: {Path(test_images[0]).name} → {Path(test_images[1]).name}")
    92→        result = orch.generate(
    93→            prompt="A neon green sprout slowly transforms into a cyan sprout, bioluminescent glow, smooth cinematic transition",
    94→            resolution="512x320",
    95→            video_length=33,          # short: 33 frames (~2s)
    96→            num_inference_steps=20,   # fewer steps for speed
    97→            guidance_scale=4.0,
    98→            seed=42,
    99→            start_image=test_images[0],
   100→            end_image=test_images[1],
   101→        )
   102→
   103→        assert result is not None, "generate() returned None"
   104→        assert os.path.isfile(result), f"Output not found: {result}"
   105→        size = os.path.getsize(result)
   106→        assert size > 1000, f"Output too small ({size} bytes), likely not a real video"
   107→        print(f"[GPU] Output: {result} ({size / 1024:.1f} KB)")
   108→
   109→
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→# Standalone runner
   112→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   113→if __name__ == "__main__":
   114→    exit_code = pytest.main([__file__, "-v", "-s", "--tb=short"])
   115→    sys.exit(exit_code)
   116→

1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→WanGP Headless Task Queue Manager
     4→
     5→A persistent service that maintains model state and processes generation tasks
     6→via a queue system. This script keeps models loaded in memory and delegates
     7→actual generation to headless_wgp.py while managing the queue, persistence,
     8→and task scheduling.
     9→
    10→Key Features:
    11→- Persistent model state (models stay loaded until switched)
    12→- Task queue with priority support
    13→- Auto model switching and memory management
    14→- Status monitoring and progress tracking
    15→- Uses wgp.py's native queue and state management
    16→- Hot-swappable task processing
    17→
    18→Usage:
    19→    # Start the headless service
    20→    python headless_model_management.py --wan-dir /path/to/WanGP --port 8080
    21→    
    22→    # Submit tasks via API or queue files
    23→    curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/generate \
    24→         -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    25→         -d '{"model": "vace_14B", "prompt": "mystical forest", "video_guide": "input.mp4"}'
    26→"""
    27→
    28→import os
    29→import sys
    30→import time
    31→
    32→import threading
    33→import queue
    34→import argparse
    35→import signal
    36→import logging
    37→from pathlib import Path
    38→from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
    39→from dataclasses import dataclass
    40→from datetime import datetime
    41→from source.core.log import queue_logger
    42→from source.task_handlers.queue.download_ops import switch_model_impl, convert_to_wgp_task_impl
    43→from source.task_handlers.queue.task_processor import (
    44→    worker_loop,
    45→    process_task_impl,
    46→    execute_generation_impl,
    47→    cleanup_memory_after_task,
    48→)
    49→from source.task_handlers.queue.queue_lifecycle import start_queue, stop_queue, submit_task_impl
    50→from source.task_handlers.queue.wgp_init import WgpInitMixin
    51→
    52→
    53→# Add WanGP to path for imports
    54→def setup_wgp_path(wan_dir: str):
    55→    """Setup WanGP path and imports."""
    56→    wan_dir = os.path.abspath(wan_dir)
    57→    if wan_dir not in sys.path:
    58→        sys.path.insert(0, wan_dir)
    59→    return wan_dir
    60→
    61→# Task definitions
    62→@dataclass
    63→class GenerationTask:
    64→    """Represents a single generation task."""
    65→    id: str
    66→    model: str
    67→    prompt: str
    68→    parameters: Dict[str, Any]
    69→    priority: int = 0
    70→    created_at: str = None
    71→    status: str = "pending"  # pending, processing, completed, failed
    72→    result_path: Optional[str] = None
    73→    error_message: Optional[str] = None
    74→    processing_time: Optional[float] = None
    75→    
    76→    def __post_init__(self):
    77→        if self.created_at is None:
    78→            self.created_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
    79→
    80→@dataclass 
    81→class QueueStatus:
    82→    """Current queue status information."""
    83→    pending_tasks: int
    84→    processing_task: Optional[str]
    85→    completed_tasks: int
    86→    failed_tasks: int
    87→    current_model: Optional[str]
    88→    uptime: float
    89→    memory_usage: Dict[str, Any]
    90→
    91→
    92→class HeadlessTaskQueue(WgpInitMixin):
    93→    """
    94→    Main task queue manager that integrates with wgp.py's existing queue system.
    95→
    96→    This class leverages wgp.py's built-in task management and state persistence
    97→    while providing a clean API for headless operation.
    98→
    99→    Mixins:
   100→        WgpInitMixin: CUDA warmup and lazy WanOrchestrator initialization
   101→    """
   102→    
   103→    def __init__(self, wan_dir: str, max_workers: int = 1, debug_mode: bool = False, main_output_dir: Optional[str] = None):
   104→        """
   105→        Initialize the headless task queue.
   106→
   107→        Args:
   108→            wan_dir: Path to WanGP directory
   109→            max_workers: Number of concurrent generation workers (recommend 1 for GPU)
   110→            debug_mode: Enable verbose debug logging (should match worker's --debug flag)
   111→            main_output_dir: Optional path for output directory. If not provided, defaults to
   112→                           'outputs' directory next to wan_dir (preserves backwards compatibility)
   113→        """
   114→        self.wan_dir = setup_wgp_path(wan_dir)
   115→        self.max_workers = max_workers
   116→        self.main_output_dir = main_output_dir
   117→        self.running = False
   118→        self.start_time = time.time()
   119→        self.debug_mode = debug_mode  # Now controlled by caller
   120→        
   121→        # Import wgp after path setup (protect sys.argv to prevent argument conflicts)
   122→        _saved_argv = sys.argv[:]
   123→        sys.argv = ["headless_wgp.py"]
   124→        # Headless stubs to avoid optional UI deps (tkinter/matanyone) during import
   125→        try:
   126→            import types
   127→            # Stub tkinter if not available
   128→            if 'tkinter' not in sys.modules:
   129→                sys.modules['tkinter'] = types.ModuleType('tkinter')
   130→            # Stub preprocessing.matanyone.app with minimal interface
   131→            dummy_pkg = types.ModuleType('preprocessing')
   132→            dummy_matanyone = types.ModuleType('preprocessing.matanyone')
   133→            dummy_app = types.ModuleType('preprocessing.matanyone.app')
   134→            def _noop_handler():
   135→                class _Dummy:
   136→                    def __getattr__(self, _):
   137→                        return None
   138→                return _Dummy()
   139→            dummy_app.get_vmc_event_handler = _noop_handler  # type: ignore
   140→            sys.modules['preprocessing'] = dummy_pkg
   141→            sys.modules['preprocessing.matanyone'] = dummy_matanyone
   142→            sys.modules['preprocessing.matanyone.app'] = dummy_app
   143→        except (TypeError, AttributeError, ImportError) as e:
   144→            logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue').debug(f"Failed to set up headless UI stubs: {e}")
   145→        # Don't import wgp during initialization to avoid CUDA/argparse conflicts
   146→        # wgp will be imported lazily when needed (e.g., in _apply_sampler_cfg_preset)
   147→        # This allows the queue to initialize even if CUDA isn't ready yet
   148→        self.wgp = None
   149→        
   150→        # Restore sys.argv immediately (no wgp import, so no need for protection)
   151→        try:
   152→            sys.argv = _saved_argv
   153→        except (TypeError, AttributeError) as e:
   154→            logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue').debug(f"Failed to restore sys.argv after init: {e}")
   155→        
   156→        # Defer orchestrator initialization to avoid CUDA init during queue setup
   157→        # Orchestrator imports wgp, which triggers deep imports that call torch.cuda
   158→        # We'll initialize it lazily when first needed
   159→        self.orchestrator = None
   160→        self._orchestrator_init_attempted = False
   161→        logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue').info(f"HeadlessTaskQueue created (orchestrator will initialize on first use)")
   162→        
   163→        # Task management
   164→        self.task_queue = queue.PriorityQueue()
   165→        self.task_history: Dict[str, GenerationTask] = {}
   166→        self.current_task: Optional[GenerationTask] = None
   167→        self.current_model: Optional[str] = None
   168→        
   169→        # Threading
   170→        self.worker_threads: List[threading.Thread] = []
   171→        self.queue_lock = threading.RLock()
   172→        self.shutdown_event = threading.Event()
   173→        
   174→        # Statistics
   175→        self.stats = {
   176→            "tasks_submitted": 0,
   177→            "tasks_completed": 0,
   178→            "tasks_failed": 0,
   179→            "model_switches": 0,
   180→            "total_generation_time": 0.0
   181→        }
   182→        
   183→        # Setup logging
   184→        self._setup_logging()
   185→        
   186→        # Initialize wgp state (reuse existing state management)
   187→        self._init_wgp_integration()
   188→        
   189→        self.logger.info(f"HeadlessTaskQueue initialized with WanGP at {wan_dir}")
   190→    
   191→    def _setup_logging(self):
   192→        """Setup structured logging that goes to Supabase via the log interceptor."""
   193→        # Keep Python's basic logging for local file backup
   194→        logging.basicConfig(
   195→            level=logging.INFO,
   196→            format='%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s',
   197→            handlers=[
   198→                logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout),
   199→                logging.FileHandler('headless.log')
   200→            ]
   201→        )
   202→        self._file_logger = logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue')
   203→        
   204→        # Use queue_logger (ComponentLogger) as main logger - this goes to Supabase
   205→        # ComponentLogger has compatible interface: .info(), .error(), .warning(), .debug()
   206→        self.logger = queue_logger
   207→    
   208→    def get_task_status(self, task_id: str) -> Optional[GenerationTask]:
   209→        """Get status of a specific task."""
   210→        return self.task_history.get(task_id)
   211→
   212→    def wait_for_completion(self, task_id: str, timeout: float = 300.0) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   213→        """
   214→        Wait for a task to complete and return the result.
   215→
   216→        Args:
   217→            task_id: ID of the task to wait for
   218→            timeout: Maximum time to wait in seconds
   219→
   220→        Returns:
   221→            Dictionary with 'success', 'output_path', and optional 'error' keys
   222→        """
   223→        start_time = time.time()
   224→
   225→        while (time.time() - start_time) < timeout:
   226→            task_status = self.get_task_status(task_id)
   227→
   228→            if task_status is None:
   229→                return {
   230→                    "success": False,
   231→                    "error": f"Task {task_id} not found in queue"
   232→                }
   233→
   234→            if task_status.status == "completed":
   235→                return {
   236→                    "success": True,
   237→                    "output_path": task_status.result_path
   238→                }
   239→            elif task_status.status == "failed":
   240→                return {
   241→                    "success": False,
   242→                    "error": task_status.error_message or "Task failed with unknown error"
   243→                }
   244→
   245→            # Task is still pending or processing, wait a bit
   246→            time.sleep(1.0)
   247→
   248→        # Timeout reached
   249→        return {
   250→            "success": False,
   251→            "error": f"Task {task_id} did not complete within {timeout} seconds"
   252→        }
   253→
   254→    def get_queue_status(self) -> QueueStatus:
   255→        """Get current queue status."""
   256→        with self.queue_lock:
   257→            return QueueStatus(
   258→                pending_tasks=self.task_queue.qsize(),
   259→                processing_task=self.current_task.id if self.current_task else None,
   260→                completed_tasks=self.stats["tasks_completed"],
   261→                failed_tasks=self.stats["tasks_failed"],
   262→                current_model=self.current_model,
   263→                uptime=time.time() - self.start_time,
   264→                memory_usage=self._get_memory_usage()
   265→            )
   266→
   267→    def _get_memory_usage(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   268→        """Get current memory usage statistics. Returns stub data for now."""
   269→        return {
   270→            "gpu_memory_used": 0,
   271→            "gpu_memory_total": 0,
   272→            "system_memory_used": 0,
   273→            "model_memory_usage": 0
   274→        }
   275→
   276→    def _cleanup_memory_after_task(self, task_id: str):
   277→        """Clean up memory after task completion WITHOUT unloading models."""
   278→        return cleanup_memory_after_task(self, task_id)
   279→    
   280→    def start(self, preload_model: Optional[str] = None):
   281→        """Start the task queue processing service."""
   282→        return start_queue(self, preload_model=preload_model)
   283→    
   284→    def stop(self, timeout: float = 30.0):
   285→        """Stop the task queue processing service."""
   286→        return stop_queue(self, timeout=timeout)
   287→    
   288→    def submit_task(self, task: GenerationTask) -> str:
   289→        """Submit a new generation task to the queue."""
   290→        return submit_task_impl(self, task)
   291→    
   292→    def _worker_loop(self):
   293→        """Main worker loop for processing tasks."""
   294→        return worker_loop(self)

   296→    def _process_task(self, task: GenerationTask, worker_name: str):
   297→        """Process a single generation task."""

   301→        """Ensure the correct model is loaded using wgp.py's model management."""

   308→    def _model_supports_vace(self, model_key: str) -> bool:
   309→        """
   310→        Check if a model supports VACE features (video guides, masks, etc.).
   311→        """
   312→        # Ensure orchestrator is initialized before checking model support
   313→        self._ensure_orchestrator()
   314→        
   315→        try:
   316→            # Use orchestrator's VACE detection with model key
   317→            if hasattr(self.orchestrator, 'is_model_vace'):
   318→                return self.orchestrator.is_model_vace(model_key)
   319→            elif hasattr(self.orchestrator, '_is_vace'):
   320→                # Fallback: load model and check (less efficient)
   321→                current_model = self.current_model
   322→                if current_model != model_key:
   323→                    # Would need to load model to check - use name-based detection as fallback
   324→                    return "vace" in model_key.lower()
   325→                return self.orchestrator._is_vace()
   326→            else:
   327→                # Ultimate fallback: name-based detection
   328→                return "vace" in model_key.lower()
   329→        except (AttributeError, ValueError, TypeError) as e:
   330→            self.logger.warning(f"Could not determine VACE support for model '{model_key}': {e}")
   331→            return "vace" in model_key.lower()
   332→    
   333→    def _is_single_image_task(self, task: GenerationTask) -> bool:
   334→        """
   335→        Check if this is a single image task that should be converted from video to PNG.
   336→        """
   337→        # Check if video_length is 1 (single frame) and this looks like an image task
   338→        video_length = task.parameters.get("video_length", 0)
   339→        return video_length == 1
   340→    
   341→    def _convert_single_frame_video_to_png(self, task: GenerationTask, video_path: str, worker_name: str) -> str:
   342→        """
   343→        Convert a single-frame video to PNG format for single image tasks.
   344→        
   345→        This restores the functionality that was in the original single_image.py handler
   346→        where single-frame videos were converted to PNG files.
   347→        """
   348→        try:
   349→            import cv2
   350→            
   351→            video_path_obj = Path(video_path)
   352→            if not video_path_obj.exists():
   353→                self.logger.error(f"Video file does not exist for PNG conversion: {video_path}")
   354→                return video_path  # Return original path if conversion fails
   355→            
   356→            # Create PNG output path with sanitized filename to prevent upload issues
   357→            original_filename = video_path_obj.stem
   358→            
   359→            # Sanitize the filename for storage compatibility
   360→            try:
   361→                from source.utils import sanitize_filename_for_storage
   362→
   363→                sanitized_filename = sanitize_filename_for_storage(original_filename)
   364→                if not sanitized_filename:
   365→                    sanitized_filename = "generated_image"
   366→
   367→            except ImportError:
   368→                # Fallback sanitization if import fails
   369→                import re
   370→                sanitized_filename = re.sub(r'[§®©™@·º½¾¿¡~\x00-\x1F\x7F-\x9F<>:"/\\|?*,]', '', original_filename)
   371→                sanitized_filename = re.sub(r'\s+', '_', sanitized_filename.strip())
   372→                if not sanitized_filename:
   373→                    sanitized_filename = "generated_image"
   374→            
   375→            # Create PNG path with sanitized filename
   376→            png_path = video_path_obj.parent / f"{sanitized_filename}.png"
   377→            
   378→            # Log sanitization if filename changed
   379→            if sanitized_filename != original_filename:
   380→                self.logger.info(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Sanitized filename '{original_filename}' -> '{sanitized_filename}'")
   381→            
   382→            self.logger.info(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Converting {video_path_obj.name} to {png_path.name}")
   383→            
   384→            # Extract the first frame using OpenCV
   385→            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path_obj))
   386→            try:
   387→                if cap.isOpened():
   388→                    ret, frame = cap.read()
   389→                    if ret:
   390→                        # Save the frame as PNG
   391→                        success = cv2.imwrite(str(png_path), frame)
   392→                        if success and png_path.exists():
   393→                            self.logger.info(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Successfully saved PNG to {png_path}")
   394→                            
   395→                            # Clean up the original video file
   396→                            try:
   397→                                video_path_obj.unlink()
   398→                                self.logger.info(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Removed original video file")
   399→                            except OSError as e_cleanup:
   400→                                self.logger.warning(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Could not remove original video: {e_cleanup}")
   401→                            
   402→                            return str(png_path)
   403→                        else:
   404→                            self.logger.error(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Failed to save PNG to {png_path}")
   405→                    else:
   406→                        self.logger.error(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Failed to read frame from video")
   407→                else:
   408→                    self.logger.error(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Failed to open video file")
   409→            finally:
   410→                cap.release()
   411→                
   412→        except ImportError:
   413→            self.logger.warning(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: OpenCV not available, keeping video format")
   414→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   415→            self.logger.error(f"[PNG_CONVERSION] Task {task.id}: Error during conversion: {e}")
   416→        
   417→        # Return original video path if conversion failed
   418→        return video_path
   419→    
   420→    def _monitor_loop(self):
   421→        """Background monitoring and maintenance loop."""
   422→        from source.task_handlers.queue.task_processor import _monitor_loop as _monitor_loop_impl
   423→        return _monitor_loop_impl(self)
   424→    
   425→    def _convert_to_wgp_task(self, task: GenerationTask) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   426→        """Convert task to WGP parameters using typed TaskConfig."""
   427→        return convert_to_wgp_task_impl(self, task)
   428→    
   429→    def _apply_sampler_cfg_preset(self, model_key: str, sample_solver: str, wgp_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   430→        """Apply sampler-specific CFG and flow_shift settings from model configuration."""
   431→        try:
   432→            # Import WGP to get model definition
   433→            # Protect sys.argv in case wgp hasn't been imported yet
   434→            _saved_argv = sys.argv[:]
   435→            try:
   436→                sys.argv = ["headless_model_management.py"]
   437→                import wgp
   438→            finally:
   439→                sys.argv = _saved_argv
   440→
   441→            model_def = wgp.get_model_def(model_key)
   442→            
   443→            # Check if model has sampler-specific presets
   444→            sampler_presets = model_def.get("sampler_cfg_presets", {})
   445→            if sample_solver in sampler_presets:
   446→                preset = sampler_presets[sample_solver]
   447→                
   448→                # Apply preset settings, but allow task parameters to override
   449→                applied_params = {}
   450→                for param, value in preset.items():
   451→                    if param not in wgp_params:  # Only apply if not explicitly set in task
   452→                        wgp_params[param] = value
   453→                        applied_params[param] = value
   454→                        
   455→                self.logger.info(f"Applied sampler '{sample_solver}' CFG preset: {applied_params}")
   456→            else:
   457→                self.logger.debug(f"No CFG preset found for sampler '{sample_solver}' in model '{model_key}'")
   458→                
   459→        except (ValueError, KeyError, AttributeError, TypeError, ImportError) as e:
   460→            self.logger.warning(f"Failed to apply sampler CFG preset: {e}")
   461→    
   462→
   463→def create_sample_task(task_id: str, model: str, prompt: str, **params) -> GenerationTask:
   464→    """Helper to create sample tasks for testing."""
   465→    return GenerationTask(
   466→        id=task_id,
   467→        model=model,
   468→        prompt=prompt,
   469→        parameters=params
   470→    )
   471→
   472→
   473→def main():
   474→    """Main entry point for the headless service."""
   475→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="WanGP Headless Task Queue")
   476→    parser.add_argument("--wan-dir", required=True, help="Path to WanGP directory")
   477→    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=1, help="Number of worker threads")
   478→    parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Enable debug logging")
   479→
   480→    args = parser.parse_args()
   481→
   482→    if args.debug:
   483→        logging.getLogger().setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
   484→
   485→    # Initialize queue
   486→    task_queue = HeadlessTaskQueue(args.wan_dir, max_workers=args.workers)
   487→
   488→    # Setup signal handlers
   489→    def signal_handler(signum, frame):
   490→        queue_logger.essential("Received shutdown signal, stopping...")
   491→        task_queue.stop()
   492→        sys.exit(0)
   493→
   494→    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, signal_handler)
   495→    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal_handler)
   496→
   497→    try:
   498→        # Start services
   499→        task_queue.start()
   500→
   501→        queue_logger.essential(f"Headless queue started")
   502→        queue_logger.essential(f"WanGP directory: {args.wan_dir}")
   503→        queue_logger.essential(f"Workers: {args.workers}")
   504→        queue_logger.essential("Press Ctrl+C to stop...")
   505→
   506→        # Example: Submit some test tasks
   507→        if args.debug:
   508→            queue_logger.debug("Submitting test tasks...")
   509→
   510→            # Test T2V task
   511→            t2v_task = create_sample_task(
   512→                "test-t2v-1",
   513→                "t2v",
   514→                "a mystical forest with glowing trees",
   515→                resolution="1280x720",
   516→                video_length=49,
   517→                seed=42
   518→            )
   519→            task_queue.submit_task(t2v_task)
   520→
   521→        # Keep running until shutdown
   522→        while task_queue.running:
   523→            time.sleep(1.0)
   524→
   525→            # Print periodic status
   526→            if args.debug:
   527→                status = task_queue.get_queue_status()
   528→                queue_logger.debug(f"Queue: {status.pending_tasks} pending, "
   529→                      f"{status.completed_tasks} completed, "
   530→                      f"{status.failed_tasks} failed")
   531→
   532→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   533→        queue_logger.essential("Shutdown requested by user")
   534→    except Exception as e:
   535→        queue_logger.error(f"Fatal error: {e}")
   536→        raise
   537→    finally:
   538→        task_queue.stop()
   539→
   540→
   541→if __name__ == "__main__":
   542→    main()
   543→

    45→def process_task_impl(queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue", task: "GenerationTask", worker_name: str):
    46→    """
    47→    Process a single generation task.
    48→
    49→    This is where we delegate to headless_wgp.py while managing

    52→    # Ensure logs emitted during this generation are attributed to this task.
    53→    # This runs inside the GenerationWorker thread, which is where wgp/headless_wgp runs.
    54→    try:

    59→        logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue').debug(f"Failed to set task logging context for {task.id}: {e}")
    60→
    61→    with queue.queue_lock:
    62→        queue.current_task = task
    63→        task.status = "processing"

    65→    queue.logger.info(f"[TASK_PROCESSING] {worker_name} processing task {task.id} (model: {task.model})")
    66→    start_time = time.time()
    67→
    68→    try:
    69→        # 1. Ensure correct model is loaded (orchestrator checks WGP's ground truth)
    70→        queue.logger.debug(f"[TASK_PROCESSING] Task {task.id}: Phase 1 - Switching to model {task.model}")
    71→        switch_start = time.time()
    72→        queue._switch_model(task.model, worker_name)
    73→        switch_elapsed = time.time() - switch_start
    74→        queue.logger.debug(f"[TASK_PROCESSING] Task {task.id}: Phase 1 complete - Model switch took {switch_elapsed:.2f}s")
    75→
    76→        # 2. Reset billing start time now that model is loaded
    77→        # This ensures users aren't charged for model loading time

    79→        try:

    83→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_billing:

    86→
    87→        # 3. Delegate actual generation to orchestrator

    94→
