```
   227→            queue.stats["tasks_failed"] += 1
   228→
   229→        queue.logger.critical(f"[TASK_ERROR] Task {task.id} hit UNEXPECTED exception after {processing_time:.1f}s: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
   230→        queue.logger.critical(f"[TASK_ERROR] Full traceback:\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   231→
   232→    finally:
   233→        with queue.queue_lock:
   234→            queue.current_task = None
   235→        try:
   236→            from source.core.log import set_current_task_context  # local import to avoid cycles
   237→            set_current_task_context(None)
   238→        except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError) as e:
   239→            # Swallowing is intentional: logging context is optional and failures are debug-only
   240→            logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue').debug(f"Failed to clear task logging context: {e}")
   241→
   242→
   243→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   244→# Generation execution
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→
   247→def execute_generation_impl(queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue", task: "GenerationTask", worker_name: str) -> str:
   248→    """
   249→    Execute the actual generation using headless_wgp.py.
   250→
   251→    This delegates to the orchestrator while providing progress tracking
   252→    and integration with our queue system. Enhanced to support video guides,
   253→    masks, image references, and other advanced features.
   254→    """
   255→    # Ensure orchestrator is initialized before generation
   256→    queue._ensure_orchestrator()
   257→
   258→    queue.logger.info(f"{worker_name} executing generation for task {task.id} (model: {task.model})")
   259→
   260→    # Convert task parameters to WanOrchestrator format
   261→    wgp_params = queue._convert_to_wgp_task(task)
   262→
   263→    # Remove model and prompt from params since they're passed separately to avoid duplication
   264→    generation_params = {k: v for k, v in wgp_params.items() if k not in ("model", "prompt")}
   265→
   266→    # DEBUG: Log all parameter keys to verify _parsed_phase_config is present
   267→    queue.logger.info(f"[PHASE_CONFIG_DEBUG] Task {task.id}: generation_params keys: {list(generation_params.keys())}")
   268→
   269→    # CRITICAL: Apply phase_config patches NOW in the worker thread where wgp is imported
   270→    # Acquire lock to prevent concurrent tasks from corrupting shared wgp globals
   271→    queue.logger.info(f"[LOCK_ACQUIRE] Task {task.id} attempting to acquire _wgp_patch_lock...")
   272→    lock_acquire_start = time.time()
   273→    with _wgp_patch_lock:
   274→        lock_acquired_elapsed = time.time() - lock_acquire_start
   275→        if lock_acquired_elapsed > 0.1:
   276→            queue.logger.warning(f"[LOCK_ACQUIRE] Task {task.id} waited {lock_acquired_elapsed:.3f}s for _wgp_patch_lock (possible contention)")
   277→        else:
   278→            queue.logger.debug(f"[LOCK_ACQUIRE] Task {task.id} acquired _wgp_patch_lock in {lock_acquired_elapsed:.3f}s")
   279→        try:
   280→            return _execute_generation_with_patches(queue, task, worker_name, generation_params)
   281→        finally:
   282→            queue.logger.debug(f"[LOCK_RELEASE] Task {task.id} releasing _wgp_patch_lock")
   283→
   284→
   285→def _execute_generation_with_patches(
   286→    queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue",
   287→    task: "GenerationTask",
   288→    worker_name: str,
   289→    generation_params: dict,
   290→):
   291→    """Run generation with wgp global patching, under _wgp_patch_lock."""
   292→    # Store patch info for cleanup in finally block
   293→    _patch_applied = False
   294→    _parsed_phase_config_for_restore = None
   295→    _model_name_for_restore = None
   296→
   297→    if "_parsed_phase_config" in generation_params and "_phase_config_model_name" in generation_params:
   298→        parsed_phase_config = generation_params.pop("_parsed_phase_config")
   299→        model_name = generation_params.pop("_phase_config_model_name")
   300→
   301→        # Save for restoration
   302→        _parsed_phase_config_for_restore = parsed_phase_config
   303→        _model_name_for_restore = model_name
   304→
   305→        queue.logger.info(f"[PHASE_CONFIG] Applying model patch in GenerationWorker for '{model_name}'")
   306→
   307→        from source.core.params.phase_config import apply_phase_config_patch
   308→        apply_phase_config_patch(parsed_phase_config, model_name, task.id)
   309→        _patch_applied = True
   310→
   311→    # Handle svi2pro: This is a model_def property, not a generate() parameter
   312→    # We need to patch it into BOTH wgp.models_def AND wan_model.model_def
   313→    # because the model captures model_def at load time, not at generation time
   314→    _svi2pro_original = None
   315→    _svi2pro_patched = False
   316→    _wan_model_patched = False
   317→    if generation_params.get("svi2pro"):
   318→        try:
   319→            import wgp
   320→            model_key = task.model
   321→
   322→            # Patch 1: wgp.models_def (for any new model loads)
   323→            if model_key in wgp.models_def:
   324→                _svi2pro_original = wgp.models_def[model_key].get("svi2pro")
   325→                wgp.models_def[model_key]["svi2pro"] = True
   326→                _svi2pro_patched = True
   327→                queue.logger.info(f"[SVI2PRO] Patched wgp.models_def['{model_key}']['svi2pro'] = True (was: {_svi2pro_original})", task_id=task.id)
   328→
   329→            # Patch 2: wan_model.model_def DIRECTLY (the actual object used during generation)
   330→            # This is critical because the model was loaded BEFORE we patched models_def
   331→            if hasattr(wgp, 'wan_model') and wgp.wan_model is not None:
   332→                if hasattr(wgp.wan_model, 'model_def') and wgp.wan_model.model_def is not None:
   333→                    # Diagnostic: check if they're the same object
   334→                    models_def_obj = wgp.models_def.get(model_key)
   335→                    wan_model_def_obj = wgp.wan_model.model_def
   336→                    same_object = models_def_obj is wan_model_def_obj
   337→                    queue.logger.info(f"[SVI2PRO_DIAG] models_def['{model_key}'] id={id(models_def_obj)}, wan_model.model_def id={id(wan_model_def_obj)}, same_object={same_object}", task_id=task.id)
   338→
   339→                    # Patch svi2pro
   340→                    wgp.wan_model.model_def["svi2pro"] = True
   341→
   342→                    # CRITICAL: Also patch sliding_window=True - required for video continuation
   343→                    # Without this, reuse_frames=0 and video_source context is ignored
   344→                    _sliding_window_original = wgp.wan_model.model_def.get("sliding_window")
   345→                    wgp.wan_model.model_def["sliding_window"] = True
   346→
   347→                    # CRITICAL: Patch sliding_window_defaults to bypass WGP's latent alignment formula
   348→                    # Without this, sliding_window_overlap=4 becomes 1 via: (4-1)//4*4+1 = 1
   349→                    # The original SVI model has overlap_default=4, which makes the formula skip
   350→                    wgp.wan_model.model_def["sliding_window_defaults"] = {"overlap_default": 4}
   351→
   352→                    # CRITICAL: In the kijai-style SVI+end-frame pixel concatenation path, the middle frames
   353→                    # are initialized as zeros before VAE encode (matching kijai and original Wan2GP).
   354→                    # Use zeros for empty frames (standard approach, matching kijai)
   355→                    wgp.wan_model.model_def["svi_empty_frames_mode"] = "zeros"
   356→
   357→                    # Also patch wgp.models_def for consistency (this is what test_any_sliding_window reads!)
   358→                    if model_key in wgp.models_def:
   359→                        _sw_before = wgp.models_def[model_key].get("sliding_window", "NOT_SET")
   360→                        wgp.models_def[model_key]["sliding_window"] = True
   361→                        wgp.models_def[model_key]["sliding_window_defaults"] = {"overlap_default": 4}
   362→                        wgp.models_def[model_key]["svi_empty_frames_mode"] = "zeros"
   363→                        queue.logger.info(f"[SVI2PRO] Patched wgp.models_def['{model_key}']['sliding_window'] = True (was: {_sw_before})", task_id=task.id)
   364→
   365→                    _wan_model_patched = True
   366→
   367→                    # Verify the patches took effect
   368→                    verify_svi2pro = wgp.wan_model.model_def.get("svi2pro")
   369→                    verify_sliding = wgp.wan_model.model_def.get("sliding_window")
   370→                    verify_defaults = wgp.wan_model.model_def.get("sliding_window_defaults")
   371→                    queue.logger.info(f"[SVI2PRO] Patched wan_model.model_def: svi2pro={verify_svi2pro}, sliding_window={verify_sliding}, sliding_window_defaults={verify_defaults} (was: {_sliding_window_original})", task_id=task.id)
   372→                else:
   373→                    queue.logger.warning(f"[SVI2PRO] wan_model exists but has no model_def", task_id=task.id)
   374→            else:
   375→                queue.logger.warning(f"[SVI2PRO] wgp.wan_model not found - model may not be loaded yet", task_id=task.id)
   376→
   377→        except (RuntimeError, AttributeError, ImportError, KeyError) as e:
   378→            queue.logger.warning(f"[SVI2PRO] Failed to patch svi2pro: {e}", task_id=task.id)
   379→        # Remove from generation_params since it's not a generate() parameter
   380→        generation_params.pop("svi2pro", None)
   381→
   382→    # Log generation parameters for debugging
   383→    queue_logger.debug(f"[GENERATION_DEBUG] Task {task.id}: Generation parameters:", task_id=task.id)
   384→    for key, value in generation_params.items():
   385→        if key in ["video_guide", "video_mask", "image_refs"]:
   386→            queue_logger.debug(f"[GENERATION_DEBUG]   {key}: {value}", task_id=task.id)
   387→        elif key in ["video_length", "resolution", "num_inference_steps"]:
   388→            queue_logger.debug(f"[GENERATION_DEBUG]   {key}: {value}", task_id=task.id)
   389→
   390→    # Determine generation type and delegate - wrap in try/finally for patch restoration
   391→    import signal
   392→    generation_start_time = time.time()
   393→    GENERATION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 1200  # 20 minutes - longer than task timeout but prevents indefinite hangs
   394→
   395→    def timeout_handler(signum, frame):
   396→        """Handler for SIGALRM when generation takes too long"""
   397→        elapsed = time.time() - generation_start_time
   398→        queue.logger.error(
   399→            f"[GENERATION_TIMEOUT] Task {task.id} exceeded {GENERATION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s timeout after {elapsed:.1f}s. "
   400→            f"Generation is likely stuck in WGP/CUDA. This usually indicates a GPU synchronization deadlock.",
   401→            task_id=task.id
   402→        )
   403→        raise RuntimeError(f"Generation timeout after {elapsed:.1f}s")
   404→
   405→    try:
   406→        # Set timeout alarm (will raise SIGALRM)
   407→        try:
   408→            signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, timeout_handler)
   409→            signal.alarm(GENERATION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
   410→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Set {GENERATION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s timeout alarm")
   411→        except (ValueError, OSError) as e:
   412→            # Signal not available on all platforms (e.g., Windows), silently continue
   413→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Timeout not available on this platform: {e}")
   414→
   415→        # Check if model supports VACE features
   416→        queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Checking if model supports VACE...")
   417→        model_supports_vace = queue._model_supports_vace(task.model)
   418→        queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: model_supports_vace={model_supports_vace}")
   419→
   420→        if model_supports_vace:
   421→            queue.logger.info(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Using VACE generation path for model {task.model}")
   422→
   423→            # CRITICAL: VACE models require a video_guide parameter
   424→            if "video_guide" in generation_params and generation_params["video_guide"]:
   425→                queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Video guide provided: {generation_params['video_guide']}")
   426→            else:
   427→                error_msg = f"VACE model '{task.model}' requires a video_guide parameter but none was provided. VACE models cannot perform pure text-to-video generation."
   428→                queue.logger.error(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: {error_msg}")
   429→                raise ValueError(error_msg)
   430→
   431→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Calling orchestrator.generate_vace()")
   432→            gen_call_start = time.time()
   433→            result = queue.orchestrator.generate_vace(
   434→                prompt=task.prompt,
   435→                model_type=task.model,  # Pass model type for parameter resolution
   436→                **generation_params
   437→            )
   438→            gen_call_elapsed = time.time() - gen_call_start
   439→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: generate_vace() returned after {gen_call_elapsed:.2f}s: {result}")
   440→        elif queue.orchestrator._is_flux():
   441→            queue.logger.info(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Using Flux generation path for model {task.model}")
   442→
   443→            # For Flux, map video_length to num_images
   444→            if "video_length" in generation_params:
   445→                generation_params["num_images"] = generation_params.pop("video_length")
   446→                queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Mapped video_length to num_images")
   447→
   448→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Calling orchestrator.generate_flux()")
   449→            gen_call_start = time.time()
   450→            result = queue.orchestrator.generate_flux(
   451→                prompt=task.prompt,
   452→                model_type=task.model,  # Pass model type for parameter resolution
   453→                **generation_params
   454→            )
   455→            gen_call_elapsed = time.time() - gen_call_start
   456→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: generate_flux() returned after {gen_call_elapsed:.2f}s: {result}")
   457→        else:
   458→            queue.logger.info(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Using T2V generation path for model {task.model}")
   459→
   460→            # T2V or other models - pass model_type for proper parameter resolution
   461→            # Note: WGP stdout is captured to svi_debug.txt file instead of logger
   462→            # to avoid recursion issues
   463→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Calling orchestrator.generate_t2v()")
   464→            gen_call_start = time.time()
   465→            result = queue.orchestrator.generate_t2v(
   466→                prompt=task.prompt,
   467→                model_type=task.model,  # CRITICAL: Pass model type for parameter resolution
   468→                **generation_params
   469→            )
   470→            gen_call_elapsed = time.time() - gen_call_start
   471→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: generate_t2v() returned after {gen_call_elapsed:.2f}s: {result}")
   472→
   473→        queue.logger.info(f"{worker_name} generation completed for task {task.id}: {result}")
   474→
   475→        # Post-process single frame videos to PNG for single_image tasks
   476→        # BUT: Skip PNG conversion for travel segments (they must remain as videos for stitching)
   477→        is_travel_segment = task.parameters.get("_source_task_type") == "travel_segment"
   478→        if queue._is_single_image_task(task) and not is_travel_segment:
   479→            png_result = queue._convert_single_frame_video_to_png(task, result, worker_name)
   480→            if png_result:
   481→                queue.logger.info(f"{worker_name} converted single frame video to PNG: {png_result}")
   482→                return png_result
   483→
   484→        queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Total generation execution completed successfully")
   485→        return result
   486→
   487→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   488→        queue.logger.error(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id} generation FAILED: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
   489→        queue.logger.error(f"[GENERATION] Traceback:\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   490→        raise
   491→    except BaseException as e:
   492→        queue.logger.critical(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id} generation hit UNEXPECTED exception: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
   493→        queue.logger.critical(f"[GENERATION] Traceback:\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   494→        raise
   495→    finally:
   496→        queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Entering finally block for cleanup")
   497→
   498→        # CRITICAL: Restore model patches to prevent contamination across tasks
   499→        if _patch_applied and _parsed_phase_config_for_restore and _model_name_for_restore:
   500→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Restoring phase config patches for model '{_model_name_for_restore}'")
   501→            try:
   502→                from source.core.params.phase_config import restore_model_patches
   503→                restore_start = time.time()
   504→                restore_model_patches(
   505→                    _parsed_phase_config_for_restore,
   506→                    _model_name_for_restore,
   507→                    task.id
   508→                )
   509→                restore_elapsed = time.time() - restore_start
   510→                queue.logger.info(f"[PHASE_CONFIG] Restored original model definition for '{_model_name_for_restore}' after task {task.id} (took {restore_elapsed:.3f}s)")
   511→            except (RuntimeError, ImportError, OSError) as restore_error:
   512→                queue.logger.warning(f"[PHASE_CONFIG] Failed to restore model patches for task {task.id}: {restore_error}")
   513→
   514→        # Restore svi2pro and sliding_window if we patched them
   515→        if _svi2pro_patched or _wan_model_patched:
   516→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Restoring svi2pro/sliding_window patches")
   517→            try:
   518→                import wgp
   519→                model_key = task.model
   520→
   521→                # Restore wgp.models_def
   522→                if _svi2pro_patched and model_key in wgp.models_def:
   523→                    if _svi2pro_original is None:
   524→                        wgp.models_def[model_key].pop("svi2pro", None)
   525→                    else:
   526→                        wgp.models_def[model_key]["svi2pro"] = _svi2pro_original
   527→                    # Also restore sliding_window
   528→                    wgp.models_def[model_key].pop("sliding_window", None)
   529→                    queue.logger.info(f"[SVI2PRO] Restored wgp.models_def['{model_key}']['svi2pro'] to {_svi2pro_original}", task_id=task.id)
   530→
   531→                # Restore wan_model.model_def
   532→                if _wan_model_patched and hasattr(wgp, 'wan_model') and wgp.wan_model is not None:
   533→                    if hasattr(wgp.wan_model, 'model_def') and wgp.wan_model.model_def is not None:
   534→                        if _svi2pro_original is None:
   535→                            wgp.wan_model.model_def.pop("svi2pro", None)
   536→                        else:
   537→                            wgp.wan_model.model_def["svi2pro"] = _svi2pro_original
   538→                        # Also restore sliding_window
   539→                        wgp.wan_model.model_def.pop("sliding_window", None)
   540→                        queue.logger.info(f"[SVI2PRO] Restored wan_model.model_def: svi2pro={_svi2pro_original}, sliding_window=removed", task_id=task.id)
   541→
   542→            except (RuntimeError, ImportError, AttributeError, KeyError) as restore_error:
   543→                queue.logger.warning(f"[SVI2PRO] Failed to restore svi2pro for task {task.id}: {restore_error}")
   544→
   545→        # Cancel the generation timeout alarm (if set and still active)
   546→        queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Canceling timeout alarm")
   547→        try:
   548→            signal.alarm(0)
   549→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Timeout alarm canceled")
   550→        except (ValueError, NameError) as e:
   551→            # Signal not available or not set, silently continue
   552→            queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Could not cancel timeout alarm: {e}")
   553→
   554→        queue.logger.debug(f"[GENERATION] Task {task.id}: Finally block cleanup complete")
   555→
   556→
   557→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   558→# Worker loop
   559→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   560→
   561→def worker_loop(queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue"):
   562→    """Main worker loop for processing tasks."""
   563→    import threading
   564→    worker_name = threading.current_thread().name
   565→    queue.logger.info(f"{worker_name} started")
   566→
   567→    iteration_count = 0
   568→    while queue.running and not queue.shutdown_event.is_set():
   569→        iteration_count += 1
   570→        try:
   571→            # Log queue status (every 10 iterations to avoid log spam)
   572→            if iteration_count % 10 == 0:
   573→                queue_size = queue.task_queue.qsize()
   574→                queue.logger.debug(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} iteration {iteration_count}: queue_size={queue_size}")
   575→
   576→            # Get next task (blocks with timeout)
   577→            try:
   578→                queue.logger.debug(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} attempting to get task from queue...")
   579→                get_start = time.time()
   580→                priority, timestamp, task = queue.task_queue.get(timeout=1.0)
   581→                get_elapsed = time.time() - get_start
   582→                queue.logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} retrieved task {task.id} from queue (queue.get took {get_elapsed:.3f}s)")
   583→            except queue_mod.Empty:
   584→                queue.logger.debug(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} queue empty, continuing...")
   585→                continue
   586→
   587→            # Process the task
   588→            queue.logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} starting process_task_impl for {task.id}")
   589→            process_task_impl(queue, task, worker_name)
   590→            queue.logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} completed process_task_impl for {task.id}")
   591→
   592→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   593→            queue.logger.error(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} error in iteration {iteration_count}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   594→            time.sleep(1.0)
   595→        except BaseException as e:
   596→            # Catch any unexpected exceptions to prevent worker from dying silently
   597→            queue.logger.critical(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} UNEXPECTED ERROR in iteration {iteration_count}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   598→            time.sleep(1.0)
   599→
   600→    queue.logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] {worker_name} stopped after {iteration_count} iterations")
   601→
   602→
   603→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   604→# Monitor loop
   605→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   606→
   607→def _monitor_loop(queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue"):
   608→    """Background monitoring and maintenance loop."""
   609→    queue.logger.info("Queue monitor started")
   610→
   611→    while queue.running and not queue.shutdown_event.is_set():
   612→        try:
   613→            # Monitor loop placeholder - future home for memory/queue/timeout monitoring
   614→            time.sleep(10.0)  # Monitor every 10 seconds
   615→
   616→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   617→            queue.logger.error(f"Monitor error: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   618→            time.sleep(5.0)
   619→
   620→    queue.logger.info("Queue monitor stopped")
   621→
```

```
    12→from headless_model_management import GenerationTask
    13→
    14→def db_task_to_generation_task(db_task_params: dict, task_id: str, task_type: str, wan2gp_path: str, debug_mode: bool = False) -> GenerationTask:
    15→    """
    16→    Convert a database task row to a GenerationTask object for the queue system.
    17→    """
    18→    headless_logger.debug(f"Converting DB task to GenerationTask", task_id=task_id)
    19→
    20→    prompt = db_task_params.get("prompt", "")
    21→
    22→    # For img2img tasks, empty prompt is acceptable (will use minimal changes)
    23→    # Provide a minimal default prompt to avoid errors
    24→    img2img_task_types = {"z_image_turbo_i2i", "qwen_image_edit", "qwen_image_style", "image_inpaint"}
    25→    if not prompt:
    26→        if task_type in img2img_task_types:
    27→            prompt = " "  # Minimal prompt for img2img
    28→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id}: Using minimal prompt for img2img task", task_id=task_id)
    29→        else:
    30→            raise ValueError(f"Task {task_id}: prompt is required")
    31→    
    32→    model = db_task_params.get("model")
    33→    if not model:
    34→        from source.task_handlers.tasks.task_types import get_default_model
    35→        model = get_default_model(task_type)
    36→    
    37→    generation_params = {}
    38→    
    39→    param_whitelist = {
    40→        "negative_prompt", "resolution", "video_length", "num_inference_steps",
    41→        "guidance_scale", "seed", "embedded_guidance_scale", "flow_shift",
    42→        "audio_guidance_scale", "repeat_generation", "multi_images_gen_type",
    43→        "guidance2_scale", "guidance3_scale", "guidance_phases", "switch_threshold", "switch_threshold2", "model_switch_phase",
    44→        "video_guide", "video_mask",
    45→        "video_prompt_type", "control_net_weight", "control_net_weight2",
    46→        "keep_frames_video_guide", "video_guide_outpainting", "mask_expand",
    47→        "image_prompt_type", "image_start", "image_end", "image_refs",
    48→        "frames_positions", "image_guide", "image_mask",
    49→        "model_mode", "video_source", "keep_frames_video_source",
    50→        "audio_guide", "audio_guide2", "audio_source", "audio_prompt_type", "speakers_locations",
    51→        "activated_loras", "loras_multipliers", "additional_loras", "loras",
    52→        "tea_cache_setting", "tea_cache_start_step_perc", "RIFLEx_setting",
    53→        "slg_switch", "slg_layers", "slg_start_perc", "slg_end_perc",
    54→        "cfg_star_switch", "cfg_zero_step", "prompt_enhancer",
    55→        # Hires fix parameters
    56→        "hires_scale", "hires_steps", "hires_denoise", "hires_upscale_method", "lightning_lora_strength",
    57→        "sliding_window_size", "sliding_window_overlap", "sliding_window_overlap_noise",
    58→        "sliding_window_discard_last_frames", "latent_noise_mask_strength",
    59→        "vid2vid_init_video", "vid2vid_init_strength",
    60→        "remove_background_images_ref", "temporal_upsampling", "spatial_upsampling",
    61→        "film_grain_intensity", "film_grain_saturation",
    62→        "image_refs_relative_size",
    63→        "output_dir", "custom_output_dir",
    64→        "override_profile",
    65→        "image", "image_url", "mask_url",
    66→        "style_reference_image", "subject_reference_image",
    67→        "style_reference_strength", "subject_strength",
    68→        "subject_description", "in_this_scene",
    69→        "output_format", "enable_base64_output", "enable_sync_mode",
    70→        # Uni3C motion guidance parameters
    71→        "use_uni3c", "uni3c_guide_video", "uni3c_strength",
    72→        "uni3c_start_percent", "uni3c_end_percent",
    73→        "uni3c_keep_on_gpu", "uni3c_frame_policy",
    74→        "uni3c_zero_empty_frames", "uni3c_blackout_last_frame",
    75→        # Image-to-image parameters
    76→        "denoising_strength",
    77→        # Multi-frame guide images
    78→        "guide_images",
    79→    }
    80→    
    81→    for param in param_whitelist:
    82→        if param in db_task_params:
    83→            generation_params[param] = db_task_params[param]
    84→    
    85→    # Layer 1 Uni3C logging - detect whitelist failures early
    86→    if "use_uni3c" in generation_params:
    87→        headless_logger.info(
    88→            f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: use_uni3c={generation_params.get('use_uni3c')}, "
    89→            f"guide_video={generation_params.get('uni3c_guide_video', 'NOT_SET')}, "
    90→            f"strength={generation_params.get('uni3c_strength', 'NOT_SET')}"
    91→        )
    92→    elif db_task_params.get("use_uni3c"):
    93→        # CRITICAL: Detect when whitelist is missing the param
    94→        headless_logger.warning(
    95→            f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: ⚠️ use_uni3c was in db_task_params but NOT in generation_params! "
    96→            f"Check param_whitelist in task_conversion.py"
    97→        )
    98→    
    99→    # Extract orchestrator parameters
   100→    extracted_params = extract_orchestrator_parameters(db_task_params, task_id)
   101→
   102→    if "phase_config" in extracted_params:
   103→        db_task_params["phase_config"] = extracted_params["phase_config"]
   104→
   105→    # Copy additional_loras from extracted params if not already in generation_params
   106→    if "additional_loras" in extracted_params and "additional_loras" not in generation_params:
   107→        generation_params["additional_loras"] = extracted_params["additional_loras"]
   108→    
   109→    if "steps" in db_task_params and "num_inference_steps" not in generation_params:
   110→        generation_params["num_inference_steps"] = db_task_params["steps"]
   111→    
   112→    # Note: LoRA resolution is handled centrally in HeadlessTaskQueue._convert_to_wgp_task()
   113→    # via TaskConfig/LoRAConfig which detects URLs and downloads them automatically
   114→
   115→    # Qwen Task Handlers
   116→    qwen_handler = QwenHandler(
   117→        wan_root=wan2gp_path,
   118→        task_id=task_id)
   119→
   120→    if task_type == "qwen_image_edit":
   121→        qwen_handler.handle_qwen_image_edit(db_task_params, generation_params)
   122→    elif task_type == "qwen_image_hires":
   123→        qwen_handler.handle_qwen_image_hires(db_task_params, generation_params)
   124→    elif task_type == "image_inpaint":
   125→        qwen_handler.handle_image_inpaint(db_task_params, generation_params)
   126→    elif task_type == "annotated_image_edit":
   127→        qwen_handler.handle_annotated_image_edit(db_task_params, generation_params)
   128→    elif task_type == "qwen_image_style":
   129→        qwen_handler.handle_qwen_image_style(db_task_params, generation_params)
   130→        prompt = generation_params.get("prompt", prompt)
   131→        model = "qwen_image_edit_20B"
   132→    elif task_type == "qwen_image":
   133→        qwen_handler.handle_qwen_image(db_task_params, generation_params)
   134→        model = "qwen_image_edit_20B"
   135→    elif task_type == "qwen_image_2512":
   136→        qwen_handler.handle_qwen_image_2512(db_task_params, generation_params)
   137→        model = "qwen_image_2512_20B"
   138→    elif task_type == "z_image_turbo":
   139→        # Z-Image turbo - fast text-to-image generation
   140→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "")  # No input image
   141→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)  # Single image output
   142→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 0)  # Z-Image uses guidance_scale=0
   143→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", int(db_task_params.get("num_inference_steps", 8)))
   144→
   145→        # Resolution handling
   146→        if "resolution" in db_task_params:
   147→            generation_params["resolution"] = db_task_params["resolution"]
   148→
   149→        # Override model to use Z-Image (user might pass "z-image" with hyphen)
   150→        model = "z_image"
   151→
   152→    elif task_type == "z_image_turbo_i2i":
   153→        # Z-Image turbo img2img - fast image-to-image generation
   154→        import tempfile
   155→        from PIL import Image
   156→
   157→        # Get image URL
   158→        image_url = db_task_params.get("image") or db_task_params.get("image_url")
   159→        if not image_url:
   160→            raise ValueError(f"Task {task_id}: 'image' or 'image_url' required for z_image_turbo_i2i")
   161→
   162→        # Download image to local file (required for WGP)
   163→        local_image_path = None
   164→        try:
   165→            import requests
   166→            import os
   167→            headless_logger.debug(f"Downloading image for img2img: {image_url}", task_id=task_id)
   168→            response = requests.get(image_url, timeout=30)
   169→            response.raise_for_status()
   170→
   171→            # Save to temp file and keep it for WGP to use
   172→            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".png", delete=False) as tmp_file:
   173→                tmp_file.write(response.content)
   174→                local_image_path = tmp_file.name
   175→
   176→            headless_logger.info(f"[Z_IMAGE_I2I] Downloaded image to: {local_image_path}", task_id=task_id)
   177→
   178→            # Handle resolution - auto-detect from image or use provided
   179→            image_size = db_task_params.get("image_size", "auto")
   180→            if image_size == "auto" or not db_task_params.get("resolution"):
   181→                with Image.open(local_image_path) as img:
   182→                    width, height = img.size
   183→
   184→                    # Scale to approximately 1 megapixel (1024x1024) while preserving aspect ratio
   185→                    target_pixels = IMG2IMG_TARGET_MEGAPIXELS
   186→                    current_pixels = width * height
   187→
   188→                    if current_pixels > 0:
   189→                        # Calculate scale factor to reach target pixels
   190→                        import math
   191→                        scale = math.sqrt(target_pixels / current_pixels)
   192→
   193→                        # Apply scale to both dimensions
   194→                        scaled_width = int(round(width * scale))
   195→                        scaled_height = int(round(height * scale))
   196→
   197→                        # Round to nearest multiple of 8 (Z-Image requirement)
   198→                        scaled_width = (scaled_width // 8) * 8
   199→                        scaled_height = (scaled_height // 8) * 8
   200→
   201→                        # Ensure minimum size
   202→                        scaled_width = max(8, scaled_width)
   203→                        scaled_height = max(8, scaled_height)
   204→
   205→                        generation_params["resolution"] = f"{scaled_width}x{scaled_height}"
   206→                        headless_logger.info(
   207→                            f"Scaled resolution: {width}x{height} ({current_pixels:,} px) → "
   208→                            f"{scaled_width}x{scaled_height} ({scaled_width*scaled_height:,} px, scale={scale:.3f})",
   209→                            task_id=task_id
   210→                        )
   211→                    else:
   212→                        generation_params["resolution"] = DEFAULT_IMAGE_RESOLUTION
   213→            elif "resolution" in db_task_params:
   214→                generation_params["resolution"] = db_task_params["resolution"]
   215→            else:
   216→                generation_params["resolution"] = DEFAULT_IMAGE_RESOLUTION
   217→
   218→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   219→            # If download fails, clean up and raise error
   220→            if local_image_path:
   221→                import os
   222→                try:
   223→                    os.unlink(local_image_path)
   224→                except OSError as e_cleanup:
   225→                    headless_logger.debug(f"Failed to clean up temp image file after download error: {e_cleanup}", task_id=task_id)
   226→            raise ValueError(f"Task {task_id}: Failed to download image for img2img: {e}") from e
   227→
   228→        # CRITICAL: Pass local file path (not URL) to WGP
   229→        generation_params["image_start"] = local_image_path
   230→
   231→        # Set img2img parameters
   232→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "")  # Image input handled via image_start
   233→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)  # Single image output
   234→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 0)  # Z-Image uses guidance_scale=0
   235→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", int(db_task_params.get("num_inference_steps", 12)))
   236→
   237→        # CRITICAL: Add denoising_strength to generation_params so it gets passed through
   238→        actual_strength = db_task_params.get("denoising_strength") or db_task_params.get("denoise_strength") or db_task_params.get("strength", 0.7)
   239→        generation_params["denoising_strength"] = actual_strength
   240→
   241→        headless_logger.info(
   242→            f"[Z_IMAGE_I2I] Setup complete - local_image={local_image_path}, resolution={generation_params['resolution']}, "
   243→            f"strength={actual_strength}, steps={generation_params['num_inference_steps']}",
   244→            task_id=task_id
   245→        )
   246→
   247→        # Override model to use Z-Image img2img
   248→        model = "z_image_img2img"
   249→
   250→    elif task_type in ("ltx2_multiframe", "ltx2_ic_multiframe"):
   251→        # Download guide images from URLs to local paths
   252→        guide_images_raw = db_task_params.get("guide_images", [])
   253→        if guide_images_raw:
   254→            import tempfile
   255→            import requests
   256→            resolved_guides = []
   257→            for i, entry in enumerate(guide_images_raw):
   258→                img_source = entry.get("image") or entry.get("image_url")
   259→                if img_source and img_source.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
   260→                    response = requests.get(img_source, timeout=30)
   261→                    response.raise_for_status()
   262→                    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".png", delete=False) as tmp:
   263→                        tmp.write(response.content)
   264→                        local_path = tmp.name
   265→                else:
   266→                    local_path = img_source
   267→                resolved_guides.append({
   268→                    "image": local_path,
   269→                    "frame_idx": entry.get("frame_idx", 0),
   270→                    "strength": entry.get("strength", 1.0),
   271→                })
   272→            generation_params["guide_images"] = resolved_guides
   273→            headless_logger.info(
   274→                f"[{task_type.upper()}] Resolved {len(resolved_guides)} guide images",
   275→                task_id=task_id
   276→            )
   277→
   278→    # Defaults
   279→    essential_defaults = {
   280→        "seed": -1,
   281→        "negative_prompt": "",
   282→    }
   283→    for param, default_value in essential_defaults.items():
   284→        if param not in generation_params:
   285→            generation_params[param] = default_value
   286→    
   287→    # Phase Config Override
   288→    if "phase_config" in db_task_params:
   289→        try:
   290→            steps_per_phase = db_task_params["phase_config"].get("steps_per_phase", [2, 2, 2])
   291→            phase_config_steps = sum(steps_per_phase)
   292→
   293→            parsed_phase_config = parse_phase_config(
   294→                phase_config=db_task_params["phase_config"],
   295→                num_inference_steps=phase_config_steps,
   296→                task_id=task_id,
   297→                model_name=model,
   298→                debug_mode=debug_mode
   299→            )
   300→
   301→            generation_params["num_inference_steps"] = phase_config_steps
   302→
   303→            for key in ["guidance_phases", "switch_threshold", "switch_threshold2",
   304→                       "guidance_scale", "guidance2_scale", "guidance3_scale",
   305→                       "flow_shift", "sample_solver", "model_switch_phase",
   306→                       "lora_names", "lora_multipliers", "additional_loras"]:
   307→                if key in parsed_phase_config and parsed_phase_config[key] is not None:
   308→                    generation_params[key] = parsed_phase_config[key]
   309→
   310→            if "lora_names" in parsed_phase_config:
   311→                generation_params["activated_loras"] = parsed_phase_config["lora_names"]
   312→            if "lora_multipliers" in parsed_phase_config:
   313→                generation_params["loras_multipliers"] = " ".join(str(m) for m in parsed_phase_config["lora_multipliers"])
   314→
   315→            if "_patch_config" in parsed_phase_config:
   316→                generation_params["_parsed_phase_config"] = parsed_phase_config
   317→                generation_params["_phase_config_model_name"] = model
   318→            
   319→            # Note: LoRA URL resolution is handled centrally in HeadlessTaskQueue._convert_to_wgp_task()
   320→            # URLs in activated_loras are detected by LoRAConfig.from_params() and downloaded there
   321→        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   322→            raise ValueError(f"Task {task_id}: Invalid phase_config: {e}") from e
   323→
   324→    priority = db_task_params.get("priority", 0)
   325→    if task_type.endswith("_orchestrator"):
   326→        priority = max(priority, 10)
   327→    
   328→    generation_task = GenerationTask(
   329→        id=task_id,
   330→        model=model,
   331→        prompt=prompt,
   332→        parameters=generation_params,
   333→        priority=priority
   334→    )
   335→    
   336→    return generation_task
   337→
   338→
```

```
   111→def get_default_model(task_type: str) -> str:
   112→    """
   113→    Get the default model for a given task type.
   114→
   115→    Args:
   116→        task_type: The task type identifier
   117→
   118→    Returns:
   119→        The default model identifier, or "t2v" as fallback
   120→    """

   125→    """Check if a task type is a WGP generation task."""
   126→    return task_type in WGP_TASK_TYPES
   127→

   130→    """Check if a task type can be directly queued."""
```

```
   102→def _get_param(key, *sources, default=_MISSING, prefer_truthy: bool = False):
   103→    """
   104→    Get a parameter from multiple sources with precedence (first source wins).
   105→
   106→    By default, only skips None values.
   107→    If prefer_truthy=True, also skips falsy non-bool values (e.g. "", {}, [], 0),
   108→    matching historical `a or b` fallback semantics while still preserving explicit booleans.
   109→    """
   110→    for source in sources:
   111→        if not source or key not in source:
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        value = source[key]
   115→        if value is None:
   116→            continue
   117→
   118→        if prefer_truthy and not isinstance(value, bool) and not value:
   119→            continue
   120→
   121→        return value
   122→
   123→    return None if default is _MISSING else default
   124→

   135→    orchestrator_run_id = segment_params.get("orchestrator_run_id")
   136→    segment_idx = segment_params.get("segment_index")

   139→    orchestrator_details: dict = segment_params.get("orchestrator_details") or segment_params.get("full_orchestrator_payload")
   140→
   141→    if is_standalone:
   142→        # Standalone mode: use provided payload, default segment_idx to 0
   143→        if segment_idx is None:
   144→            segment_idx = 0
   145→        if not orchestrator_details:

   147→        headless_logger.debug(f"Running in standalone mode (individual_travel_segment)", task_id=task_id)
```

```
    77→def _set_wgp_output_paths(wgp_module, output_dir: str) -> None:
    78→    """Set output paths on WGP module-level state and server_config dict.
    79→
    80→    Must be called after initializing WGP defaults. The WGP vendored
    81→    code reads these paths at initialization AND at generation time.
    82→    """
    83→    if not hasattr(wgp_module, 'server_config'):
    84→        wgp_module.server_config = {}
    85→    wgp_module.server_config['save_path'] = output_dir
    86→    wgp_module.server_config['image_save_path'] = output_dir
    87→    wgp_module.save_path = output_dir
    88→    wgp_module.image_save_path = output_dir
    89→
    90→
    91→class WanOrchestrator:
    92→    """Thin adapter around `wgp.generate_video` for easier programmatic use."""
    93→
    94→    def __init__(self, wan_root: str, main_output_dir: Optional[str] = None):
    95→        """Initialize orchestrator with WanGP directory.
    96→
    97→        Args:
    98→            wan_root: Path to WanGP repository root directory (MUST be absolute path to Wan2GP/)
    99→            main_output_dir: Optional path for output directory. If not provided, defaults to
   100→                           'outputs' directory next to wan_root (preserves backwards compatibility)
   101→
   102→        IMPORTANT: Caller MUST have already changed to wan_root directory before calling this.
   103→        wgp.py uses relative paths and expects to run from Wan2GP/.
   104→        """
   105→        import logging
   106→        _init_logger = logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue')
   107→
   108→        # Store the wan_root (should match current directory)
   109→        self.wan_root = os.path.abspath(wan_root)
   110→        current_dir = os.getcwd()
   111→
   112→        if is_debug_enabled():
   113→            _init_logger.info(f"[INIT_DEBUG] WanOrchestrator.__init__ called with wan_root: {self.wan_root}")
   114→            _init_logger.info(f"[INIT_DEBUG] Current working directory: {current_dir}")
   115→
   116→        # CRITICAL CHECK: Verify caller already changed to the correct directory
   117→        # wgp.py will import and execute module-level code that uses relative paths like "defaults/*.json"
   118→        # If we're in the wrong directory, wgp will load 0 models and fail mysteriously
   119→        if current_dir != self.wan_root:
   120→            error_msg = (
   121→                f"CRITICAL: WanOrchestrator must be initialized from Wan2GP directory!\n"
   122→                f"  Current directory: {current_dir}\n"
   123→                f"  Expected directory: {self.wan_root}\n"
   124→                f"  Caller must chdir() before creating WanOrchestrator instance."
   125→            )
   126→            if is_debug_enabled():
   127→                _init_logger.error(f"[INIT_DEBUG] {error_msg}")
   128→            raise RuntimeError(error_msg)
   129→
   130→        # Verify Wan2GP structure
   131→        if not os.path.isdir("defaults"):
   132→            raise RuntimeError(
   133→                f"defaults/ directory not found in {current_dir}. "
   134→                f"This doesn't appear to be a valid Wan2GP directory!"
   135→            )
   136→        if not os.path.isdir("models"):
   137→            if is_debug_enabled():
   138→                _init_logger.warning(f"[INIT_DEBUG] models/ directory not found in {current_dir}")
   139→
   140→        # Ensure Wan2GP is first in sys.path so wgp.py imports correctly
   141→        if self.wan_root in sys.path:
   142→            sys.path.remove(self.wan_root)
   143→        sys.path.insert(0, self.wan_root)
   144→        if is_debug_enabled():
   145→            _init_logger.info(f"[INIT_DEBUG] Added {self.wan_root} to sys.path[0]")
   146→
   147→        # Optional smoke/CPU-only modes
   148→        self.smoke_mode = bool(os.environ.get("HEADLESS_WAN2GP_SMOKE", ""))
   149→        force_cpu = os.environ.get("HEADLESS_WAN2GP_FORCE_CPU", "0") == "1"
   150→
   151→        # Force CPU if requested and guard CUDA capability queries before importing WGP
   152→        if force_cpu and not os.environ.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"):
   153→            os.environ["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = "-1"
   154→        try:
   155→            import torch  # type: ignore
   156→            # If CUDA isn't available, stub capability query used by upstream on import
   157→            if force_cpu or not torch.cuda.is_available():
   158→                try:
   159→                    def _safe_get_device_capability(device=None):
   160→                        return (8, 0)
   161→                    torch.cuda.get_device_capability = _safe_get_device_capability  # type: ignore
   162→                except (RuntimeError, AttributeError) as e:
   163→                    _init_logger.debug("Failed to stub torch.cuda.get_device_capability: %s", e)
   164→        except ImportError as e:
   165→            _init_logger.debug("Failed to import torch for CPU/CUDA guards: %s", e)
   166→
   167→        # Force a headless Matplotlib backend to avoid Tkinter requirements during upstream imports
   168→        if not os.environ.get("MPLBACKEND"):
   169→            os.environ["MPLBACKEND"] = "Agg"
   170→        try:
   171→            import matplotlib  # type: ignore
   172→            _orig_use = matplotlib.use  # type: ignore
   173→            def _force_agg(_backend=None, *args, **kwargs):
   174→                try:
   175→                    return _orig_use('Agg', force=True)
   176→                except (RuntimeError, ValueError):
   177→                    return None
   178→            matplotlib.use = _force_agg  # type: ignore
   179→        except ImportError as e:
   180→            _init_logger.debug("Failed to force matplotlib Agg backend: %s", e)
   181→
   182→        # Pre-import config hygiene for upstream wgp.py
   183→        # Some older configs may store preload_model_policy as an int, but upstream now expects a list
   184→        try:
   185→            cfg_path = os.path.join(self.wan_root, "wgp_config.json")
   186→            if os.path.isfile(cfg_path):
   187→                import json as _json
   188→                with open(cfg_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as _r:
   189→                    _cfg = _json.load(_r)
   190→                changed = False
   191→                pmp = _cfg.get("preload_model_policy", [])
   192→                if isinstance(pmp, int):
   193→                    _cfg["preload_model_policy"] = []  # disable preloading and fix type
   194→                    changed = True
   195→                # Ensure save paths exist and are strings
   196→                if not isinstance(_cfg.get("save_path", ""), str):
   197→                    _cfg["save_path"] = "outputs"
   198→                    changed = True
   199→                if not isinstance(_cfg.get("image_save_path", ""), str):
   200→                    _cfg["image_save_path"] = "outputs"
```

```
1→"""Generation strategy convenience methods for WanOrchestrator.
     2→
     3→Provides typed wrappers (T2V, VACE, Flux, config-based) around the core
     4→:meth:`WanOrchestrator.generate` method.  Each function is bound to the
     5→orchestrator at class-definition time via ``orchestrator.py``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Optional
     9→
    10→from source.core.log import generation_logger
    11→
    12→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    13→    from source.core.params import TaskConfig
    14→
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Text-to-Video
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→def generate_t2v(self, prompt: str, model_type: str = None, **kwargs) -> str:
    21→    """Generate text-to-video content.
    22→
    23→    Thin wrapper around :meth:`WanOrchestrator.generate` that adds a
    24→    warning if the currently-loaded model is not a T2V model.
    25→
    26→    Args:
    27→        self: WanOrchestrator instance.
    28→        prompt: Text prompt for generation.
    29→        model_type: Optional model override.
    30→        **kwargs: Additional parameters forwarded to generate().
    31→
    32→    Returns:
    33→        Path to generated video file.
    34→    """
    35→    if not self._is_t2v():
    36→        generation_logger.warning(f"Current model {self.current_model} may not be optimized for T2V")
    37→    return self.generate(prompt=prompt, model_type=model_type, **kwargs)
    38→
    39→
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→# VACE (Video Any Controllable Edit)
    42→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→def generate_vace(
    45→    self,
    46→    prompt: str,
    47→    video_guide: str,
    48→    model_type: str = None,
    49→    video_mask: Optional[str] = None,
    50→    video_prompt_type: str = "VP",
    51→    control_net_weight: float = 1.0,
    52→    control_net_weight2: float = 1.0,
    53→    **kwargs,
    54→) -> str:
    55→    """Generate VACE controlled video content.
    56→
    57→    Thin wrapper around :meth:`WanOrchestrator.generate` that adds
    58→    a warning if the currently-loaded model is not a VACE model.
    59→
    60→    Args:
    61→        self: WanOrchestrator instance.
    62→        prompt: Text prompt for generation.
    63→        video_guide: Path to primary control video (required).
    64→        model_type: Optional model override.
    65→        video_mask: Path to primary mask video (optional).
    66→        video_prompt_type: VACE encoding type (e.g., "VP", "VPD", "VPDA").
    67→        control_net_weight: Strength for first VACE encoding.
    68→        control_net_weight2: Strength for second VACE encoding.
    69→        **kwargs: Additional parameters forwarded to generate().
    70→
    71→    Returns:
    72→        Path to generated video file.
    73→    """
    74→    if not self._is_vace():
    75→        generation_logger.warning(f"Current model {self.current_model} may not be a VACE model")
    76→
    77→    # Log LoRA parameters at VACE level
    78→    generation_logger.debug(f"generate_vace received kwargs: {list(kwargs.keys())}")
    79→    if "lora_names" in kwargs:
    80→        generation_logger.debug(f"generate_vace lora_names: {kwargs['lora_names']}")
    81→    if "lora_multipliers" in kwargs:
    82→        generation_logger.debug(f"generate_vace lora_multipliers: {kwargs['lora_multipliers']}")
    83→
    84→    return self.generate(
    85→        prompt=prompt,
    86→        model_type=model_type,
    87→        video_guide=video_guide,
    88→        video_mask=video_mask,
    89→        video_prompt_type=video_prompt_type,
    90→        control_net_weight=control_net_weight,
    91→        control_net_weight2=control_net_weight2,
    92→        **kwargs,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97→# Flux image generation
    98→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99→
   100→def generate_flux(self, prompt: str, images: int = 4, model_type: str = None, **kwargs) -> str:
   101→    """Generate Flux images.
   102→
   103→    Thin wrapper around :meth:`WanOrchestrator.generate` that adds a
   104→    warning if the currently-loaded model is not a Flux model.
   105→
   106→    Args:
   107→        self: WanOrchestrator instance.
   108→        prompt: Text prompt for generation.
   109→        images: Number of images to generate (maps to video_length).
   110→        model_type: Optional model override.
   111→        **kwargs: Additional parameters forwarded to generate().
   112→
   113→    Returns:
   114→        Path to generated image(s).
   115→    """
   116→    if not self._is_flux():
   117→        generation_logger.warning(f"Current model {self.current_model} may not be a Flux model")
   118→
   119→    return self.generate(
   120→        prompt=prompt,
   121→        model_type=model_type,
   122→        video_length=images,  # For Flux, video_length = number of images
   123→        **kwargs,
   124→    )
   125→
   126→
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→# Config-based generation (Qwen / universal typed API)
   129→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   130→
   131→def generate_with_config(self, config: 'TaskConfig') -> str:
   132→    """Generate content using a typed TaskConfig object.
   133→
   134→    This is the new typed API that provides cleaner parameter handling:
   135→    1. All parameters are already parsed and validated in TaskConfig
   136→    2. LoRAs are already resolved (downloaded if needed)
   137→    3. Phase config is already applied
   138→
   139→    Works for all model types (T2V, VACE, Flux, Qwen) but is the
   140→    primary entry-point for Qwen-style image generation.
   141→
   142→    Args:
   143→        self: WanOrchestrator instance.
   144→        config: TaskConfig object with all generation parameters.
   145→
   146→    Returns:
   147→        Path to generated output file(s).
```

```
1→"""Travel orchestrator task handler - creates and manages segment tasks."""
     2→
     3→from pathlib import Path
     4→import uuid
     5→from datetime import datetime
     6→
     7→# Import structured logging
     8→from ...core.log import travel_logger, safe_json_repr, safe_dict_repr
     9→
    10→from ... import db_operations as db_ops
    11→from ...core.db import config as db_config
    12→from ...utils import (
    13→    parse_resolution,
    14→    snap_resolution_to_model_grid,
    15→    get_model_grid_size,
    16→    upload_and_get_final_output_location,
    17→    get_video_frame_count_and_fps)
    18→from ...core.params.structure_guidance import StructureGuidanceConfig
    19→from ...core.params.task_result import TaskResult
    20→
    21→from .svi_config import SVI_DEFAULT_PARAMS, SVI_STITCH_OVERLAP
    22→from .debug_utils import log_ram_usage
    23→
    24→# Default seed used when no seed_base is provided in the orchestrator payload
    25→DEFAULT_SEED_BASE = 12345
    26→# Offset added to the base seed to derive a deterministic but distinct seed for upscaling
    27→UPSCALE_SEED_OFFSET = 5000
    28→
    29→
    30→def _get_model_fps(orchestrator_payload: dict) -> int:
    31→    """Return native FPS for the model. LTX-2 = 24, Wan = 16."""
    32→    return 24 if orchestrator_payload.get("use_ltx2") else 16
    33→
    34→
    35→def _get_frame_step(orchestrator_payload: dict) -> int:
    36→    """Return frame quantization step. LTX-2 = 8, Wan = 4."""
    37→    return 8 if orchestrator_payload.get("use_ltx2") else 4
    38→
    39→
    40→def _quantize_frames(raw_length: int, frame_step: int) -> int:
    41→    """Quantize a frame count to *frame_step*·N + 1."""
    42→    return ((raw_length - 1) // frame_step) * frame_step + 1
    43→
    44→
    45→def handle_travel_orchestrator_task(task_params_from_db: dict, main_output_dir_base: Path, orchestrator_task_id_str: str, orchestrator_project_id: str | None):
    46→    travel_logger.essential("Starting travel orchestrator task", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    47→    log_ram_usage("Orchestrator start", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    48→    travel_logger.debug(f"Project ID: {orchestrator_project_id}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    49→    # Safe logging: Use safe_json_repr to prevent hangs on large nested structures
    50→    travel_logger.debug(f"Task params: {safe_json_repr(task_params_from_db)}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    51→
    52→    try:
    53→        if 'orchestrator_details' not in task_params_from_db:
    54→            travel_logger.error("'orchestrator_details' not found in task_params_from_db", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    55→            return TaskResult.failed("orchestrator_details missing")
    56→        
    57→        orchestrator_payload = task_params_from_db['orchestrator_details']
    58→        # Safe logging: Use safe_dict_repr for better performance than JSON serialization
    59→        travel_logger.debug(f"Orchestrator payload: {safe_dict_repr(orchestrator_payload)}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    60→
    61→        # Validate required keys are present before proceeding
    62→        from source.core.params.contracts import validate_orchestrator_details
    63→        validate_orchestrator_details(orchestrator_payload, context="travel_orchestrator", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    64→
    65→        # Normalize chain_segments: true = chain segments together (default), false = keep separate
    66→        chain_segments_raw = orchestrator_payload.get("chain_segments", True)
    67→        orchestrator_payload["chain_segments"] = bool(chain_segments_raw)
    68→
    69→        # Parse phase_config if present and add parsed values to orchestrator_payload
    70→        if "phase_config" in orchestrator_payload:
    71→            travel_logger.info(f"phase_config detected in orchestrator - parsing comprehensive phase configuration", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    72→
    73→            try:
    74→                from source.core.params.phase_config_parser import parse_phase_config
    75→
    76→                # Get total steps from phase_config
    77→                phase_config = orchestrator_payload["phase_config"]
    78→                steps_per_phase = phase_config.get("steps_per_phase", [2, 2, 2])
    79→                total_steps = sum(steps_per_phase)
    80→
    81→                # Parse phase_config to get all parameters
    82→                parsed = parse_phase_config(
    83→                    phase_config=phase_config,
    84→                    num_inference_steps=total_steps,
    85→                    task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str,
    86→                    model_name=orchestrator_payload.get("model_name")
    87→                )
    88→
    89→                # Add parsed values to orchestrator_payload so segments can use them
    90→                # NOTE: We use lora_names + lora_multipliers directly, NOT additional_loras
    91→                for key in ["guidance_phases", "switch_threshold", "switch_threshold2",
    92→                           "guidance_scale", "guidance2_scale", "guidance3_scale",
    93→                           "flow_shift", "sample_solver", "model_switch_phase",
    94→                           "lora_names", "lora_multipliers"]:
    95→                    if key in parsed and parsed[key] is not None:
    96→                        orchestrator_payload[key] = parsed[key]
    97→                        travel_logger.debug(f"[ORCHESTRATOR_PHASE_CONFIG] Added {key} to orchestrator_payload: {parsed[key]}")
    98→
    99→                # Also update num_inference_steps
   100→                orchestrator_payload["num_inference_steps"] = total_steps
```

```
1→"""
     2→Join Clips Orchestrator - Main handler for sequentially joining video clips.
     3→
     4→Takes a list of video clips and creates a chain of join_clips_child
     5→tasks to progressively build them into a single seamless video.
     6→
     7→__all__ is defined to ensure underscore-prefixed names are exported via import *.
     8→
     9→Pattern:
    10→    Input: [clip_A, clip_B, clip_C, clip_D]
    11→
    12→    Creates:
    13→        join_0: clip_A + clip_B -> AB.mp4 (no dependency)
    14→        join_1: AB.mp4 + clip_C -> ABC.mp4 (depends on join_0)
    15→        join_2: ABC.mp4 + clip_D -> ABCD.mp4 (depends on join_1)
    16→"""
    17→
    18→import json
    19→import subprocess
    20→from pathlib import Path
    21→from typing import Tuple, List, Optional
    22→
    23→from source import db_operations as db_ops
    24→from source.utils import download_video_if_url, upload_intermediate_file_to_storage
    25→from source.media.video import reverse_video
    26→from source.core.log import orchestrator_logger
    27→
    28→from source.task_handlers.join.shared import (
    29→    _check_existing_join_tasks,
    30→    _extract_join_settings_from_payload)
    31→from source.task_handlers.join.clip_validator import validate_clip_frames_for_join
    32→from source.task_handlers.join.task_builder import (
    33→    _create_join_chain_tasks,
    34→    _create_parallel_join_tasks)
    35→from source.task_handlers.join.vlm_enhancement import (
    36→    _extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm,
    37→    _generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins)
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["_get_video_resolution", "_handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task"]
    40→
    41→def _get_video_resolution(video_path: str | Path) -> Tuple[int, int] | None:
    42→    """
    43→    Get video resolution (width, height) using ffprobe.
    44→
    45→    Returns:
    46→        (width, height) tuple, or None if detection fails
    47→    """
    48→    try:
    49→        probe_cmd = [
    50→            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',
    51→            '-select_streams', 'v:0',
    52→            '-show_entries', 'stream=width,height',
    53→            '-of', 'csv=p=0',
    54→            str(video_path)
    55→        ]
    56→        result = subprocess.run(probe_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    57→        if result.returncode != 0:
    58→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[GET_RESOLUTION] ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
    59→            return None
    60→        width_str, height_str = result.stdout.strip().split(',')
    61→        return (int(width_str), int(height_str))
    62→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
    63→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[GET_RESOLUTION] Error detecting resolution: {e}")
    64→        return None
    65→
    66→def _handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task(
    67→    task_params_from_db: dict,
    68→    main_output_dir_base: Path,
    69→    orchestrator_task_id_str: str,
    70→    orchestrator_project_id: str | None) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    71→    """
    72→    Handle join_clips_orchestrator task - creates chained join_clips_child tasks.
    73→
    74→    Args:
    75→        task_params_from_db: Task parameters containing orchestrator_details
    76→        main_output_dir_base: Base output directory
    77→        orchestrator_task_id_str: Orchestrator task ID
    78→        orchestrator_project_id: Project ID for authorization
    79→
    80→    Returns:
    81→        (success: bool, message: str)
    82→    """
    83→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Starting orchestrator task {orchestrator_task_id_str}")
    84→
    85→    try:
    86→        # === 1. PARSE ORCHESTRATOR PAYLOAD ===
    87→        if 'orchestrator_details' not in task_params_from_db:
    88→            orchestrator_logger.debug("[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] ERROR: orchestrator_details missing")
    89→            return False, "orchestrator_details missing"
    90→
    91→        orchestrator_payload = task_params_from_db['orchestrator_details']
    92→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Orchestrator payload keys: {list(orchestrator_payload.keys())}")
    93→
    94→        # Extract required fields
    95→        clip_list = orchestrator_payload.get("clip_list", [])
    96→        run_id = orchestrator_payload.get("run_id")
    97→        loop_first_clip = orchestrator_payload.get("loop_first_clip", False)
    98→
    99→        # === DYNAMIC CLIP_LIST FROM SEGMENT TASKS ===
   100→        segment_task_ids = orchestrator_payload.get("segment_task_ids", [])
```

```
1→"""
     2→Travel Segment Processor - Orchestrator for Travel Segment Generation
     3→
     4→Coordinates guide video creation, mask video creation, and video_prompt_type
     5→construction for travel segments. The heavy lifting is delegated to:
     6→- guide_builder: guide video creation and supporting helpers
     7→- mask_builder: mask video creation and frame control
     8→
     9→Key Components:
    10→- TravelSegmentContext: dataclass holding all parameters for segment processing
    11→- TravelSegmentProcessor: orchestrator that delegates to builders and constructs results
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from dataclasses import dataclass
    16→from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple
    17→
    18→from source.core.log import travel_logger
    19→from source.utils import get_video_frame_count_and_fps
    20→
    21→from source.task_handlers.travel.guide_builder import (
    22→    create_guide_video as _build_guide_video,
    23→)
    24→from source.task_handlers.travel.mask_builder import (
    25→    create_mask_video as _build_mask_video,
    26→)
    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass
    30→class TravelSegmentContext:
    31→    """Contains all parameters needed for travel segment processing."""
    32→    task_id: str
    33→    segment_idx: int
    34→    model_name: str
    35→    total_frames_for_segment: int
    36→    parsed_res_wh: Tuple[int, int]
    37→    segment_processing_dir: Path
    38→    main_output_dir_base: Path  # Base output directory from worker
    39→    orchestrator_details: Dict[str, Any]
    40→    segment_params: Dict[str, Any]
    41→    mask_active_frames: bool
    42→    debug_enabled: bool
    43→
    44→
    45→class TravelSegmentProcessor:
    46→    """
    47→    Orchestrator for travel segment generation logic.
    48→
    49→    Eliminates code duplication between blocking and queue-based handlers
    50→    by providing a single implementation of guide video creation, mask video
    51→    creation, and video_prompt_type construction.
    52→
    53→    Guide and mask creation are delegated to guide_builder and mask_builder
    54→    respectively; this class handles VACE detection, video_prompt_type
    55→    construction, and the top-level process_segment workflow.
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    def __init__(self, ctx: TravelSegmentContext):
    59→        self.ctx = ctx
    60→        self.is_vace_model = self._detect_vace_model()
    61→
    62→    def _detect_vace_model(self) -> bool:
    63→        """Detect if this is a VACE model that requires guide videos."""
    64→        model_name = self.ctx.model_name.lower()
    65→
    66→        # Restrict VACE detection to explicit VACE model names only.
    67→        # Broad keywords like "lightning" misclassify non-VACE models.
    68→        is_vace = "vace" in model_name
    69→
    70→        travel_logger.debug(f"[VACE_DEBUG] Seg {self.ctx.segment_idx}: Model '{self.ctx.model_name}' -> is_vace_model = {is_vace}", task_id=self.ctx.task_id)
    71→        return is_vace
    72→
    73→    def _is_ltx2_model(self) -> bool:
    74→        """Check if this is an LTX-2 model."""
    75→        return "ltx2" in self.ctx.model_name.lower()
    76→
    77→    def create_guide_video(self) -> Optional[Path]:
    78→        """Create guide video for VACE models or debug mode.
    79→
    80→        Delegates to guide_builder.create_guide_video.
    81→        """
    82→        return _build_guide_video(self)
    83→
    84→    def create_mask_video(self) -> Optional[Path]:
    85→        """Create mask video for frame control.
    86→
    87→        Delegates to mask_builder.create_mask_video.
    88→        """
    89→        return _build_mask_video(self)
    90→
    91→    def create_video_prompt_type(self, mask_video_path: Optional[Path]) -> str:
    92→        """
    93→        Create video_prompt_type string for VACE compatibility.
    94→
    95→        Args:
    96→            mask_video_path: Path to mask video, if created
    97→
    98→        Returns:
    99→            video_prompt_type string (e.g., "VM", "VIM", "UM")
   100→        """
```

```
   334→    def test_latent_index_calculation(self):
   335→        """_to_latent_index should divide frame_idx by stride (integer division)."""
   336→        # Verify logic via inline replication (avoids torch import from ltx2.py)
   337→        def _to_latent_index(frame_idx, stride):
   338→            return int(frame_idx) // int(stride)
   339→
   340→        assert _to_latent_index(0, 8) == 0
   341→        assert _to_latent_index(7, 8) == 0
   342→        assert _to_latent_index(8, 8) == 1
   343→        assert _to_latent_index(40, 8) == 5
   344→        assert _to_latent_index(80, 8) == 10
   345→        assert _to_latent_index(104, 8) == 13
```

```
   473→                embedded_guidance_scale: Optional[float] = None,
   474→                # LoRA parameters

   476→                lora_multipliers: Optional[List] = None,  # Can be List[float] or List[str] for phase-config
   477→                # Other parameters
   478→                negative_prompt: Optional[str] = None,
   479→                batch_size: Optional[int] = None,
   480→                **kwargs) -> str:
   481→        """Generate content using the loaded model.
   482→
   483→        Args:

   486→            video_length: Number of frames for video or images for Flux

   489→            seed: Random seed for reproducibility

   493→            control_net_weight: Strength for first VACE encoding
   494→            control_net_weight2: Strength for second VACE encoding
   495→            embedded_guidance_scale: Flux-specific guidance
   496→            lora_names: List of LoRA filenames
   497→            lora_multipliers: List of LoRA strength multipliers
   498→            negative_prompt: Negative prompt text
   499→            batch_size: Batch size for generation
   500→            **kwargs: Additional parameters passed to generate_video
   501→
   502→        Returns:
   503→            Path to generated output file(s)
   504→
   505→        Raises:
   506→            RuntimeError: If no model is loaded
   507→            ValueError: If required parameters are missing for model type
   508→        """
   509→        if not self.current_model:

   511→

   525→        try:

   536→                except Exception as _e:

   538→
   539→            # end_image → image_end (PIL)
   540→            _ei = kwargs.pop("end_image", None)

   542→                try:

   547→                except Exception as _e:
```

```
1→"""
     2→Model-switching and task-conversion logic extracted from HeadlessTaskQueue.
     3→
     4→Every public function in this module takes the ``HeadlessTaskQueue`` instance
     5→(aliased *queue*) as its first argument so that it can access ``queue.logger``,
     6→``queue.orchestrator``, ``queue.current_model``, etc.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import os
    12→import time
    13→from typing import Any, Dict, TYPE_CHECKING
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from headless_model_management import HeadlessTaskQueue, GenerationTask
    17→
    18→from source.core.log import is_debug_enabled
    19→
    20→
    21→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→# Model switching
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→
    25→def switch_model_impl(queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue", model_key: str, worker_name: str) -> bool:
    26→    """
    27→    Ensure the correct model is loaded using wgp.py's model management.
    28→
    29→    This leverages the orchestrator's model loading while tracking
    30→    the change in our queue system. The orchestrator checks WGP's ground truth
    31→    (wgp.transformer_type) to determine if a switch is actually needed.
    32→
    33→    Returns:
    34→        bool: True if a model switch actually occurred, False if already loaded
    35→    """
    36→    # Ensure orchestrator is initialized before switching models
    37→    queue._ensure_orchestrator()
    38→
    39→    queue.logger.debug(f"{worker_name} ensuring model {model_key} is loaded (current: {queue.current_model})")
    40→    switch_start = time.time()
    41→
    42→    try:
    43→        # Use orchestrator's model loading - it checks WGP's ground truth
    44→        # and returns whether a switch actually occurred
    45→        switched = queue.orchestrator.load_model(model_key)
    46→
    47→        if switched:
    48→            # Only do switch-specific actions if a switch actually occurred
    49→            queue.logger.info(f"{worker_name} switched model: {queue.current_model} → {model_key}")
    50→
    51→            queue.stats["model_switches"] += 1
    52→            switch_time = time.time() - switch_start
    53→            queue.logger.info(f"Model switch completed in {switch_time:.1f}s")
    54→
    55→        # Always sync our tracking with orchestrator's state
    56→        queue.current_model = model_key
    57→        return switched
    58→
    59→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
    60→        queue.logger.error(f"Model switch failed: {e}")
    61→        raise
    62→
    63→
    64→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→# Task conversion
    66→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67→
    68→def convert_to_wgp_task_impl(queue: "HeadlessTaskQueue", task: "GenerationTask") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    69→    """
    70→    Convert task to WGP parameters using typed TaskConfig.
    71→
    72→    This:
    73→    1. Parses all params into TaskConfig at the boundary
    74→    2. Handles LoRA downloads via LoRAConfig
    75→    3. Converts to WGP format only at the end
    76→    """
    77→    from source.core.params import TaskConfig
    78→
    79→    # Parse into typed config
    80→    config = TaskConfig.from_db_task(
    81→        task.parameters,
    82→        task_id=task.id,
    83→        task_type=task.parameters.get('_source_task_type', ''),
    84→        model=task.model,
    85→        debug_mode=is_debug_enabled()
    86→    )
    87→
    88→    # Add prompt and model
    89→    config.generation.prompt = task.prompt
    90→    config.model = task.model
    91→
    92→    # Log the parsed config
    93→    if is_debug_enabled():
    94→        config.log_summary(queue.logger.info)
    95→
    96→    # Handle LoRA downloads if any are pending
    97→    if config.lora.has_pending_downloads():
    98→        queue.logger.info(f"[LORA_PROCESS] Task {task.id}: {len(config.lora.get_pending_downloads())} LoRAs need downloading")
    99→
   100→        # Ensure we're in Wan2GP directory for LoRA operations
```
